/*
Tools:
0 - layout
1 - boundary
2 - floor
3 - ceiling
4 - lighting
5 - dynamnic lighting
6 - doors
7 - triggers
8 - add enemies
9 - add pickups
10 - load zone
11 - overlap
*/

/*
Shapes:
0-Full
1-Filled Top Left Corner
2-Filled Top Right Corner
3-Filled Bottom Right Corner
4-Filled Bottom Left Corner
5-Rounded Top Left Corner
6-Rounded Top Right Corner
7-Rounded Bottom Right Corner
8-Rounded Bottom Left Corner
9-Full Circle
10-Vertical Wall
11-Horizontal Wall
12-Top Left Corner Thin Wall
13-Top Right Corner Thin Wall
14-Bottom Right Corner Thin Wall
15-Bottom Left Corner Thin Wall
16-Right Wall
17-Bottom Wall
18-Left Wall
19-Top Wall
20-Right Half Wall
21-Bottom Half Wall
22-Left Half Wall
23-Top Half Wall
24-Top Left Corner Half Wall
25-Top Right Corner Half Wall
26-Bottom Right Corner Half Wall
27-Bottom Left Corner Half Wall
*/
   
  
frameRate(60);

/** Create Functions **/

//Functions for loading and unloading maps

var uncompressMap = function(map){
    
    var uncompressGrid = function(grid){
        
        //[[[first number,how many times it repeats],[second number, how many times it repeats]...],Number of line copies this is],...]
        
        var returnGrid = [];
        
        for(var a = 0; a <= grid.length-1; a++){
            
            var uncompressedLine = [];
            
            for(var b = 0; b <= grid[a].length-2; b++){
                
                for(var num = 1; num<=grid[a][b][1]; num++){
                    
                    uncompressedLine.push(grid[a][b][0]);
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            for(var line = 1; line <= grid[a][grid[a].length-1]; line++){
                
                returnGrid.push(uncompressedLine.slice());
                
            }
            
        }
        
        return returnGrid;
        
    };
    
    var doorDefs = [{}];
    
    for(var i = 1; i <= map[6].length-1; i++){
        
        var door = {
            
            type:map[6][i][0],
            
            lor:map[6][i][1],
            
            player:map[6][i][2],
            
            max:map[6][i][3],
            
            min:map[6][i][4],
            
            speed:map[6][i][5],
            
            direction:map[6][i][6],
            
            trigger:map[6][i][7],
            
        };
        
        doorDefs.push(door);
        
    }
    
    var teleporters = [];
    
    for(var i = 0; i <= map[7].length-1; i++){
        
        var tele = {
            
            x1:map[7][i][0],
            y1:map[7][i][1],
            w1:map[7][i][2],
            h1:map[7][i][3],
            
            x2:map[7][i][4],
            y2:map[7][i][5],
            w2:map[7][i][6],
            h2:map[7][i][7],
            
        };
        
        teleporters.push(tele);
        
    }
    
    var levelExits=[];
    
    for(var i = 0; i <= map[8].length-1; i++){
        
        var exit = {
            
            x:map[8][i][0],
            y:map[8][i][1],
            w:map[8][i][2],
            h:map[8][i][3],
            
            level:map[8][i][4],
            
            sx:map[8][i][5],
            sy:map[8][i][6],
            
        };
        
        levelExits.push(exit);
        
    }
    
    //Var we return
    var returnMap = {
        
        name:map[0],
        
        player:map[1],
        
        direction:map[2],
        
        enemies:{
            x:map[3][0],
            y:map[3][1],
            z:map[3][2],
            angle:map[3][3],
            start:map[3][4],
            spawnTrigger:map[3][5],
            condition:map[3][6],
            type:map[3][7],
            state:map[3][8],
            },
        
        pickups:{
            x:map[4][0],
            y:map[4][1],
            start:map[4][2],
            spawnTrigger:map[4][3],
            condition:map[4][4],
            type:map[4][5],
        },
        
        decor:{
            x:map[5][0],
            y:map[5][1],
            z:map[5][2],
            object:map[5][3],
        },
        
        doorDefs:doorDefs,
        
        teleporters:teleporters,
        
        levelExits:levelExits,
        
        triggerTypes:map[9],
        triggerCondition:map[10],
        
        layout:uncompressGrid(map[11]),
        
        boundaries:uncompressGrid(map[12]),
        
        lighting:uncompressGrid(map[13]),
        defaultLighting:uncompressGrid(map[14]),
        dynamicLighting:uncompressGrid(map[15]),
        
        floorLayout:uncompressGrid(map[16]),
        ceilingLayout:uncompressGrid(map[17]),
        
        doors:uncompressGrid(map[18]),
        doorOpen:uncompressGrid(map[19]),
        defaultDoorOpen:uncompressGrid(map[20]),
        triggers:uncompressGrid(map[21]),
        
        loadZone:uncompressGrid(map[22]),
        loadOverlap:uncompressGrid(map[23]),
        
        shape:uncompressGrid(map[24]),
        
        floorHeight:uncompressGrid(map[25]),
        ceilingHeight:uncompressGrid(map[26]),
        
        floorWallLayout:uncompressGrid(map[27]),
        ceilingWallLayout:uncompressGrid(map[28]),
        
        floorSecondaryHeight:uncompressGrid(map[29]),
        ceilingSecondaryHeight:uncompressGrid(map[30]),
        
        floorShape:uncompressGrid(map[31]),
        ceilingShape:uncompressGrid(map[32]),
        
        skyBox:map[33],
        floorBox:map[34],
        customAssets:map[35],
        assets:customAssets[map[35][1]],
    };
    
    return returnMap;
    
};

//Key detection
var keys={};

var keyPressed=function(){

	keys[keyCode]=true;

};

	delete keys[keyCode];

};

//Mouse Lock

var mouseLocked = false;
var mouseMoving = false;

function mouseMoved (e) {
	if (game.cam === 2) {
		if (mouseLocked) {
		//-e.movementX*player.mouseSensitivity;
		//(e.movementY)*Mouse_Sensitivity;
		
		player.angle+=e.movementX*player.mouseSensitivity;
		
        //both camera direction and camera plane must be rotated
        var oldDirX = player.dirX;
        player.dirX = player.dirX * cos(e.movementX*player.mouseSensitivity) - player.dirY * sin(e.movementX*player.mouseSensitivity);
        player.dirY = oldDirX * sin(e.movementX*player.mouseSensitivity) + player.dirY * cos(e.movementX*player.mouseSensitivity);
        
        var oldPlaneX = player.planeX;
        player.planeX = player.planeX * cos(e.movementX*player.mouseSensitivity) - player.planeY * sin(e.movementX*player.mouseSensitivity);
        player.planeY = oldPlaneX * sin(e.movementX*player.mouseSensitivity) + player.planeY * cos(e.movementX*player.mouseSensitivity);
		
		if(e.movementX>0){
            
            //Sway to right
            if(weaponSway.turnX<weaponSway.maxTurn){
                
                weaponSway.turnX+=e.movementX*player.mouseSensitivity;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Reset Sway
        else if(weaponSway.turnX>0){
            
            weaponSway.turnX-=player.turnSpeed;
            
        }
        
        if(e.movementX<0){
            
            //Sway to left
            if(weaponSway.turnX>-weaponSway.maxTurn){
                
                weaponSway.turnX+=e.movementX*player.mouseSensitivity;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Reset Sway
        else if(weaponSway.turnX<0){
            
            weaponSway.turnX+=player.turnSpeed;
            
        }
        
		
		if(e.movementY < 0 && player.vAngle < game.camera.maxAngle){
            
            //game.camera.horizon -= (e.movementY*player.mouseSensitivity*5)/(game.height/game.realHeight);
            player.vAngle -= (e.movementY*player.mouseSensitivity*2);
            
		}
		
		if(e.movementY > 0 && player.vAngle > -game.camera.maxAngle){
            
            //game.camera.horizon -= (e.movementY*player.mouseSensitivity*5)/(game.height/game.realHeight);
            
            player.vAngle -= (e.movementY*player.mouseSensitivity*2);
            
		}
		
		}
		
		
        /*
		else {
			player.yaw += mouseX-pmouseX;
		player.pitch -= mouseY-pmouseY;
		}
		*/
		mouseMoving = true;
	}
}

var doc = (function(){return this[["document"]];})();
var canvas = doc.getElementById("output-canvas");

function mouseClicked () {
    
    if(game.cam === 2){
        
        if(doc.pointerLockElement!==canvas){
            canvas.requestPointerLock();
            if(!mouseLocked){
            //doc.removeEventListener("mousemove", mouseMoved, false);
            //doc.addEventListener("mousemove", mouseMoved, false);
            doc.onmousemove = mouseMoved;
            }
            mouseLocked = true;
        }
        
    }
}

//Mouse Scroll

var mouseIsScrolled = false;

var realMouseScroll = 0;

mouseScrolled = function() {
    
    mouseIsScrolled = true;
    
    if(mouseScroll > 0){
        
        realMouseScroll = 1;
        
    }
    
    else{
        
        realMouseScroll = -1;
        
    }
    
};

//Varaibles that are only initialized once
var lastMapX = floor(player.x/20);
var lastMapY = floor(player.y/20);
var lastPS = true;
var ps =true;

//Antialiasing
smooth();

//Fills in light based on light sources 
var fillLight = function(step){
    
    var lowestValue = Infinity;
    
    var highestValue = -Infinity;
    
    for(var y = 0; y <= loadedMap.lighting.length-1; y++){
        
        for(var x = 0; x <= loadedMap.lighting[y].length-1; x++){
            
            lowestValue = min(lowestValue,loadedMap.lighting[y][x]);
            
            highestValue = max(highestValue,loadedMap.lighting[y][x]);
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //println('min: ' + lowestValue);
    //println('max: ' + highestValue);
    
    var targetValue = highestValue;
    
    var currentValue = highestValue-step;
    
    while(currentValue > lowestValue){
        
        //Go through all the map spaces looking for the current value
        for(var y = 0; y <= loadedMap.lighting.length-1; y++){
            
            for(var x = 0; x <= loadedMap.lighting[y].length-1; x++){
                
                //We've found our target value
                if(loadedMap.lighting[y][x] === targetValue){
                    
                    for(var i = constrain(y-1,0,loadedMap.lighting.length-1); i <= constrain(y+1,0,loadedMap.lighting.length-1); i++){
                        
                        for(var j = constrain(x-1,0,loadedMap.lighting[i].length-1); j <= constrain(x+1,0,loadedMap.lighting[i].length-1); j++){
                            
                            if(currentValue > loadedMap.lighting[i][j] && (loadedMap.layout[i][j] === 0 || loadedMap.shape[i][j] !== 0)){
                                
                                loadedMap.lighting[i][j] = currentValue;
                                loadedMap.defaultLighting[i][j] = currentValue;
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Move to next step
        targetValue = currentValue;
        
        currentValue = floor(currentValue*(1/step));
        
        currentValue--;
        
        currentValue /= (1/step);
        
    }
    
};

//Function to find if the point that is being checked is on a primary or secondary height
var primaryOrSecondary = function(x,y,type){
    
    //Tile with fraction at end
    var tileX = x/20;
    var tileY = y/20;
    
    //Map space
    var mapY = constrain(floor(tileY),0,loadedMap.floorShape.length-1);
    var mapX = constrain(floor(tileX),0,loadedMap.floorShape[mapY].length-1);
    
    var decX = tileX-mapX;
    var decY = tileY-mapY;
    
    //What shape
    var shape;
    
    //Set values based on floor and ceiling
    var primary;
    var secondary;
    
    if(type === 'floor'){
        
        shape = loadedMap.floorShape[mapY][mapX];
        
        primary = tempMap.floorHeight[mapY][mapX];
        secondary = tempMap.floorSecondaryHeight[mapY][mapX];
        
    }
    
    else{
        
        shape = loadedMap.ceilingShape[mapY][mapX];
        
        primary = tempMap.ceilingHeight[mapY][mapX];
        secondary = tempMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight[mapY][mapX];
        
    }
    
    //Figure out what values to return
    var returnValue = primary;
    
    switch(shape){
        
        //Square
        case 0:
            
            returnValue = primary;
            
            break;
        
        //Diagonal
        case 1:
            
            if(1-decX<decY){
                
                returnValue = secondary;
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                returnValue = primary;
                
            }
            
            break;
        
        //Diagonal
        case 2:
            
            if(decX<decY){
                
                returnValue = secondary;
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                returnValue = primary;
                
            }
            
            break;
            
        //Vertical Wall
        case 3:
            
            //In the secondary space
            if(decX>0.5){
                
                returnValue = secondary;
                
            }
            
            //In the primary space
            else{
                
                returnValue = primary;
                
            }
            
            break;
        
        //Horizontal Wall
        case 4:
            
            //In the secondary space
            if(decY>0.5){
                
                returnValue = secondary;
                
            }
            
            //In the primary space
            else{
                
                returnValue = primary;
                
            }
            
            break;
        
    }
    
    //A check for the player falling system
    ps = returnValue === primary;
    
    return returnValue;
    
};

//Play sounds
var playSoundEffect=function(sound){
    
    if(game.sound){
        
        switch(sound){
            
            //Crowbar swing
            case 0:
                
                //playSound(sounds.swing);
                
                var audio = getSound("rpg/battle-swing").audio.cloneNode(false);
                
                audio.volume = 0.7;
                
                audio.play();
                
                
                break;
               
            
            //Pistol Shot
            case 1:
                
                playSound(sounds.deepLaser);
                
                playSound(sounds.base);
                
                break;
                
            //Candy shot
            case 2:
                
                //playSound(sounds.mutedExplosion);
                
                playSound(sounds.base);
                
                playSound(sounds.thud);
                
                playSound(sounds.hit);
                
                break;
                
            //Shotgun Shot
            case 3:
                
                playSound(sounds.deepLaser);
                
                playSound(sounds.mutedExplosion);
                
                playSound(sounds.base);
                
                break;
                
                
            //Gattling Shot
            case 4:
                
                playSound(sounds.automaticLaser);
                
                playSound(sounds.shortSteam);
                
                playSound(sounds.base);
                
                break;
                
            //Launcher Shot
            case 5:
                
                playSound(sounds.mutedExplosion);
                
                playSound(sounds.magic);
                
                playSound(sounds.shortSteam);
                
                playSound(sounds.base);
                
                break;
            
            //Explosion
            case 10:
                
                /*
                playSound(sounds.rumble);
                
                playSound(sounds.base);
                
                playSound(sounds.warble);
                
                //playSound(sounds.mutedExplosion);
                
                playSound(sounds.longSteam);
                */
                
                
                playSound(sounds.rumble);
                
                playSound(sounds.base);
                
                playSound(sounds.loudExplosion);
                
                playSound(sounds.mutedExplosion);
                
                break;
            
            //Default damage sound
            case 17:
                
                playSound(sounds.splat);
                
                break;
            
            //Pickup sound
            case 18:
                
                playSound(sounds.fabric);
                
                playSound(sounds.splat);
                
                break;
            
            //Hitting wall with melee
            case 19:
                
                playSound(sounds.base);
                
                playSound(sounds.thud);
                
                var metalClank = getSound("rpg/metal-chime").audio.cloneNode(false);
                
                metalClank.volume = 0.3;
                
                metalClank.play();
                
                break;
            
            //Double Barrel Shot
            case 20:
                
                playSound(sounds.mutedExplosion);
                
                playSound(sounds.loudExplosion);
                
                playSound(sounds.hit);
                
                playSound(sounds.base);
                
                break;
            
            
        }
        
    }
    
};

//Create Particles
var createParticle=function(x,y,speedX,speedY,verticalPos,verticalSpeed,type){
    
    
    //Check all particles
    var dud=false;
    var dudNum=0;
    
    //Find duds
    {
        
        //Variables for search
        var i=0;
        var search=true;
        
        //Search
        while(search){
            
            if(particles.dud[i]){
                
                dudNum=i;
                
                dud=true;
                
                search=false;
                
            }
            
            i++;
            
            if(i>=particles.dud.length-1){
                
                search=false;
                
            }
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Create Projectile
    if(!dud){
        
        particles.x.push(x);
        particles.y.push(y);
        
        particles.speedX.push(speedX);
        particles.speedY.push(speedY);
        
        particles.life.push(particleConstants[type].lifetime);
        
        particles.transparency.push(particleConstants[type].transparency);
        
        particles.verticalPos.push(verticalPos);
        
        particles.verticalSpeed.push(verticalSpeed);
        
        particles.animation.push(0);
        
        particles.animationFrame.push(0);
        
        particles.type.push(type);
        
        particles.dud.push(false);
        
    }
    
    else{
        
        
        particles.x[dudNum]=x;
        particles.y[dudNum]=y;
        
        particles.speedX[dudNum]=speedX;
        particles.speedY[dudNum]=speedY;
        
        particles.life[dudNum]=particleConstants[type].lifetime;
        
        particles.transparency[dudNum]=particleConstants[type].transparency;
        
        particles.verticalPos[dudNum]=verticalPos;
        
        particles.verticalSpeed[dudNum]=verticalSpeed;
        
        particles.animation[dudNum]=0;
        
        particles.animationFrame[dudNum]=0;
        
        particles.type[dudNum]=type;
        
        particles.dud[dudNum]=false;
        
    }
    
};

//Hurt entities
var hurt=function(type,num,damage,knockback,angle,vAngle){
    
    switch(type){
        
        //Player
        case 0:
            
            switch(num){
                
                default:
                
                playSoundEffect(17);
                
                
            }
            
            if(player.suit>0){
                
                player.health-=damage/5;
                
                player.suit-=(damage-damage/5)/2;
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                player.health-=damage;
                
            }
            var oldSpeed = dist(0,0,player.speedX,player.speedY);
            
            player.speedX+=knockback*cos(angle);
            player.speedY+=knockback*sin(angle);
            player.vertSpeed += knockback * sin(vAngle)*20;
            
            //Make a player able to be launched faster than walk speed
            if(dist(0,0,player.speedX,player.speedY) >= player.maxSpeed * 1.1 && oldSpeed < player.maxSpeed * 1.1){
                
                player.overSpeed = true;
                
            }
            
            break;
        
        //Enemy
        case 1:
            
            enemies.health[num]-=damage;
            
            switch(enemies.type[num]){
                
                default:
                
                    playSoundEffect(17);
                
            }
            
            enemies.speedX[num]+=(knockback*cos(angle))/enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].weight;
            enemies.speedY[num]+=(knockback*sin(angle))/enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].weight;
            enemies.vertSpeed[num]+=(knockback*sin(vAngle)*10)/enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].weight;
            
            enemies.state[num] = 'attacking';
            
            var painR = random(0,1);
            
            if((painR<=enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].painChance || dist(0,0,enemies.speedX[num],enemies.speedY[num]) >= enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].speed * 1.1) && enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].moveType === 2){
                
                enemies.state[num] = 'pain';
                
                enemies.animation[num] = 3;
                
                enemies.animationFrame[num] = 0;
                
                enemies.animationTime[num] = 0;
                
                enemies.moveTime[num] = enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].painTime;
                
            } 
            
            //Special case 
            switch(enemies.type[num]){
                
                
                //Skeleton King
                case 9:
                    
                    enemies.animation[num] = 2;
                    
                    enemies.animationFrame[num] = 0;
                    
                    enemies.animationTime[num] = 20;
                    
                    break;
                
            }
            
            //Kill enemy
            if(enemies.health[num]<=0 && !enemies.dud[num]){
                
                switch(enemies.type[num]){
                    
                    default:
                        
                        //Make a drop
                        if(enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].drop){
                            
                            var r = random(0,1);
                            
                            if(r < enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].dropRate){
                                
                                //Add to current variable
                                pickups.x.push(enemies.x[num]);
                                pickups.y.push(enemies.y[num]);
                                pickups.type.push(enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].dropType);
                                pickups.trigger.push(0);
                                pickups.dud.push(false);
                                
                                game.pickupsSpawned.push(true);
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                        //Make the dead enemy (Non Gibbed)
                        if(enemies.health[num]>(-enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].health)/2 || !enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].gib){
                            
                            createParticle(enemies.x[num],enemies.y[num],enemies.speedX[num],enemies.speedY[num],enemies.verticalPos[num]+enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].height/2-300,enemies.vertSpeed[num],enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].nonGibType);
                            
                        }
                        
                        //Make the dead enemy (Gibbed)
                        else if(enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].gib){
                            
                            var velocityNum = constrain(-(enemies.health[num]+(enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].health/2))/3,1,10);
                            
                            for(var i = 0; i <= random(2,5); i++){
                                
                                createParticle(enemies.x[num],enemies.y[num],enemies.speedX[num]+random(-3 * velocityNum,3 * velocityNum),enemies.speedY[num]+random(-3 * velocityNum,3 * velocityNum),enemies.verticalPos[num],enemies.vertSpeed[num]+random(-10,20),enemyConstants[enemies.type[num]].gibType);
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                         
                        //Kill Enemy
                        enemies.dud[num]=true;
                        
                
                }
                
            }
            
    }
    
};

//Give things to the player and enemies
var give=function(targetType,targetNum,giveType,giveNum,giveAmount){
    
    //Enemy or player or...?
    switch(targetType){
        
        //Player
        case 'player':
            
            //Health, suit, ammo, weapon, key?
            switch(giveType){
                
                //Add health
                case 'health':
                    
                    var totalHealth=player.health+giveAmount;
                    
                    //Less than full
                    if(totalHealth<100){
                        
                        player.health=totalHealth;
                        
                    }
                    
                    //Over healed
                    else{
                        
                        player.health=100;
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                    
                //Add suit
                case 'suit':
                    
                    if(!player.hev){
                        
                        player.hev=true;
                        
                    }
                    
                    var totalSuit=player.suit+giveAmount;
                    
                    //Less than full
                    if(totalSuit<100){
                        
                        player.suit=totalSuit;
                        
                    }
                    
                    //Over healed
                    else{
                        
                        player.suit=100;
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                
                //Add ammo
                case 'ammo':
                    
                    var totalAmmo=player.ammo[giveNum]+giveAmount;
                    
                    //Less than full
                    if(totalAmmo<player.maxAmmo[giveNum]){
                        
                        player.ammo[giveNum]=totalAmmo;
                        
                    }
                    
                    //Over Filled
                    else{
                        
                        player.ammo[giveNum]=player.maxAmmo[giveNum];
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                    
                case 'weapon':
                    
                    player.weapons[giveNum]=true;
                    
                    player.armed=true;
                    
                    player.projectile=giveNum;
                    
                    player.reloading=false;
                    
                    player.reloadTime=0;
                    
                    drawnWeapon=giveNum;
                    
                    //remove the earlier weapons
                    switch(giveNum){
                        
                        case 5:
                            
                            player.weapons[1] = false;
                            
                            break;
                        
                        case 6:
                            
                            player.weapons[2] = false;
                            
                            break;
                        
                        case 7:
                            
                            player.weapons[3] = false;
                            
                            break;
                        
                        case 8:
                            
                            player.weapons[4] = false;
                            
                            break;
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                
                case 'key':
                    
                    for(var i = 0; i <= loadedMap.triggerTypes[giveNum].length-1; i++){
                        
                        if(loadedMap.triggerTypes[giveNum][i] === 1){
                            
                            game.triggers[giveNum][i] = true;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                
            }
            
            break;
        
        //Enemy
        case 'enemy':
            
            //Health, suit, ammo, weapon?
            switch(giveType){
                
                //Add health
                case 'health':
                    
                    var totalHealth=enemies.health[targetNum]+giveAmount;
                    
                    //Less than full
                    if(totalHealth<enemyConstants[enemies.type[targetNum]].health){
                        
                        enemies.health[targetNum]=totalHealth;
                        
                    }
                    
                    //Over healed
                    else{
                        
                        enemies.health[targetNum]=enemyConstants[enemies.type[targetNum]].health;
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                    
            }
            
            break;
        
    }
    
};

//Load Map and Reset Stuff
var loadMap = function(sx,sy){
    
    //Tell the player you are loading
    fill(207, 207, 207);
    textAlign(CENTER,CENTER);
    text('Loading...',width/2,height/2);
    
    player.x=0;
    player.y=0;
    player.height = 0;
    
    game.triggers=[];
    
    //Uncompress the map saved in the maps array
    loadedMap = uncompressMap(maps[game.map]);
    
    //Check if there are custom assets
    if(loadedMap.customAssets[0]){
        
        //Wall Constants
        {
            
            wallConstants=loadedMap.assets.walls;
            
        }
        
        //Floor Constants
        {
            
            floorConstants=loadedMap.assets.floors;
            
        }
        
        //Ceiling Constants
        {
            
            ceilingConstants=loadedMap.assets.ceilings;
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Set Player Positions
    /*
    if(sx && sy){
        
        player.x = sx;
        player.y = sy;
        
    }
    
    else{
        
        player.x=loadedMap.player[0];
        player.y=loadedMap.player[1];
        
    }
    */
    
    player.x=loadedMap.player[0];
    player.y=loadedMap.player[1];
    
    //Player Health Reset
    player.health=100;
    
    //Reset Speed 
    player.speedX=0;
    player.speedY=0;
    
    //Set Direction
    switch(loadedMap.direction){
        
        case 0:
            
            //Player Angle
            player.angle=0;
            
            //Direction Vector
            player.dirX=0;
            player.dirY=-0.8;
            
            //Plane Vector
            player.planeX=0.66;
            player.planeY=0;
            
            break;
            
        case 1:
            
            //Player Angle
            player.angle=90;
            
            //Direction Vector
            player.dirX=0.8;
            player.dirY=0;
            
            //Plane Vector
            player.planeX=0;
            player.planeY=0.66;
            
            break;
            
        case 2:
            
            //Player Angle
            player.angle=180;
            
            //Direction Vector
            player.dirX=0;
            player.dirY=0.8;
            
            //Plane Vector
            player.planeX=-0.66;
            player.planeY=0;
            
            break;
            
        case 3:
            
            //Player Angle
            player.angle=270;
            
            //Direction Vector
            player.dirX=-0.8;
            player.dirY=0;
            
            //Plane Vector
            player.planeX=0;
            player.planeY=-0.66;
            
            break;
        
    }
    
    //Reset Lighting
    loadedMap.lighting=[];
    tempMap.lighting=[];
    
    for(var y = 0; y <= loadedMap.defaultLighting.length-1; y++){
        
        loadedMap.lighting.push([]);
        
        tempMap.lighting.push([]);
        
        for(var x = 0; x <= loadedMap.defaultLighting[y].length-1; x++){
        
            loadedMap.lighting[y].push(loadedMap.defaultLighting[y][x]);
            
            tempMap.lighting[y].push(loadedMap.defaultLighting[y][x]);
        
        }
        
    }
    
    //Reset Doors
    loadedMap.doorOpen=[];
    
    tempMap.doorOpen=[];
    
    tempMap.doorActivated=[];
    
    tempMap.doorTimeOpen=[];
    
    for(var y = 0; y <= loadedMap.defaultDoorOpen.length-1; y++){
        
        loadedMap.doorOpen.push([]);
        
        tempMap.doorOpen.push([]);
        
        tempMap.doorActivated.push([]);
        
        tempMap.doorTimeOpen.push([]);
        
        for(var x = 0; x <= loadedMap.defaultDoorOpen[y].length-1; x++){
            
            loadedMap.doorOpen[y].push(loadedMap.defaultDoorOpen[y][x]);
            
            tempMap.doorOpen[y].push(loadedMap.defaultDoorOpen[y][x]);
            
            tempMap.doorActivated[y].push(false);
            
            tempMap.doorTimeOpen[y].push(0);
            
        }
        
    }
    
    tempMap.floorHeight = [];
    
    tempMap.ceilingHeight = [];
    
    tempMap.floorSecondaryHeight = [];
    
    tempMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight = [];
    
    //Set the floor and ceiling heights in the temp variable
    for(var y = 0; y <= loadedMap.floorHeight.length-1; y++){
        
        tempMap.floorHeight.push(loadedMap.floorHeight[y].slice());
        
        tempMap.ceilingHeight.push(loadedMap.ceilingHeight[y].slice());
        
        tempMap.floorSecondaryHeight.push(loadedMap.floorSecondaryHeight[y].slice());
        
        tempMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight.push(loadedMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight[y].slice());
        
    }
    
    //Load Enemies
    {
        
        enemies.x=[];
        enemies.y=[];
        enemies.speedX=[];
        enemies.speedY=[];
        enemies.vertSpeed=[];
        enemies.moving=[];
        enemies.faceAngle=[];
        enemies.angle=[];
        enemies.state=[];
        enemies.moveTime=[];
        enemies.cooldown=[];
        enemies.health=[];
        enemies.verticalPos=[];
        enemies.animation=[];
        enemies.animationFrame=[];
        enemies.animationTime=[];
        enemies.type=[];
        enemies.trigger=[];
        enemies.dud=[];
        enemies.levelID=[];
        game.enemiesSpawned=[];
        
        enemies.lastSeen=[];
        enemies.target=[];
        enemies.path=[];
        enemies.next=[];
        
        var states = ['alert','ambush','idle','hunting','attacking'];
        
        for(var i = 0; i <= loadedMap.enemies.x.length-1; i++){
            
            game.enemiesSpawned.push(loadedMap.enemies.start[i]);
            
            if(loadedMap.enemies.start[i]){
                
                enemies.x.push(loadedMap.enemies.x[i]);
                enemies.y.push(loadedMap.enemies.y[i]);
                enemies.speedX.push(0);
                enemies.speedY.push(0);
                enemies.vertSpeed.push(0);
                enemies.moving.push(false);
                enemies.faceAngle.push(0);
                enemies.angle.push(loadedMap.enemies.angle[i]);
                enemies.state.push(states[loadedMap.enemies.state[i]]);
                enemies.moveTime.push(0);
                enemies.cooldown.push(0);
                enemies.health.push(enemyConstants[loadedMap.enemies.type[i]].health);
                //enemies.verticalPos.push(objectConstants[enemyConstants[loadedMap.enemies.type[i]].object].verticalPos + primaryOrSecondary(loadedMap.enemies.x[i],loadedMap.enemies.y[i],'floor'));
                enemies.verticalPos.push(loadedMap.enemies.z[i]);
                enemies.animation.push(0);
                enemies.animationFrame.push(0);
                enemies.animationTime.push(0);
                enemies.type.push(loadedMap.enemies.type[i]);
                enemies.trigger.push(loadedMap.enemies.condition[i]);
                enemies.dud.push(false);
                enemies.levelID.push(i);
                
                enemies.lastSeen.push([]);
                enemies.target.push([]);
                enemies.path.push([]);
                enemies.next.push([]);
                
            }
            
            
        }
        
    }
    //Load Pickups
    {
        
        pickups.x=[];
        pickups.y=[];
        pickups.type=[];
        pickups.dud=[];
        game.pickupsSpawned=[];
        pickups.trigger=[];
        pickups.levelID=[];
        
        for(var i = 0; i <= loadedMap.pickups.x.length-1; i++){
            
            game.pickupsSpawned.push(loadedMap.pickups.start[i]);
            
            if(loadedMap.pickups.start[i]){
                
                pickups.x.push(loadedMap.pickups.x[i]);
                pickups.y.push(loadedMap.pickups.y[i]);
                pickups.type.push(loadedMap.pickups.type[i]);
                pickups.trigger.push(loadedMap.pickups.condition[i]);
                pickups.dud.push(false);
                pickups.levelID.push(i);
                
            }
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Reset particles
    {
        
        particles.x=[];
        particles.y=[];
        particles.type=[];
        particles.dud=[];
        particles.speedX=[];
        particles.speedY=[];
        particles.life=[];
        particles.transparency=[];
        particles.verticalPos=[];
        particles.verticalSpeed=[];
        particles.animation=[];
        particles.animationFrame=[];
        
    }
    
    //Reset projectiles
    {
        
        projectiles.x=[];
        projectiles.y=[];
        projectiles.speedX=[];
        projectiles.speedY=[];
        projectiles.life=[];
        projectiles.transparency=[];
        projectiles.verticalPos=[];
        projectiles.verticalSpeed=[];
        projectiles.animation=[];
        projectiles.animationFrame=[];
        projectiles.type=[];
        projectiles.dud=[];
        
    }
    
    //Set triggers
    game.triggers=[];
    
    for(var i = 0; i <= loadedMap.triggerTypes.length-1; i++){
        
        game.triggers.push([]);
        
        for(var s = 0; s <= loadedMap.triggerTypes[i].length-1; s++){
            
            game.triggers[i].push(false);
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Set player height
    player.height=-300-player.floorHeight+primaryOrSecondary(player.x,player.y,'floor');
    
    game.camera.x=player.x;
    game.camera.y=player.y;
    
};

//Set variables each frame
var updateVars=function(){
    
    //Set deltaF
    var deltaT = millis() - game.lastFTime;
    
    game.lastFTime = millis();
    
    game.deltaF = constrain(game.baseF * (deltaT/1000),0,game.baseF); 
    
    //println(game.deltaF);
    
    //println(game.totalF/game.numF);
    
    //Show hud in hev suit
    if(player.hev){
        
        game.hud=true;
        
    }
    
    //Kill player
    if(player.health<=0){
        
        loadMap(false,false);
        
    }
    
    //Reset Suit
    if(player.suit<0){
        
        player.suit=0;
        
    }
    
    //Reset cooldown
    if(player.cooldown<0){
        
        player.cooldown=0;
        
    }
    
    //Reload
    if(player.reloading){
        
        if(player.reloadTime>=projectileConstants[player.projectile].reloadTime){
            
            //Full reload
            if(projectileConstants[player.projectile].reloadType==="full"){
                
                if(projectileConstants[player.projectile].clip-player.clip[player.projectile]<player.ammo[projectileConstants[player.projectile].ammo]){
                    
                    player.ammo[projectileConstants[player.projectile].ammo]-=projectileConstants[player.projectile].clip-player.clip[player.projectile];
                    
                    player.clip[player.projectile]=projectileConstants[player.projectile].clip;
                    
                    player.reloading=false;
                    
                }
                
                else{
                    
                    player.clip[player.projectile]+=player.ammo[projectileConstants[player.projectile].ammo];
                    
                    player.ammo[projectileConstants[player.projectile].ammo]=0;
                    
                    player.reloading=false;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            //Individual/Charging Reload
            if(projectileConstants[player.projectile].reloadType==="individual"||projectileConstants[player.projectile].reloadType==="charging"){
                
                player.ammo[projectileConstants[player.projectile].ammo]--;
                
                player.clip[player.projectile]++;
                
                if(player.ammo[projectileConstants[player.projectile].ammo]<=0 || player.clip[player.projectile]>=projectileConstants[player.projectile].clip){
                    
                    player.reloading=false;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            player.reloadTime=0;
            
            //Sound Effects
            switch(player.projectile){
                
                case 1:
                    
                    playSoundEffect(11);
                    
                    break;
                
                case 2:
                    
                    playSoundEffect(11);
                    
                    break;
                    
                case 3:
                    
                    playSoundEffect(12);
                    
                    break;
                
                case 4:
                    
                    playSoundEffect(13);
                    
                    break;
                    
                case 5:
                    
                    playSoundEffect(14);
                    
                    break;
                
                case 7:
                    
                    playSoundEffect(15);
                    
                    break;
                
                case 8:
                    
                    playSoundEffect(16);
                
            }
            
        }
        
        else{
            
            player.reloadTime+=game.deltaF;
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Make nonreloadable weapons
    if(!projectileConstants[player.projectile].reloadable){
        
        player.clip[player.projectile]=player.ammo[projectileConstants[player.projectile].ammo];
        
    }
    
    //Move Transitioning Weapon
    if(weaponSway.switchY>0){
        
        weaponSway.switchY+=weaponSway.switchSpeed*weaponSway.switchFactor*game.deltaF;
        
    }
    
    if(weaponSway.switchY>weaponSway.maxSwitch){
        
        weaponSway.switchFactor=-1;
        
    }
    
    if(weaponSway.reloadY>0){
        
        weaponSway.reloadY+=weaponSway.reloadSpeed*weaponSway.reloadFactor*game.deltaF;
        
    }
    
    if(weaponSway.reloadY>weaponSway.maxReload){
        
        weaponSway.reloadFactor=0;
        
    }
    
    if(!player.reloading){
        
        weaponSway.reloadFactor=-1;
        
    }
    
    //Recoil
    if(weaponSway.recoilY<0){
        
        weaponSway.recoilY+=weaponSway.recoilSpeed*weaponSway.recoilFactor*game.deltaF;
        
    }
    
    if(weaponSway.recoilY<-weaponSway.maxRecoil){
        
        weaponSway.recoilFactor=1;
        
    }
    
    //Update Projectiles
    for(var i = 0; i <= projectiles.x.length-1; i++){
        
        if(!game.semiPause){
            
            switch(projectiles.type[i]){
                
                
                
            }
            
        }
        
    }
    //Update Particles
    for(var i = 0; i <= particles.x.length-1; i++){
        
        if(!game.semiPause){
            
            switch(particles.type[i]){
                
                case 12:
                case 13:
                    
                    var speed = sqrt(sq(particles.speedX[i]) + sq(particles.speedY[i]));
                    
                    if(speed>0){
                        
                        particles.animationFrame[i] += floor(random((speed*game.deltaF)/3,2));
                        
                        if(particles.animationFrame[i] >= 2){
                            
                            particles.animationFrame[i] = 0;
                            
                            //println(speed);
                            
                            //println(speed % 3);
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                
                case 15:
                case 32:
                case 34:
                case 38:
                    
                    var speed = sqrt(sq(particles.speedX[i]) + sq(particles.speedY[i]));
                    
                    if(speed>0){
                        
                        particles.animationFrame[i] += floor(random((speed*game.deltaF)/5,2));
                        
                        if(particles.animationFrame[i] >= objectConstants[particleConstants[particles.type[i]].object].texture[0].length){
                            
                            particles.animationFrame[i] = 0;
                            
                            //println(speed);
                            
                            //println(speed % 3);
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                    
                
            }
            
        }
        
    }
    //Calulate Distances and angles (Enemies)
    for(var i = 0; i <= enemies.x.length-1; i++){
        
        enemies.distance[i]=dist(player.x,player.y,enemies.x[i],enemies.y[i]);
        
        //Calculate angle between object and player.
        var objAngle = atan2(game.camera.x-enemies.x[i],game.camera.y-enemies.y[i]);
        
        //Shift perspective of player.angle.
        var playerAngle = abs(player.angle-360);
        
        //Convert object angle to 360°.
        if(objAngle<0){
            
            objAngle+=360;
            
        }
        
        var angle;
        
        //Calculate relative angle
        angle = objAngle - playerAngle;
        //Make sure the relative angle <= 180.
        if(angle > 180){
            
            angle -= 360;
            
        }
        
        if(angle < -180){
            
            angle += 360;
            
        }
        //Check if player is facing object.
        if(abs(angle)>60){
            
            enemies.inView[i]=false;
            
        }
        
        else{
            
            enemies.inView[i]=true;
            
        }
        
        //Animate enemy
        switch(enemies.type[i]){
            
            default:
                
                //enemies.animation[i]=0;
                
                //enemies.animationFrame[i]=0;
                
                //enemies.animationTime[i]=0;
            
        }
        
    }
    //globalTimer
    game.globalTimer+=game.deltaF;
    
    if(game.globalTimer>1000){
        
        game.globalTimer=0;
        
    }
    
    //Reset the button toggel
    if(!mouseIsPressed){
        
        controls.toggles.button = false;
        
    }
    
    //Set the player's zone
    var y = floor(player.y/20);
    var x = floor(player.x/20);
    
    player.zones[0]=loadedMap.loadZone[y][x];
    player.zones[1]=loadedMap.loadOverlap[y][x];
    
    //Set Camera
    
    if(game.cam !== 0){
        
        game.camera.x=player.x;
        game.camera.y=player.y;
        
    }
    
    game.camera.dirX=player.dirX;
    game.camera.dirY=player.dirY;
    game.camera.planeX=player.planeX;
    game.camera.planeY=player.planeY;
    game.camera.height = player.height*(game.height/game.realHeight);
    
    game.camera.normX = player.dirX/dist(0,0,player.dirX,player.dirY);
    game.camera.normY = player.dirY/dist(0,0,player.dirX,player.dirY);
    
    if(player.vAngle>game.camera.maxAngle){
        
        player.vAngle=game.camera.maxAngle;
        
    }
    
    if(player.vAngle<-game.camera.maxAngle){
        
        player.vAngle=-game.camera.maxAngle;
        
    }
    
    game.camera.horizon = (game.realHeight/2)*tan(player.vAngle);
    
    //println(game.camera.horizon);
    

	if(game.camera.horizon > game.camera.maxHor){
        
        //game.camera.horizon = game.camera.maxHor-1;
        
	}
	
	if(game.camera.horizon < game.camera.minHor){
        
        //game.camera.horizon = game.camera.minHor+1;
        
	}
	
	//Correct for yshear
	//game.camera.shearCorrection=cos(player.vAngle);
    
    //enemies.x[0]=player.x;
    //enemies.y[0]=player.y;
    
};

//Update map variables
var updateMap=function(){
    
    //Refresh temp lighting
    tempMap.lighting=[];
    
    for(var y = 0; y <= loadedMap.lighting.length-1; y++){
        
        tempMap.lighting.push([]);
        
        for(var x = 0; x <= loadedMap.lighting[y].length-1; x++){
            
            tempMap.lighting[y].push(loadedMap.lighting[y][x]);
            
        }
        
    }
    
    
    /*
    //Dynamic Lighting
    for(var y = 0; y <= loadedMap.dynamicLighting.length-1; y++){
        
        for(var x = 0; x <= loadedMap.dynamicLighting[y].length-1; x++){
            
            switch(loadedMap.dynamicLighting[y][x]){
                
                case 1:
                    
                    if((game.globalTimer>0 && game.globalTimer<60) || (game.globalTimer>240 && game.globalTimer<300) || (game.globalTimer>560 && game.globalTimer<680) || (game.globalTimer>810 && game.globalTimer<840) || (game.globalTimer>860 && game.globalTimer<880)){
                        
                        tempMap.lighting[y][x]=loadedMap.defaultLighting[y][x];
                        
                    }
                    
                    else{
                        
                        tempMap.lighting[y][x]=loadedMap.defaultLighting[y][x]/2;
                        
                    }
                
                break;
                
                case 2:
                    
                    
                    
                    if((game.globalTimer>0 && game.globalTimer<60) || (game.globalTimer>240 && game.globalTimer<300) || (game.globalTimer>560 && game.globalTimer<680) || (game.globalTimer>810 && game.globalTimer<840) || (game.globalTimer>860 && game.globalTimer<880)){
                        
                        tempMap.lighting[y][x]=loadedMap.defaultLighting[y][x];
                        
                    }
                    
                    else{
                        
                        tempMap.lighting[y][x]=loadedMap.defaultLighting[y][x]/1.3;
                        
                    }
                    
                
                
            }
            
        }
        
    }
    */
    //Check triggers
    
    if(!game.noClip){
        //Floor tiles
        var tile = loadedMap.triggers[floor(player.y/20)][floor(player.x/20)];
        
        if(tile !== 0 && tile<=game.triggers.length-1){
            
            for(var i = 0; i <= loadedMap.triggerTypes[tile].length-1; i++){
                
                if(loadedMap.triggerTypes[tile][i] === 0){
                    
                    game.triggers[tile][i] = true;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Check enemies
    for(var i = 0; i <= game.triggers.length-1; i++){
        
        var triggered = true;
        
        for(var c = 0; c <= enemies.trigger.length-1; c++){
            
            if(enemies.trigger[c] === i && !enemies.dud[c]){
                
                triggered = false;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        for(var c = 0; c <= loadedMap.enemies.condition.length-1; c++){
            
            if(loadedMap.enemies.condition[c] === i && !game.enemiesSpawned[c]){
                
                triggered = false;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        for(var b = 0; b <= loadedMap.triggerTypes[i].length-1; b++){
            
            if(loadedMap.triggerTypes[i][b] === 2){
                
                game.triggers[i][b] = triggered;
                
            }
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Check pickups
    for(var i = 0; i <= game.triggers.length-1; i++){
        
        var triggered = true;
        
        for(var c = 0; c <= pickups.trigger.length-1; c++){
            
            if(pickups.trigger[c] === i && !pickups.dud[c]){
                
                triggered = false;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        for(var b = 0; b <= loadedMap.triggerTypes[i].length-1; b++){
            
            if(loadedMap.triggerTypes[i][b] === 3){
                
                game.triggers[i][b] = triggered;
                
            }
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Check required triggers
    for(var i = 0; i <= game.triggers.length-1; i++){
        
        var triggered = true;
        
        for(var c = 0; c <= loadedMap.triggerCondition[i].length-1; c++){
            
            if(game.triggers[loadedMap.triggerCondition[i][c]].includes(false)){
                
                triggered = false;
                
            }
            
            
        }
        
        if(loadedMap.triggerTypes[i].includes(4)){
            
            game.triggers[i][loadedMap.triggerTypes[i].indexOf(4)] = triggered;
            
            if(!triggered){
                
                for(var s = 0; s <= loadedMap.triggerTypes[i].length-1; s++){
                    
                    game.triggers[i][s] = false;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Activate things based on triggers
    for(var i = 0; i <= game.triggers.length-1; i++){
        
        //Check if its triggered
        var triggered = true;
        
        for(var s = 0; s <= game.triggers[i].length-1; s++){
            
            if(!game.triggers[i][s]){
                
                triggered=false;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //If its been triggered do things
        if(triggered){
            
            //Door open
            for(var y = 0; y <= loadedMap.doors.length-1; y++){
                
                for(var x = 0; x <= loadedMap.doors[y].length-1; x++){
                    
                    var doorSpeed = loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].speed*(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].direction);
                    
                    if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].trigger===i){
                        
                        switch(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].type){
                            
                            //Floor Lift
                            case 7:
                                
                                if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor.includes('p') && tempMap.floorHeight[y][x]>=loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].min && tempMap.floorHeight[y][x]<=loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].max){
                                    
                                    tempMap.floorHeight[y][x]+=doorSpeed*game.deltaF;
                                    
                                }
                                
                                if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor.includes('s') && tempMap.floorSecondaryHeight[y][x]>=loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].min && tempMap.floorSecondaryHeight[y][x]<=loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].max){
                                    
                                    tempMap.floorSecondaryHeight[y][x]+=doorSpeed*game.deltaF;
                                    
                                }
                                
                                tempMap.floorHeight[y][x] = constrain(tempMap.floorHeight[y][x],loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].min,loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].max);
                                
                                
                                break;
                            
                            //Ceiling Lift
                            case 8:
                                
                                if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor.includes('p') && tempMap.ceilingHeight[y][x]>=loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].min && tempMap.ceilingHeight[y][x]<=loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].max){
                                    
                                    tempMap.ceilingHeight[y][x]+=doorSpeed*game.deltaF;
                                    
                                }
                                
                                if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor.includes('s') && tempMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight[y][x]>=loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].min && tempMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight[y][x]<=loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].max){
                                    
                                    tempMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight[y][x]+=doorSpeed*game.deltaF;
                                    
                                }
                                
                                tempMap.ceilingHeight[y][x] = constrain(tempMap.ceilingHeight[y][x],loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].min,loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].max);
                                
                                break;
                            
                            //Regular door
                            default:
                                
                                if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].player){
                                    
                                    //Open when activated
                                    if(tempMap.doorOpen[y][x]>=loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].min && tempMap.doorOpen[y][x]<=loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].max && tempMap.doorActivated[y][x]){
                                        
                                        tempMap.doorOpen[y][x]+=doorSpeed*game.deltaF;
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    //Close when not
                                    if(!tempMap.doorActivated[y][x] && tempMap.doorOpen[y][x]>=loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].min && tempMap.doorOpen[y][x]<=loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].max){
                                        
                                        tempMap.doorOpen[y][x]-=doorSpeed*game.deltaF;
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    //Set the timer to close the door
                                    if(tempMap.doorActivated[y][x] && ((doorSpeed > 0 && tempMap.doorOpen[y][x] >= loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].max) || (doorSpeed < 0 && tempMap.doorOpen[y][x] <= loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].min)) && tempMap.doorTimeOpen[y][x]<= 0){
                                        
                                        tempMap.doorTimeOpen[y][x] = 300;
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    //Run down the timer and tell the door to close when its over.
                                    if(tempMap.doorTimeOpen[y][x]>0){
                                        
                                        tempMap.doorTimeOpen[y][x] -= game.deltaF;
                                        
                                        if(tempMap.doorTimeOpen[y][x] <= 0){
                                            
                                            tempMap.doorActivated[y][x] = false;
                                            
                                        }
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    if(tempMap.doorOpen[y][x]>=loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].min && tempMap.doorOpen[y][x]<=loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].max){
                                        
                                        tempMap.doorOpen[y][x]+=doorSpeed*game.deltaF;
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                }
                                
                                if(tempMap.doorOpen[y][x]<0){
                                    
                                    tempMap.doorOpen[y][x]=0;
                                    
                                }
                                
                                if(tempMap.doorOpen[y][x]>1){
                                    
                                    tempMap.doorOpen[y][x]=1;
                                    
                                }
                                
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            var states = ['alert','ambush','idle','hunting','attacking'];
            
            //Enemy spawn
            for(var c = 0; c <= loadedMap.enemies.start.length-1; c++){
                
                if(!loadedMap.enemies.start[c] && !game.enemiesSpawned[c] && loadedMap.enemies.spawnTrigger[c] === i){
                    
                    enemies.x.push(loadedMap.enemies.x[c]);
                    enemies.y.push(loadedMap.enemies.y[c]);
                    enemies.speedX.push(0);
                    enemies.speedY.push(0);
                    enemies.vertSpeed.push(0);
                    enemies.moving.push(false);
                    enemies.faceAngle.push(0);
                    enemies.angle.push(loadedMap.enemies.angle[c]);
                    enemies.state.push(states[loadedMap.enemies.state[c]]);
                    enemies.moveTime.push(0);
                    enemies.cooldown.push(0);
                    enemies.health.push(enemyConstants[loadedMap.enemies.type[c]].health);
                    enemies.verticalPos.push(loadedMap.enemies.z[i]);
                    enemies.animation.push(0);
                    enemies.animationFrame.push(0);
                    enemies.animationTime.push(0);
                    enemies.type.push(loadedMap.enemies.type[c]);
                    enemies.trigger.push(loadedMap.enemies.condition[c]);
                    enemies.dud.push(false);
                    enemies.levelID.push(c);
                    
                    enemies.lastSeen.push([]);
                    enemies.target.push([]);
                    enemies.path.push([]);
                    enemies.next.push([]);
                    
                    game.enemiesSpawned[c]=true;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            //Pickup spawn
            for(var c = 0; c <= loadedMap.pickups.start.length-1; c++){
                
                if(!loadedMap.pickups.start[c] && !game.pickupsSpawned[c] && loadedMap.pickups.spawnTrigger[c] === i){
                    
                    
                    pickups.x.push(loadedMap.pickups.x[c]);
                    pickups.y.push(loadedMap.pickups.y[c]);
                    pickups.type.push(loadedMap.pickups.type[c]);
                    pickups.trigger.push(loadedMap.pickups.condition[c]);
                    
                    pickups.dud.push(false);
                    
                    pickups.levelID.push(c);
                    
                    game.pickupsSpawned[c]=true;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
        }
        
    }
    
};

//Place objects in the map
var updateObjects=function(){
    
    //Reset variables
    objects.x=[];
    objects.y=[];
    
    objects.speedX=[];
    objects.speedY=[];
    
    objects.angle=[];
    
    objects.transparency=[];
    
    objects.verticalPos=[];
    
    objects.animation=[];
    
    objects.animationFrame=[];
    
    objects.type=[];
    
    objects.playerDistances=[];
    
    objects.cameraAngles=[];
    
    objects.cameraDistances=[];
    
    objects.realDistances=[];
    
    objects.inView=[];
    
    objects.drawn=[];
    
    //Projectiles
    for(var i = 0; i <= projectiles.x.length-1; i++){
        
        var inZone = false;
        
        var y = floor(projectiles.y[i]/20);
        var x = floor(projectiles.x[i]/20);
        
        //println(loadedMap.loadZone);
        var zone = loadedMap.loadZone[y][x];
        var overlap = loadedMap.loadOverlap[y][x];
        
        inZone = (player.zones[0] === zone || player.zones[0] === overlap) || (player.zones[1] === zone || player.zones[1] === overlap);
        
        if(!projectiles.dud[i] && (game.cam<2 || inZone)){
            
            objects.x.push(projectiles.x[i]);
            objects.y.push(projectiles.y[i]);
            
            objects.angle.push(projectiles.angle[i]);
            
            objects.animation.push(projectiles.animation[i]);
            
            objects.animationFrame.push(projectiles.animationFrame[i]);
            
            objects.transparency.push(objectConstants[projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].object].transparency);
            
            objects.verticalPos.push(projectiles.verticalPos[i]);
            
            objects.type.push(projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].object);
            
            objects.inView.push(false);
            
            objects.drawn.push(false);
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Enemies
    for(var i = 0; i <= enemies.x.length-1; i++){
        
        var inZone = false;
        
        var y = floor(enemies.y[i]/20);
        var x = floor(enemies.x[i]/20);
        
        var zone = loadedMap.loadZone[y][x];
        var overlap = loadedMap.loadOverlap[y][x];
        
        inZone = (player.zones[0] === zone || player.zones[0] === overlap) || (player.zones[1] === zone || player.zones[1] === overlap);
        
        if(!enemies.dud[i] && (game.cam<2 || (inZone && enemies.inView[i]))){
            
            objects.x.push(enemies.x[i]);
            objects.y.push(enemies.y[i]);
            
            objects.angle.push(enemies.angle[i]);
            
            objects.animation.push(enemies.animation[i]);
            
            objects.animationFrame.push(enemies.animationFrame[i]);
            
            objects.transparency.push(objectConstants[enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].object].transparency);
            
            objects.verticalPos.push(enemies.verticalPos[i]-300 + enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].height/2);
            
            objects.type.push(enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].object);
            
            objects.inView.push(false);
            
            objects.drawn.push(false);
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Pickups
    for(var i = 0; i <= pickups.x.length-1; i++){
        
        var inZone = false;
        
        var y = floor(pickups.y[i]/20);
        var x = floor(pickups.x[i]/20);
        
        var zone = loadedMap.loadZone[y][x];
        var overlap = loadedMap.loadOverlap[y][x];
        
        inZone = (player.zones[0] === zone || player.zones[0] === overlap) || (player.zones[1] === zone || player.zones[1] === overlap);
        
        if(!pickups.dud[i] && (game.cam<2 || inZone)){
            
            objects.x.push(pickups.x[i]);
            objects.y.push(pickups.y[i]);
            
            objects.angle.push(0);
            
            objects.animation.push(0);
            
            objects.animationFrame.push(0);
            
            objects.transparency.push(objectConstants[pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].object].transparency);
            
            objects.verticalPos.push(-300+objectConstants[pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].object].height/2 + primaryOrSecondary(pickups.x[i],pickups.y[i],'floor'));
            
            objects.type.push(pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].object);
            
            objects.inView.push(false);
            
            objects.drawn.push(false);
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Decor
    for(var i = 0; i <= loadedMap.decor.x.length-1; i++){
        
        var inZone = false;
        
        var y = floor(loadedMap.decor.y[i]/20);
        var x = floor(loadedMap.decor.x[i]/20);
        
        var zone = loadedMap.loadZone[y][x];
        var overlap = loadedMap.loadOverlap[y][x];
        
        inZone = (player.zones[0] === zone || player.zones[0] === overlap) || (player.zones[1] === zone || player.zones[1] === overlap);
        
        if(game.cam<2 || inZone){
            
            objects.x.push(loadedMap.decor.x[i]);
            objects.y.push(loadedMap.decor.y[i]);
            
            objects.angle.push(0);
            
            objects.animation.push(0);
            
            objects.animationFrame.push(0);
            objects.transparency.push(objectConstants[loadedMap.decor.object[i]].transparency);
            
            //objects.verticalPos.push(objectConstants[pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].object].verticalPos + primaryOrSecondary(pickups.x[i],pickups.y[i],'floor'));
            
            objects.verticalPos.push(loadedMap.decor.z[i]);
            
            objects.type.push(loadedMap.decor.object[i]);
            
            objects.inView.push(false);
            
            objects.drawn.push(false);
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Particles
    for(var i = 0; i <= particles.x.length-1; i++){
        
        var inZone = false;
        
        var y = floor(particles.y[i]/20);
        var x = floor(particles.x[i]/20);
        
        var zone = loadedMap.loadZone[y][x];
        var overlap = loadedMap.loadOverlap[y][x];
        
        inZone = (player.zones[0] === zone || player.zones[0] === overlap) || (player.zones[1] === zone || player.zones[1] === overlap);
        
        if(!particles.dud[i] && (game.cam<2 || inZone)){
            
            objects.x.push(particles.x[i]);
            objects.y.push(particles.y[i]);
            
            if(objectConstants[particleConstants[particles.type[i]].object].multiAngle){
                
                objects.angle.push(atan2(particles.speedX[i],particles.speedY[i])+180);
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                objects.angle.push(0);
                
            }
            
            objects.animation.push(particles.animation[i]);
            
            objects.animationFrame.push(particles.animationFrame[i]);
            
            objects.transparency.push(particles.transparency[i]);
            
            objects.verticalPos.push(particles.verticalPos[i]);
            
            objects.type.push(particleConstants[particles.type[i]].object);
            
            objects.inView.push(false);
            
            objects.drawn.push(false);
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Do one last pass through for variables if in first person
    if(game.cam===2){
        
        //Calulate Distances and angles (Objects)
        for(var i = 0; i <= objects.x.length-1; i++){
            
            objects.playerDistances[i]=dist(player.x,player.y,objects.x[i],objects.y[i]);
            
            //objects.cameraDistances[i]=dist(game.camera.x,game.camera.y,objects.x[i],objects.y[i]);
             
            objects.cameraDistances[i]=(objects.x[i] - game.camera.x)*game.camera.normX + (objects.y[i]-game.camera.y)*game.camera.normY;
            
            objects.realDistances[i] = sqrt(sq(objects.cameraDistances[i])+sq((game.camera.height-(objects.verticalPos[i]/2))/20));
            
            objects.cameraAngles[i]=-1*(atan2(game.camera.x-objects.x[i],game.camera.y-objects.y[i])-180);
            
            //Calculate angle between object and player.
            var objAngle = atan2(game.camera.x-objects.x[i],game.camera.y-objects.y[i]);
            
            //Shift perspective of player.angle.
            var playerAngle = abs(player.angle-360);
            
            //Convert object angle to 360°.
            if(objAngle<0){
                
                objAngle+=360;
                
            }
            
            var angle;
            
            //Calculate relative angle
            angle = objAngle - playerAngle;
            //Make sure the relative angle <= 180.
            if(angle > 180){
                
                angle -= 360;
                
            }
            
            if(angle < -180){
                
                angle += 360;
                
            }
            
            //text(playerAngle,20,40);
            //text(objAngle,20,50);
            //text(angle,20,60);
            
            //Check if player is facing object.
            if(abs(angle)>60){
                
                objects.inView[i]=false;
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                objects.inView[i]=true;
                
            }
            
            //Check which side you're looking at
            {
                
                //Find true angle
                var trueAngle=objects.cameraAngles[i]-objects.angle[i];
                
                if(trueAngle>360){
                    
                    trueAngle-=360;
                    
                }
                
                if(trueAngle<0){
                    
                    trueAngle+=360;
                    
                }
                
                //Set side
                if(objectConstants[objects.type[i]].multiAngle){
                    
                    if(trueAngle>337.5 || trueAngle<=22.5){
                        
                        objects.side[i]=0;
                        
                    }
                    
                    else if(trueAngle>22.5 && trueAngle<=67.5){
                        
                        objects.side[i]=1;
                        
                    }
                    
                    else if(trueAngle>67.5 && trueAngle<=112.5){
                        
                        objects.side[i]=2;
                        
                    }
                    
                    else if(trueAngle>112.5 && trueAngle<=157.5){
                        
                        objects.side[i]=3;
                        
                    }
                    
                    else if(trueAngle>157.5 && trueAngle<=202.5){
                        
                        objects.side[i]=4;
                        
                    }
                    
                    else if(trueAngle>202.5 && trueAngle<=247.5){
                        
                        objects.side[i]=5;
                        
                    }
                    
                    else if(trueAngle>247.5 && trueAngle<=292.5){
                        
                        objects.side[i]=6;
                        
                    }
                    
                    else if(trueAngle>292.5 && trueAngle<=337.5){
                        
                        objects.side[i]=7;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                else{
                    
                    objects.side[i] = 0;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Create a new temp list
        var tempObjects={
            
            x:[],
            y:[],
            
            angle:[],
            
            transparency:[],
            
            verticalPos:[],
            
            animation:[],
            
            animationFrame:[],
            
            type:[],
            
            realDistances:[],
            
            playerDistances:[],
            
            cameraDistances:[],
            
            cameraAngles:[],
            
            side:[],
            
            inView:[],
            
            drawn:[],
            
        };
        
        //Now remove the ones not in view by adding only them to a temp array
