        for(var i = 0; i <= objects.x.length-1; i++){
            
            if(objects.inView[i] && objects.cameraDistances[i]<=game.renderDistance && objects.verticalPos[i] + game.realHeight/2 >= primaryOrSecondary(objects.x[i],objects.y[i],'floor') && objects.verticalPos[i] + game.realHeight/2 <= primaryOrSecondary(objects.x[i],objects.y[i],'ceiling')){
                
                tempObjects.x.push(objects.x[i]);
                tempObjects.y.push(objects.y[i]);
                tempObjects.angle.push(objects.angle[i]);
                tempObjects.transparency.push(objects.transparency[i]);
                tempObjects.verticalPos.push(objects.verticalPos[i]);
                tempObjects.animation.push(objects.animation[i]);
                tempObjects.animationFrame.push(objects.animationFrame[i]);
                tempObjects.type.push(objects.type[i]);
                tempObjects.realDistances.push(objects.realDistances[i]);
                tempObjects.playerDistances.push(objects.playerDistances[i]);
                tempObjects.cameraDistances.push(objects.cameraDistances[i]);
                tempObjects.cameraAngles.push(objects.cameraAngles[i]);
                tempObjects.side.push(objects.side[i]);
                tempObjects.inView.push(objects.inView[i]);
                tempObjects.drawn.push(objects.drawn[i]);
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Reset the object array
        objects.x=[];
        objects.y=[];
        
        objects.speedX=[];
        objects.speedY=[];
        
        objects.angle=[];
        
        objects.transparency=[];
        
        objects.verticalPos=[];
        
        objects.animation=[];
        
        objects.animationFrame=[];
        
        objects.type=[];
        
        objects.realDistances=[];
        
        objects.playerDistances=[];
        
        objects.cameraAngles=[];
        
        objects.cameraDistances=[];
        
        objects.inView=[];
        
        objects.drawn=[];
        
        objects.side = [];
        
        //Now recreate the object array with the objects in order
        for(var s = 0; s <= tempObjects.x.length-1; s++){
            
            //Reset variables for loop
            var farthestDistance=0;
            
            var i = 0;
            
            for(var o = 0; o <= tempObjects.x.length-1; o++){
                
                //Find which object is closest
                if(tempObjects.cameraDistances[o]>farthestDistance && !tempObjects.drawn[o]){
                    
                    farthestDistance=tempObjects.cameraDistances[o];
                    
                    i=o;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            
            //Set variable to make sure the object isnt drawn again.
            tempObjects.drawn[i]=true;
            
            //Now we add info back to the object array
            objects.x.push(tempObjects.x[i]);
            objects.y.push(tempObjects.y[i]);
            objects.angle.push(tempObjects.angle[i]);
            objects.transparency.push(tempObjects.transparency[i]);
            objects.verticalPos.push(tempObjects.verticalPos[i]);
            objects.animation.push(tempObjects.animation[i]);
            objects.animationFrame.push(tempObjects.animationFrame[i]);
            objects.type.push(tempObjects.type[i]);
            objects.realDistances.push(tempObjects.realDistances[i]);
            objects.playerDistances.push(tempObjects.playerDistances[i]);
            objects.cameraDistances.push(tempObjects.cameraDistances[i]);
            objects.cameraAngles.push(tempObjects.cameraAngles[i]);
            objects.side.push(tempObjects.side[i]);
            objects.inView.push(true);
            objects.drawn.push(false);
            
        }
        
    }
    
};

//Check if a circle is being intersected
var hitCircle = function(centerX,centerY,originX,originY,directionX,directionY,radius,startX,startY){
    
    //Center of circle
    var center = [centerX,centerY];
    
    //Origin of ray
    var origin = [originX,originY];
    
    //Direction of ray
    var direction = [directionX,directionY];
    
    var d = sqrt(sq(directionX)+sq(directionY));
    
    direction[0] /= d;
    direction[1] /= d;
    
    //Relative origin
    var oc = [(center[0]-origin[0]),(center[1]-origin[1])];
    
    //componenets of the quadratic equation
    var a = sq(d);
    
    var h = direction[0]*oc[0] + direction[1]*oc[1];
    
    var c = sq(oc[0])+sq(oc[1]) - sq(radius);
    
    //Discriminant
    var discriminant = sq(h) - (a*c);
    
    //Distance the ray travels to intersect the circle
    var t = (h - sqrt(discriminant)) / a;
    
    direction[0] *= t;
    direction[1] *= t;
    
    //var int = PVector.add(origin,direction);
    var int = {x:origin[0]+direction[0],y:origin[1]+direction[1]};
    
    if(t < 0 || int.x !== int.x || int.y !== int.y){
        
        
        t = -1;
        
    }
    
    var tVec = [directionX,directionY];
    
    var start = [startX,startY];
    
    //var newTVal = start.dist(int);
    var newTVal = dist(start[0],start[1],int[0],int[1]);
    
    tVec[0]*=newTVal;
    tVec[1]*=newTVal;
    
    var cVec = [game.camera.dirX,game.camera.dirY];
    
    var newT = (tVec[0]*cVec[0] + tVec[1]*cVec[1]) / sqrt(sq(cVec[0])+sq(cVec[1]));
    
    return [t,int,newT];
    
    
};

//What position and speed you are given if you touch a line
var lineBoundaryReturn = function(x1,x2,y1,y2,n1,n2,px,py,vx,vy){
    
    //Total speed
    var s = dist(0,0,vx,vy);
    
    //The two slopes we'll use to check for intersection
    var a = (y2-y1)/(x2-x1);
    var b = vy/vx;

    //Find the x intercept
    var Ix = (py - y1 + (a*x1) - (b*px))/(a-b);
    
    var Iy = b * (Ix-px) + py;
    
    var t = (Ix-px)/(vx/s);
    
    if(abs(b) === Infinity){
        
        Ix = px;
        
        Iy = a * (px - x1) + y1;
        
        if(Iy === py){
            
            t = px-Ix;
            
        }
        
        else{
            
            t = (Iy-py)/(vy/s);
            
        }
        
    }
    
    if(abs(a) === Infinity){
        
        Ix = x1;
        
        Iy = b * (x1-px) + py;
        
        if(Iy === py){
            
            t = px-Ix;
            
        }
        
        else{
            
            t = (Iy-py)/(vy/s);
            
        }
        
    }
    
    
    //How much it will have to move to reach it
    
    //Set the normal (unit vector)
    var N = [n1,n2];
    
    var nd=dist(0,0,n1,n2);
    
    N[0]/=nd;
    N[1]/=nd;
    
    //Set the velocity vector
    var v = [vx,vy];
    
    //dot product
    var k = v[0]*N[0]+v[1]*N[1];
    
    //New vector to be applied 
    var c = [N[0]*k*1.0000001,N[1]*k*1.0000001];
    
    //New suggested velocity
    var vf = {x:v[0],y:v[1]};
    
    vf.x-=c[0];
    vf.y-=c[1];
    //stroke(255, 0, 0);
    
    //Figure out the range for the intersection
    var xl;
    var xr;
    
    if(x1<x2){
        
        xl = x1;
        xr = x2;
        
    }
    
    else{
        
        xl = x2;
        xr = x1;
        
    }
    
    var yl;
    var yr;
    
    if(y1<y2){
        
        yl = y1;
        yr = y2;
        
    }
    
    else{
        
        yl = y2;
        yr = y1;
        
    }
    
    var collision = ((t >= 0 || (t<0 && t>-0.5)) && t<s && ((xl<Ix && xr>Ix) || xl === xr) && ((yl<Iy && yr>Iy) || yl === yr) && k<0);
    
    return [collision,Ix,Iy,vf,t,s];
    
};

//What position and speed you are given if you touch a circle
var circleBoundaryReturn = function(x,y,r,a1,a2,px,py,vx,vy){
    
    //Find the intersection 
    var ans = hitCircle(x,y,px,py,vx,vy,r);
    
    //Find the player's total speed and make a vector out of it
    var s = dist(0,0,vx,vy);
    var v = [vx,vy];
    
    //Parse out the intersection
    var Ix = ans[1].x;
    var Iy = ans[1].y;
    var t = ans[0];
    
    //Normal Vector
    var N = [Ix - x,Iy - y];
    
    var nd = dist(0,0,N[0],N[1]);
    
    N[0]/=nd;
    N[1]/=nd;
    
    //dot product
    var k = v[0]*N[0] + v[1]*N[1];
    
    //New vector to be applied 
    var c = [N[0],N[1]];
    
    c[0]*= k*1.0000001;
    c[1]*= k*1.0000001;
    
    //New suggested velocity
    var vf = {x:v[0],y:v[1]};
    
    vf.x-=c[0];
    vf.y-=c[1];
    
    //figure out the limits for angles
    var aMin;
    var aMax;
    
    if(a1<a2){
        
        aMin = a1;
        aMax = a2;
        
    }
    
    else{
        
        aMin = a2;
        aMax = a1;
        
    }
    
    //Find the actual angles
    var ca = abs(atan2((Ix-x),(Iy-y))-180);
    
    var collision = ((t >= 0 || (t<0 && t>-0.1)) && t<s && ca >= aMin && ca <= aMax && k<=0);
    
    Ix += N[0]*0.1;
    Iy += N[1]*0.1;
    
    return [collision,Ix,Iy,vf,t,s];
    
};

//Turning a thin line into one that stops the player (returns info for line boundaries)
var constructLineBoundary = function(x1,x2,y1,y2,width){
    
    //Start and end
    var startR = new PVector(x1,y1);
    
    var endR = new PVector(x2,y2);
    
    //Direction vectors
    var normal = new PVector(-(y2-y1),x2-x1);
    
    var antiNormal = new PVector(y2-y1,-(x2-x1));
    
    var parallel = new PVector(x2-x1,y2-y1);
    
    var antiParallel = new PVector(-(x2-x1),-(y2-y1));
    
    //Make them as long as the boundary around the line
    normal.normalize();
    normal.mult(width);
    
    antiNormal.normalize();
    antiNormal.mult(width);
    
    parallel.normalize();
    parallel.mult(width);
    
    antiParallel.normalize();
    antiParallel.mult(width);
    
    //Create the start and end of each boundary
    var normL={
        
        s:PVector.add(PVector.add(startR,antiParallel),normal),
        e:PVector.add(PVector.add(endR,parallel),normal),
        n:normal,
        
    };
    
    var aNormL={
        
        s:PVector.add(PVector.add(startR,antiParallel),antiNormal),
        e:PVector.add(PVector.add(endR,parallel),antiNormal),
        n:antiNormal,
        
    };
    
    var parL={
        
        s:PVector.add(PVector.add(endR,parallel),normal),
        e:PVector.add(PVector.add(endR,parallel),antiNormal),
        n:parallel,
        
    };
    
    var aParL={
        
        s:PVector.add(PVector.add(startR,antiParallel),normal),
        e:PVector.add(PVector.add(startR,antiParallel),antiNormal),
        n:antiParallel,
        
    };
    
    return [[normL.s,normL.e,normL.n],[aNormL.s,aNormL.e,aNormL.n],[parL.s,parL.e,parL.n],[aParL.s,aParL.e,aParL.n],];
    
};

//Add a new boundary to the list
var addBoundary = function(x1,y1,x2,y2,normal,radius,ang1,ang2,type,tile,check,oWidth){
    
    var dupe = false;
    
    if(check){
        
        for(var s = boundaries.x1.length-1; s >= 0; s--){
            
            if(boundaries.x1.length-1>0){
                
                s = constrain(s,-1,boundaries.x1.length-1);
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                break;
                
            }
            
            //Make sure we aren't checking something against itself
            if(!(tile[0] === boundaries.tile[s][0] && tile[1] === boundaries.tile[s][1])){
                
                //If we find a set of lines
                if(type === 'line' && boundaries.type[s] === 'line' && (normal[0] === 0 || normal[1]===0) && (boundaries.normal[s][0] === 0 || boundaries.normal[s][1]===0) && (abs(normal[0]) === 1 || abs(normal[1])===1) && (abs(boundaries.normal[s][0]) === 1 || abs(boundaries.normal[s][1]) === 1) && !(boundaries.normal[s][0]===normal[0] && boundaries.normal[s][1]===normal[1])){
                    
                    //var normalILength = dist(0,0,boundaries.normal[i][0],boundaries.normal[i][1]);
                    //var normalSLength = dist(0,0,boundaries.normal[s][0],boundaries.normal[s][1]);
                    
                    var x1I = x1 - (normal[0])*(oWidth/2);
                    var x2I = x2 - (normal[0])*(oWidth/2);
                    var x1S = boundaries.x1[s] - (boundaries.normal[s][0])*(oWidth/2);
                    var x2S = boundaries.x2[s] - (boundaries.normal[s][0])*(oWidth/2);
                    var y1I = y1 - (normal[1])*(oWidth/2);
                    var y2I = y2 - (normal[1])*(oWidth/2);
                    var y1S = boundaries.y1[s] - (boundaries.normal[s][1])*(oWidth/2);
                    var y2S = boundaries.y2[s] - (boundaries.normal[s][1])*(oWidth/2);
                    
                    var minIX = min(x1I,x2I);
                    var maxIX = max(x1I,x2I);
                    var minSX = min(x1S,x2S);
                    var maxSX = max(x1S,x2S);
                    var minIY = min(y1I,y2I); 
                    var maxIY = max(y1I,y2I);
                    var minSY = min(y1S,y2S);
                    var maxSY = max(y1S,y2S);
                    
                    //println((minIX === minSX) +', ' + (maxIX === maxSX) +', ' + (minIY === minSY) +', ' + (maxIY === maxSY));
                    
                    if(minIX === minSX && maxIX === maxSX && minIY === minSY && maxIY === maxSY){
                        
                        boundaries.x1.splice(s,1);
                        boundaries.y1.splice(s,1);
                        boundaries.x2.splice(s,1);
                        boundaries.y2.splice(s,1);
                        boundaries.normal.splice(s,1);
                        boundaries.radius.splice(s,1);
                        boundaries.ang1.splice(s,1);
                        boundaries.ang2.splice(s,1);
                        boundaries.type.splice(s,1);
                        boundaries.tile.splice(s,1);
                        
                        dupe = true;
                        break;
                        
                    }
                    
                    else{
                        
                        //println((minIX === minSX) +', ' + (maxIX === maxSX) +', ' + (minIY === minSY) +', ' + (maxIY === maxSY));
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
        }
        
    }
    
    if(!dupe){
        
        boundaries.x1.push(x1);
        boundaries.y1.push(y1);
        boundaries.x2.push(x2);
        boundaries.y2.push(y2);
        boundaries.normal.push(normal);
        boundaries.radius.push(radius);
        boundaries.ang1.push(ang1);
        boundaries.ang2.push(ang2);
        boundaries.type.push(type);
        boundaries.tile.push(tile);
        
    }
    
};

//Combine boundaries and stop things from moving through them
var checkBoundaries = function(ix,iy,vx,vy,top,bottom,oWidth,stairs,restrictEnemies,restrictPlayer){
    
    var collision = true;
    var loops = 0;
    var lastTagged = -1;
    
    var lastX = ix;
    var lastY = iy;
    
    var rloops = 0;
    
    //Check if there are solid surfaces adjacent to the hitbox
    
    //Set values to check
    var minX = 0;
    var maxX = 0;
    
    var minY = 0;
    var maxY = 0;
    
    var ox = floor(ix/20);
    var oy = floor(iy/20);
    
    var nx = floor((ix + vx*game.deltaF)/20);
    var ny = floor((iy + vy*game.deltaF)/20);
    
    if(ox<=nx){
        
        minX = ox-1;
        maxX = nx+1;
        
    }
    
    else{
        
        minX = nx-1;
        maxX = ox+1;
        
    }
    
    if(oy<=ny){
        
        minY = oy-1;
        maxY = ny+1;
        
    }
    
    else{
        
        minY = ny-1;
        maxY = oy+1;
        
    }
    
    minX = constrain(minX,0,loadedMap.boundaries[0].length-1);
    maxX = constrain(maxX,0,loadedMap.boundaries[0].length-1);
    minY = constrain(minY,0,loadedMap.boundaries.length-1);
    maxY = constrain(maxY,0,loadedMap.boundaries.length-1);
    
    //Clear boundaries
    boundaries.x1 = [];
    boundaries.y1 = [];
    boundaries.x2 = [];
    boundaries.y2 = [];
    boundaries.normal = [];
    boundaries.radius = [];
    boundaries.ang1 = [];
    boundaries.ang2 = [];
    boundaries.type = [];
    boundaries.tile = [];
    
    var index = [];
    
    //Add the boundaries based on the grid
    for(var y = minY; y <= maxY; y++){
        
        for(var x = minX; x <= maxX; x++){
            
            //println('x: ' + x);
            
            if(loadedMap.boundaries[y][x] === 1){
                
                if(loadedMap.doors[y][x] === 0){
                    
                    switch(loadedMap.shape[y][x]){
                        
                        case 0:
                            
                            {
                                
                                
                                //Left
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Right
                                addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Up
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Down
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                        case 1:
                            
                            {
                                
                                //Left
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Right
                                addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Up
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Down
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Diagonal
                                addBoundary(x*20 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 +oWidth/2,[0.7071067811865475,0.7071067811865475],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],false,oWidth);
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                        case 2:
                            
                            {
                                
                                //Left
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Right
                                addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Up
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Down
                                addBoundary(x*20+20 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Diagonal
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-0.7071067811865475,0.7071067811865475],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],false);
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                        case 3:
                            
                            {
                                
                                //Left
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Right
                                addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Up
                                addBoundary(x*20+20 -oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Down
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);

                                addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Up
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Down
                                addBoundary(x*20+10 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //In Left
                                addBoundary(x*20+10 -oWidth/2,y*20+10 -oWidth/2,x*20+10 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //In Up
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20+10 +oWidth/2,x*20+10 +oWidth/2,y*20+10 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                        case 28:
                            
                            {
                                
                                var a = constructLineBoundary(x*20,x*20+20,y*20+20,y*20,oWidth/2);
                                
                                for(var i = 0; i <= a.length-1; i++){
                                    
                                    addBoundary(a[i][0].x,a[i][0].y,a[i][1].x,a[i][1].y,[a[i][2].x,a[i][2].y],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                        case 29:
                            
                            {
                                
                                var a = constructLineBoundary(x*20,x*20+20,y*20,y*20+20,oWidth/2);
                                
                                for(var i = 0; i <= a.length-1; i++){
                                    
                                    addBoundary(a[i][0].x,a[i][0].y,a[i][1].x,a[i][1].y,[a[i][2].x,a[i][2].y],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                        default:
                            
                            {
                                
                                //Place holder assume its a solid block
                                
                                //Left
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Right
                                addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Up
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Down
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                            
                    }
                    
                }
                
                else{
                    
                    var doorOpen = tempMap.doorOpen[y][x];
                    
                    switch(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].type){
                        
                        //Slide Left and Right
                        case 0:
                            
                            if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor === 'left'){
                                
                                var a = constructLineBoundary(x*20,x*20 + (doorOpen*20), y*20 + 10, y*20+10,oWidth/2);
                                
                                for(var i = 0; i <= a.length-1; i++){
                                    
                                    addBoundary(a[i][0].x,a[i][0].y,a[i][1].x,a[i][1].y,[a[i][2].x,a[i][2].y],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],false);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor === 'right'){
                                
                                var a = constructLineBoundary(x*20 + ((1-doorOpen)*20),x*20 + 20, y*20 + 10, y*20+10,oWidth/2);
                                
                                for(var i = 0; i <= a.length-1; i++){
                                    
                                    addBoundary(a[i][0].x,a[i][0].y,a[i][1].x,a[i][1].y,[a[i][2].x,a[i][2].y],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],false);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                        //Slide Up and Down
                        case 1:
                            
                            if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor === 'left'){
                                
                                var a = constructLineBoundary(x*20 +10,x*20 +10, y*20 + 20 - (doorOpen*20), y*20+20,oWidth/2);
                                
                                for(var i = 0; i <= a.length-1; i++){
                                    
                                    addBoundary(a[i][0].x,a[i][0].y,a[i][1].x,a[i][1].y,[a[i][2].x,a[i][2].y],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],false);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor === 'right'){
                                
                                var a = constructLineBoundary(x*20 +10,x*20 +10, y*20, y*20 + (doorOpen*20),oWidth/2);
                                
                                for(var i = 0; i <= a.length-1; i++){
                                    
                                    addBoundary(a[i][0].x,a[i][0].y,a[i][1].x,a[i][1].y,[a[i][2].x,a[i][2].y],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],false);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                        //Full Block
                        case 2:
                            
                            if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor === 'left'){
                                
                                //Left
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2 ,x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20+doorOpen*20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Right
                                addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 +20 * (1-doorOpen)-oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 +20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Up
                                addBoundary(x*20 +20*(1-doorOpen)-oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 + 20 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Down
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20*doorOpen +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                
                            }
                            
                            if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor === 'right'){
                                
                                //Left
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 + (1-doorOpen)*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 +20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Right
                                addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 +20*doorOpen +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Up
                                addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 + 20*doorOpen + oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                //Down
                                addBoundary(x*20 +20*(1-doorOpen)-oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+ 20 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                
                                
                            }
                            
                            
                            
                            break;
                        
                        //Swing Door bottom
                        case 3:
                            
                            if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor === 'left'){
                                
                                var a = constructLineBoundary(x*20+0.001,x*20 + cos(90-90*doorOpen)*20, y*20 +20, y*20 + 20 - sin(90-90*doorOpen)*20,oWidth/2);
                                
                                for(var i = 0; i <= a.length-1; i++){
                                    
                                    addBoundary(a[i][0].x,a[i][0].y,a[i][1].x,a[i][1].y,[a[i][2].x,a[i][2].y],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor === 'right'){
                                
                                var a = constructLineBoundary(x*20 + 20 - cos(90-90*doorOpen)*20,x*20+0.001 +20, y*20 + 20 - sin(90-90*doorOpen)*20, y*20 +20,oWidth/2);
                                
                                for(var i = 0; i <= a.length-1; i++){
                                    
                                    addBoundary(a[i][0].x,a[i][0].y,a[i][1].x,a[i][1].y,[a[i][2].x,a[i][2].y],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                    
                                }
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                        //Swing Door top
                        case 4:
                            
                            if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor === 'right'){
                                
                                var a = constructLineBoundary(x*20+0.001,x*20 + cos(90-90*doorOpen)*20, y*20, y*20 +  sin(90-90*doorOpen)*20,oWidth/2);
                                
                                for(var i = 0; i <= a.length-1; i++){
                                    
                                    addBoundary(a[i][0].x,a[i][0].y,a[i][1].x,a[i][1].y,[a[i][2].x,a[i][2].y],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor === 'left'){
                                
                                var a = constructLineBoundary(x*20+20+0.001,x*20 + 20 - cos(90-90*doorOpen)*20, y*20, y*20 + sin(90-90*doorOpen)*20,oWidth/2);
                                
                                for(var i = 0; i <= a.length-1; i++){
                                    
                                    addBoundary(a[i][0].x,a[i][0].y,a[i][1].x,a[i][1].y,[a[i][2].x,a[i][2].y],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                        //Swing Door left
                        case 5:
                            
                            if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor === 'left'){
                                
                                var a = constructLineBoundary(x*20+0.001,x*20 + cos(90*doorOpen)*20, y*20, y*20 + sin(90*doorOpen)*20,oWidth/2);
                                
                                for(var i = 0; i <= a.length-1; i++){
                                    
                                    addBoundary(a[i][0].x,a[i][0].y,a[i][1].x,a[i][1].y,[a[i][2].x,a[i][2].y],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor === 'right'){
                                
                                var a = constructLineBoundary(x*20 +0.001,x*20 + cos(90*doorOpen)*20, y*20 + 20, y*20 + 20 - sin(90*doorOpen)*20,oWidth/2);
                                
                                for(var i = 0; i <= a.length-1; i++){
                                    
                                    addBoundary(a[i][0].x,a[i][0].y,a[i][1].x,a[i][1].y,[a[i][2].x,a[i][2].y],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                        //Swing Door right
                        case 6:
                            
                            if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor === 'left'){
                                
                                var a = constructLineBoundary(x*20+0.001 +20,x*20 + 20 - cos(90*doorOpen)*20, y*20 +20, y*20 + 20 - sin(90*doorOpen)*20,oWidth/2);
                                
                                for(var i = 0; i <= a.length-1; i++){
                                    
                                    addBoundary(a[i][0].x,a[i][0].y,a[i][1].x,a[i][1].y,[a[i][2].x,a[i][2].y],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[y][x]].lor === 'right'){
                                
                                var a = constructLineBoundary(x*20+0.001 +20,x*20 + 20 - cos(90*doorOpen)*20, y*20 , y*20 + sin(90*doorOpen)*20,oWidth/2);
                                
                                for(var i = 0; i <= a.length-1; i++){
                                    
                                    addBoundary(a[i][0].x,a[i][0].y,a[i][1].x,a[i][1].y,[a[i][2].x,a[i][2].y],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //println('y: ' + y);
        
    }
    
    if(stairs){
        
        bottom = top - ((top - bottom) * (2/3));
        
    }
    
    for(var y = minY; y <= maxY; y++){
        
        for(var x = minX; x <= maxX; x++){
            
            //Floor
            switch(loadedMap.floorShape[y][x]){
                
                default:
                
                    //println(bottom);
                    
                    if(tempMap.floorHeight[y][x] > bottom){
                        
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                    
                case 1:
                    
                    if(tempMap.floorHeight[y][x] > bottom){
                        
                        //Left
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Right
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Up
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Down
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Diagonal
                        addBoundary(x*20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 +oWidth/2,[1,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(tempMap.floorSecondaryHeight[y][x] > bottom){
                        
                        //Left
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Right
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Up
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Down
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Diagonal
                        addBoundary(x*20 +20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 -oWidth/2,[-1,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                    
                case 2:
                    
                    if(tempMap.floorHeight[y][x] > bottom){
                        
                        //Left
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Right
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Up
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Down
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Diagonal
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(tempMap.floorSecondaryHeight[y][x] > bottom){
                            
                            //Left
                            addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                            
                            //Right
                            addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                            
                            //Up
                            addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                            
                            //Down
                            addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                            
                            //Diagonal
                            addBoundary(x*20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 -oWidth/2,[1,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                            
                        }
                    
                    break;
                    
                case 3:
                    
                    if(tempMap.floorHeight[y][x] > bottom){
                        
                        //Left
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Right
                        addBoundary(x*20+10 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+10 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Up
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+10 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Down
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+10 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(tempMap.floorSecondaryHeight[y][x] > bottom){
                        
                        //Left
                        addBoundary(x*20+10 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+10 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Right
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Up
                        addBoundary(x*20+10 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Down
                        addBoundary(x*20+10 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                    
                case 4:
                    
                    if(tempMap.floorHeight[y][x] > bottom){
                        
                        //Left
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+10 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Right
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+10 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Up
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Down
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+10 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+10 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                            
                        }
                    
                    if(tempMap.floorSecondaryHeight[y][x] > bottom){
                        
                        //Left
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+10 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Right
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+10 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Up
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+10 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+10 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Down
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                    
            }
            
            //Ceiling
            switch(loadedMap.ceilingShape[y][x]){
                
                default:
                    
                    if(tempMap.ceilingHeight[y][x] < top){
                        
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                
                case 1:
                    
                    if(tempMap.ceilingHeight[y][x] < top){
                        
                        //Left
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Right
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Up
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Down
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Diagonal
                        addBoundary(x*20 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20 +oWidth/2,[1,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(tempMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight[y][x] < top){
                        
                        //Left
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Right
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Up
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Down
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Diagonal
                        addBoundary(x*20 +20 -oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 -oWidth/2,[-1,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                    
                case 2:
                    
                    if(tempMap.ceilingHeight[y][x] < top){
                        
                        //Left
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Right
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Up
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Down
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Diagonal
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(tempMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight[y][x] < top){
                            
                            //Left
                            addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                            
                            //Right
                            addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                            
                            //Up
                            addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                            
                            //Down
                            addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                            
                            //Diagonal
                            addBoundary(x*20 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 -oWidth/2,[1,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                            
                        }
                    
                    break;
                    
                case 3:
                    
                    if(tempMap.ceilingHeight[y][x] < top){
                        
                        //Left
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Right
                        addBoundary(x*20+10 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+10 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Up
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+10 +oWidth/2,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Down
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+10 +oWidth/2,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(tempMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight[y][x] < top){
                        
                        //Left
                        addBoundary(x*20+10 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+10 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Right
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Up
                        addBoundary(x*20+10 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Down
                        addBoundary(x*20+10 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                    
                case 4:
                    
                    if(tempMap.ceilingHeight[y][x] < top){
                        
                        //Left
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+10 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Right
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+10 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Up
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Down
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+10 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+10 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                            
                        }
                    
                    if(tempMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight[y][x] < top){
                        
                        //Left
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+10 -oWidth/2,x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[-1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Right
                        addBoundary(x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+10 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[1,0],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Up
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+10 -oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+10 -oWidth/2,[0,-1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                        //Down
                        addBoundary(x*20 -oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,x*20+20 +oWidth/2+0.001,y*20+20 +oWidth/2,[0,1],0,0,0,'line',[x,y],true,oWidth);
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                    
            }
            
        }
        
    }
    
    if(restrictEnemies){
        
        for(var e = 0; e <= enemies.x.length-1; e++){
            
            if(!enemies.dud[e] && top >= enemies.verticalPos[e] && bottom <= enemies.verticalPos[e] + enemyConstants[enemies.type[e]].height){
                
                //circle
                addBoundary(enemies.x[e],enemies.y[e],0,0,[1,1],enemyConstants[enemies.type[e]].width/2+oWidth/2,-1,360,'circle',[floor(enemies.x[e]/20),floor(enemies.y[e]/20)],false);
                
            }
        }
        
    }
    
    if(restrictPlayer){
        
        //circle
        addBoundary(player.x,player.y,0,0,[1,1],player.width/2+oWidth/2,-1,360,'circle',[floor(player.x/20),floor(player.x/20)],false);
        
    }
    
    var impact = false;
    
    while(collision){
        
        collision = false;
        
        var l = dist(0,0,vx,vy);
        
        var tList = [];
        var tID = [];
        
        //Player collision
        
        for(var i = 0; i <= boundaries.x1.length-1; i++){
            
            if(boundaries.type[i] === 'line'){
                
                var answer = lineBoundaryReturn(boundaries.x1[i],boundaries.x2[i],boundaries.y1[i],boundaries.y2[i],boundaries.normal[i][0],boundaries.normal[i][1],ix,iy,vx*game.deltaF,vy*game.deltaF);
                
                if(answer[0]){
                    
                    tList.push(answer);
                    tID.push(i);
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            if(boundaries.type[i] === 'circle'){
                
                var answer = circleBoundaryReturn(boundaries.x1[i],boundaries.y1[i],boundaries.radius[i],boundaries.ang1[i],boundaries.ang2[i],ix,iy,vx*game.deltaF,vy*game.deltaF);
                
                if(answer[0]){
                    
                    tList.push(answer);
                    tID.push(i);
                    
                }
                
            }
            
        }
        
        var bestT = Infinity;
        var bestI = -1;
        
        for (var i = 0; i <= tList.length-1; i++){
            
            if(tList[i][4]<bestT && tID[i] !== lastTagged){
                
                bestT = tList[i][4];
                
                bestI = i;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        if(tList.length > 0 && bestI > -1){
            
            //if(dist(ix,iy,lastX,lastY)>=1){
            
            ix = tList[bestI][1];
            iy = tList[bestI][2];
            
            lastX = ix;
            lastY = iy;
            
            vx = tList[bestI][3].x/game.deltaF;
            vy = tList[bestI][3].y/game.deltaF;
            
            lastTagged = tID[bestI];
            
            collision = true;
            
            impact = true;
            
            
        }
        
        //lastTagged = tID[bestI];
        
        loops ++;
        
        if(stairs && loops>2 && dist(0,0,vx,vy)<player.maxSpeed/2){
            
            collision = false;
            
            vx = 0;
            vy = 0;
            
        }
        
        if(loops>10){
            
            collision = false;
            
            vx = 0;
            vy = 0;
            
        }
        
    }
    
    
    //println('end');
    
    //println(game.globalTimer);
    
    //if(restrictEnemies){println(rloops);println(boundaries.x1.length);println(enemies.x.length);}
    
    return [ix,iy,vx,vy,impact];
    
};

//Add stuff for the dev tools
var devAdd = function(func){
    
    switch(func){
        
        case 'x':
            
            //Add to grid X
            for(var y = 0; y <= loadedMap.layout.length-1; y++){
                 
                loadedMap.layout[y].push(0);
                loadedMap.boundaries[y].push(0);
                loadedMap.lighting[y].push(1);
                tempMap.lighting[y].push(1);
                loadedMap.defaultLighting[y].push(1);
                loadedMap.dynamicLighting[y].push(0);
                loadedMap.floorLayout[y].push(0);
                loadedMap.ceilingLayout[y].push(0);
                loadedMap.doors[y].push(0);
                loadedMap.doorOpen[y].push(0);
                loadedMap.defaultDoorOpen[y].push(0);
                loadedMap.triggers[y].push(0);
                loadedMap.loadZone[y].push(0);
                loadedMap.loadOverlap[y].push(0);
                loadedMap.shape[y].push(0);
                loadedMap.floorHeight[y].push(0);
                loadedMap.ceilingHeight[y].push(game.defaultCeilingHeight);
                loadedMap.floorSecondaryHeight[y].push(0);
                loadedMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight[y].push(game.defaultCeilingHeight);
                loadedMap.floorShape[y].push(0);
                loadedMap.ceilingShape[y].push(0);
                loadedMap.floorWallLayout[y].push(1);
                loadedMap.ceilingWallLayout[y].push(1);
                
                tempMap.doorOpen[y].push(0);
                tempMap.floorHeight[y].push(0);
                tempMap.ceilingHeight[y].push(game.defaultCeilingHeight);
                tempMap.floorSecondaryHeight[y].push(0);
                tempMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight[y].push(game.defaultCeilingHeight);
                
            }
            
            break;
        
        case 'y':
            
            loadedMap.layout.push([]);
            loadedMap.boundaries.push([]);
            loadedMap.lighting.push([]);
            tempMap.lighting.push([]);
            loadedMap.defaultLighting.push([]);
            loadedMap.dynamicLighting.push([]);
            loadedMap.floorLayout.push([]);
            loadedMap.ceilingLayout.push([]);
            loadedMap.doors.push([]);
            loadedMap.doorOpen.push([]);
            loadedMap.defaultDoorOpen.push([]);
            loadedMap.triggers.push([]);
            loadedMap.loadZone.push([]);
            loadedMap.loadOverlap.push([]);
            loadedMap.shape.push([]);
            loadedMap.floorHeight.push([]);
            tempMap.floorHeight.push([]);
            loadedMap.ceilingHeight.push([]);
            tempMap.ceilingHeight.push([]);
            loadedMap.floorSecondaryHeight.push([]);
            tempMap.floorSecondaryHeight.push([]);
            loadedMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight.push([]);
            tempMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight.push([]);
            loadedMap.floorShape.push([]);
            loadedMap.ceilingShape.push([]);
            loadedMap.floorWallLayout.push([]);
            loadedMap.ceilingWallLayout.push([]);
            
            tempMap.doorOpen.push([]);
            
            //Add to grid Y
            for(var y = 0; y <= loadedMap.layout[0].length-1; y++){
                
                loadedMap.layout[loadedMap.layout.length-1].push(0);
                loadedMap.boundaries[loadedMap.boundaries.length-1].push(0);
                loadedMap.lighting[loadedMap.lighting.length-1].push(1);
                tempMap.lighting[loadedMap.lighting.length-1].push(1);
                loadedMap.defaultLighting[loadedMap.defaultLighting.length-1].push(1);
                loadedMap.dynamicLighting[loadedMap.defaultLighting.length-1].push(0);
                loadedMap.floorLayout[loadedMap.floorLayout.length-1].push(0);
                loadedMap.ceilingLayout[loadedMap.ceilingLayout.length-1].push(0);
                loadedMap.doors[loadedMap.doors.length-1].push(0);
                loadedMap.doorOpen[loadedMap.doorOpen.length-1].push(0);
                loadedMap.defaultDoorOpen[loadedMap.defaultDoorOpen.length-1].push(0);
                loadedMap.triggers[loadedMap.triggers.length-1].push(0);
                loadedMap.loadZone[loadedMap.loadZone.length-1].push(0);
                loadedMap.loadOverlap[loadedMap.loadOverlap.length-1].push(0);
                loadedMap.shape[loadedMap.shape.length-1].push(0);
                loadedMap.floorHeight[loadedMap.floorHeight.length-1].push(0);
                tempMap.floorHeight[loadedMap.floorHeight.length-1].push(0);
                loadedMap.ceilingHeight[loadedMap.floorHeight.length-1].push(game.defaultCeilingHeight);
                tempMap.ceilingHeight[loadedMap.floorHeight.length-1].push(game.defaultCeilingHeight);
                
                loadedMap.floorSecondaryHeight[loadedMap.floorHeight.length-1].push(0);
                tempMap.floorSecondaryHeight[loadedMap.floorHeight.length-1].push(0);
                loadedMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight[loadedMap.floorHeight.length-1].push(game.defaultCeilingHeight);
                tempMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight[loadedMap.floorHeight.length-1].push(game.defaultCeilingHeight);
                
                loadedMap.floorShape[loadedMap.floorHeight.length-1].push(0);
                loadedMap.ceilingShape[loadedMap.floorHeight.length-1].push(0);
                
                loadedMap.floorWallLayout[loadedMap.floorHeight.length-1].push(1);
                loadedMap.ceilingWallLayout[loadedMap.floorHeight.length-1].push(1);
                
                tempMap.doorOpen[tempMap.doorOpen.length-1].push(0);
                
            }
            
            
            break;
        
        case 'enemy':
            
            
            
            break;
        
        case 'pickup':
            
            
            
            break;
        
    }
    
};

//How do you draw a wall
var drawWall = function(x,ty,by,minY,maxY,d,t,l,lerp,wallX){
    
    
    noStroke();
    rectMode(CORNER);
    
    var texture = loadedMap.assets.walls.textures[t];
    var colors = loadedMap.assets.walls.color;
    
    wallX = constrain(wallX,0,0.999);
    
    var texX = floor((texture.length)*wallX);
    
    //if(x===game.width/2){println(texX);}
    
    switch(loadedMap.assets.walls.scaling[t]){
        
        case 'stretch':
            
            //Find height and middle point
            var v = ty-by;
            
            var m = by + v/2;
            
            //Find max and min of texture
            var highest = -Infinity;
            var lowest = Infinity;
            
            for(var y = 0; y <= texture[texX].length-1; y++){
                
                var higher = max(texture[texX][y][0],texture[texX][y][1]);
                var lower = min(texture[texX][y][0],texture[texX][y][1]);
                
                highest=max(higher,highest);
                lowest=min(lower,lowest);
                
            }
            
            //dif between highest and lowest part of texture
            var texHeight = highest-lowest;
            
            //Ratio of texture height and drawn height
            var ratio = abs(v/texHeight);
            
            //Draw texture
            for(var y = 0; y <= texture[texX].length-1; y++){
                
                //Set color
                var rayColor = color(255, 0, 255);
                
                //Has transparency value
                if(colors[texture[texX][y][2]].length>3){
                    
                    rayColor = color(colors[texture[texX][y][2]][0]*l,colors[texture[texX][y][2]][1]*l,colors[texture[texX][y][2]][2]*l,colors[texture[texX][y][2]][3]*255);
                    
                }
                
                //No transparency value
                else{
                    
                    rayColor = color(colors[texture[texX][y][2]][0]*l,colors[texture[texX][y][2]][1]*l,colors[texture[texX][y][2]][2]*l);
                    
                }
                
                rayColor=lerpColor(rayColor,color(0,0,0),1-lerp);
                
                fill(rayColor);
                
                
                var low = min(-texture[texX][y][1],-texture[texX][y][0]);
                var high = max(-texture[texX][y][1],-texture[texX][y][0]);
                
                var yl = (high-low)*ratio;
                
                var c = m + low*ratio;
                
                if(c<maxY && c+yl>minY){
                    
                    if(c<minY){
                        
                        yl -= minY-c;
                        
                    }
                    
                    c = max(c,minY);
                    
                    yl = min(yl,abs(maxY-c));
                    
                    if(maxY>c){
                        
                        rect(x,c-0.1,1,yl+0.2);
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            break;
            
        case 'top':
            
            
            //Find height and middle point
            
            //Find max and min of texture
            var highest = -Infinity;
            var lowest = Infinity;
            
            for(var y = 0; y <= texture[texX].length-1; y++){
                
                var higher = max(texture[texX][y][0],texture[texX][y][1]);
                var lower = min(texture[texX][y][0],texture[texX][y][1]);
                
                highest=max(higher,highest);
                lowest=min(lower,lowest);
                
            }
            
            //dif between highest and lowest part of texture
            var texHeight = highest-lowest;
            
            var m = by + texHeight/(2*d);
            
            //Draw texture
            for(var y = 0; y <= texture[texX].length-1; y++){
                
                //Set color
                var rayColor = color(255, 0, 255);
                
                //Has transparency value
                if(colors[texture[texX][y][2]].length>3){
                    
                    rayColor = color(colors[texture[texX][y][2]][0]*l,colors[texture[texX][y][2]][1]*l,colors[texture[texX][y][2]][2]*l,colors[texture[texX][y][2]][3]*255);
                    
                }
                
                //No transparency value
                else{
                    
                    rayColor = color(colors[texture[texX][y][2]][0]*l,colors[texture[texX][y][2]][1]*l,colors[texture[texX][y][2]][2]*l);
                    
                }
                
                rayColor=lerpColor(rayColor,color(0,0,0),1-lerp);
                
                fill(rayColor);
                
                
                var low = min(-texture[texX][y][1],-texture[texX][y][0]);
                var high = max(-texture[texX][y][1],-texture[texX][y][0]);
                
                var yl = (high-low)/d;
                
                var c = m + low/d;
                
                if(c<ty && c+yl>by && c<maxY && c+yl>minY){
                    
                    if(c<by && c<minY){
                        
                        yl -= by-c;
                        
                    }
                    
                    c = max(c,by);
                    
                    c = max(c,minY);
                    
                    yl = min(yl,abs(ty-c));
                    yl = min(yl,abs(maxY-c));
                    
                    if(ty>c){
                        
                        rect(x,c-0.1,1,yl+0.2);
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            break;
        
        case 'bottom':
            
            /*
            tbd
            //Find height and middle point
            var v = ty-by;
            
            var m = by + v/2;
            
            //Find max and min of texture
            var highest = -Infinity;
            var lowest = Infinity;
            
            for(var y = 0; y <= texture[texX].length-1; y++){
                
                var higher = max(texture[texX][y][0],texture[texX][y][1]);
                var lower = min(texture[texX][y][0],texture[texX][y][1]);
                
                highest=max(higher,highest);
                lowest=min(lower,lowest);
                
            }
            
            //dif between highest and lowest part of texture
            var texHeight = highest-lowest;
            
            //Ratio of texture height and drawn height
            var ratio = abs(v/texHeight);
            
            //Draw texture
            for(var y = 0; y <= texture[texX].length-1; y++){
                
                //Set color
                var rayColor = color(255, 0, 255);
                
                //Has transparency value
                if(colors[texture[texX][y][2]].length>3){
                    
                    rayColor = color(colors[texture[texX][y][2]][0]*l,colors[texture[texX][y][2]][1]*l,colors[texture[texX][y][2]][2]*l,colors[texture[texX][y][2]][3]*255);
                    
                }
                
                //No transparency value
                else{
                    
                    rayColor = color(colors[texture[texX][y][2]][0]*l,colors[texture[texX][y][2]][1]*l,colors[texture[texX][y][2]][2]*l);
                    
                }
                
                rayColor=lerpColor(rayColor,color(0,0,0),1-lerp);
                
                fill(rayColor);
                
                
                var low = min(-texture[texX][y][1],-texture[texX][y][0]);
                var high = max(-texture[texX][y][1],-texture[texX][y][0]);
                
                var yl = (high-low)*ratio;
                
                var c = m + low*ratio;
                
                if(c<maxY && c+yl>minY){
                    
                    if(c<minY){
                        
                        yl -= minY-c;
                        
                    }
                    
                    c = max(c,minY);
                    
                    yl = min(yl,abs(maxY-c));
                    
                    if(maxY>c){
                        
                        rect(x,c-0.1,1,yl+0.2);
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            */
            
            break;
        
    }
    
};

//Iterable Raycast Function
var drawRayCast=function(x,ox,oy,dx,dy,mapX,mapY,minY,maxY,floorHeight,ceilingHeight,priorDist,depth,hs,dz,ih){
    
    /**Set Initial Vars**/
    
    //Quick variable to use for the center line for testing
    var cs = game.width/2 === x;
    
    //Stop recursion if we do it too many times
    if(depth>20){
        
        return;
        
    }
    
    depth++;
    
    var hitScanReturn = {
        
        //Distance the ray traveled
        t:0,
        
        //Where it stopped on the gorund or ceiling or wall
        x:0,
        y:0,
        z:0,
        
        //normals
        nx:0,
        ny:0,
        nz:0,
        
        //Which objects have been hit
        hits:[],
        
        //What map tile it hit
        mapX:0,
        mapY:0,
        
    };  
    
    //if(cs){println('recursion ' + ceilingHeight + ', ' + depth);}
    
    //Information about the current ray
    var ray={
        
        //Position
        posX:ox/20,
        posY:oy/20,
        
        //Direction Vector
        dirX:dx,
        dirY:dy,
        
        //Map position taken up by ray
        mapX:mapX,
        mapY:mapY,
        
        //Length of the ray from the current position to the next side
        sideDistX:0,
        sideDistY:0,
        
        //Length of ray from one x-side to the next y-side
        deltaDistX:0,
        delatDistY:0,
        
        //Perpendicular Wall Distance
        perpWallDist:0,
        
        //What direction to step in
        stepX:0,
        stepY:0,
        
        //Has a wall been hit?
        hit:false,
        
        //Was a verticle or horizontal wall hit?
        side:0,
        
        //How tall the line will be
        lineHeight:0,
        
        //Last floor/ceiling line dist from player
        lastDist:0,
        
        //last floor
        lastFloor:0,
        lastBrightness:0,
        
        //Drawing pixels
        drawStart:0,
        drawEnd:200,
        
        //Wall X position
        wallX:0,
        
        //What color the wall will be
        color:0,
        
        //What the default lerp is
        lerp:0,
        
    };
    
    ray.hit=false;
    
    //Check for three things, a wall, a change in floor height, and a change in ceiling height
    var wallVars={
        
        hit:false,
        
    };
    
    var floorVars={
        
        hit:false,
        
    };
    
    var ceilingVars={
        
        hit:false,
        
    };
    
    var farthest={
        
        hit:false,
        
    };
    
    ray.deltaDistX=sqrt(1+(ray.dirY*ray.dirY)/(ray.dirX*ray.dirX));
    ray.deltaDistY=sqrt(1+(ray.dirX*ray.dirX)/(ray.dirY*ray.dirY));
    
    ray.lastDist=0;
    
    //if(cs){println('initial calc: ' + ray.dirX + ', ' + ray.dirY + ', ' +  ray.deltaDistX + ',' + ray.deltaDistY);}
    
    //hitscan vars
    var fht = -1;
    var fhfx = 0;
    var fhfy = 0;
    
    var cht = -1;
    var chfx = 0;
    var chfy = 0;
    
    var ht = -1;
    var hfx = 0;
    var hfy = 0;
    var hfz = 0;
    var hfc = 'neither';
    
    var normXHS = 0;
    var normYHS = 0;
    
    //Calculate the intial step and side step
    if(ray.dirX < 0){
        
        ray.stepX=-1;
