        ray.sideDistX=(ray.posX - ray.mapX) * ray.deltaDistX;
        
    }
    
    else{
        
        ray.stepX=1;
        
        ray.sideDistX=(ray.mapX + 1.0 - ray.posX) * ray.deltaDistX;
        
    }
    
    if(ray.dirY < 0){
        
        ray.stepY=-1;
        
        ray.sideDistY=(ray.posY - ray.mapY) * ray.deltaDistY;
        
    }
    
    else{
        
        ray.stepY=1;
        
        ray.sideDistY=(ray.mapY + 1.0 - ray.posY) * ray.deltaDistY;
        
    }
    
    var limit = false;
    
    //Set up our initial look up table for objects
    if(objectList.setup){
        
        objectList.drawn = [];
        objectList.closestPoint = [];
        objectList.index = [];
        
        for(var i = 0; i <= objects.x.length-1; i++){
            
            //Ray Next Position
            var rayNextX=game.camera.x+ray.dirX;
            var rayNextY=game.camera.y+ray.dirY;
            
            //Check if the line intersects
            var closestPointToLine = (rayNextX-game.camera.x) * objects.y[i] + (game.camera.y-rayNextY)*objects.x[i] + (game.camera.x-rayNextX) * game.camera.y + (rayNextY-game.camera.y) * game.camera.x;
            
            if(abs(closestPointToLine)<objectConstants[objects.type[i]].widths[objects.animation[i]][objects.animationFrame[i]][objects.side[i]]/2){
                
                objectList.drawn.push(false);
                objectList.closestPoint.push(closestPointToLine);
                objectList.index.push(i);
                
            }
            
        }
        
        objectList.setup = false;
        
    }
    
    /**Define Functions**/
    
    //Function to draw Floor 
    var drawFloor = function(start,end,currentHeight,distance,difX,difY){
        
        //game height ratio we use for iteration so it is pixel perfect
        var heightRatio = game.height/game.realHeight;
        
        //Variables used to track if something has changed as the floor is drawn
        var lastLightningFloor=0;
        var lastLayoutFloor=0;
        
        //var used to track if we are at our starting point
        var lastPosFloor = start;
        
        //Set the params based on how the camera is oriented
        var h = game.camera.height-(currentHeight*(game.height/game.realHeight));
        var horizon = game.camera.horizon*(game.height/game.realHeight);
        
        //Check if the floor should be drawn
        if(h > -game.height/2 && horizon<game.height+4 && start<end && game.camera.height*(game.realHeight/game.height)+300 > currentHeight){
            
            //Draw the floor from the wall to the bottom of the screen
            
            var drawnEnd;
            
            for(var y = start; y <= end; y+=heightRatio){
                
                //if(cs){if(y + 1 < end && y+2>end){println(y+1 + ', ' + end);}}
                
                noStroke();
                
                if(y < end && y+heightRatio>end){
                    
                    drawnEnd = end;
                    
                }
                
                else{
                    
                    drawnEnd = y;
                    
                }
                
                if(lastPosFloor < game.height+10){
                    
                    var currentDist= ((game.height + 2*h)) / (2.0 * (y-horizon) - game.height);
                    
                    //if(cs){println('both: ' + difX + ', ' + difY);}
                    
                    var scale=(currentDist) / (distance);
                    
                    scale = constrain(scale,0,0.9999);
                    
                    var currentFloorX=ox+(difX*scale);
                    var currentFloorY=oy+(difY*scale);
                    
                    //var currentFloorX=game.camera.x+ray.dirX*currentDist*20;
                    //var currentFloorY=game.camera.y+ray.dirY*currentDist*20;
                    
                    //currentFloorX = constrain(currentFloorX,0,(loadedMap.floorLayout[0].length-1)*20);
                    //currentFloorY = constrain(currentFloorY,0,(loadedMap.floorLayout.length-1)*20);
                    
                    //if(cs && y === game.height/2 && mouseIsPressed){println('realScale: ' + (scale));}
                    
                    //Draw floor
                    {
                        
                        var pixX;
                        var pixY;
                        
                        var texX;
                        var texY;
                        
                        var cPixX;
                        var cPixY;
                        
                        var cTexX;
                        var cTexY;
                        
                        var lastColorFloor;
                        
                        var currentColorFloor;
                        
                        
                        //println(currentFloorX +', ' +currentFloorY);
                        if(loadedMap.assets.floors.type[loadedMap.floorLayout[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)]]==="texture"){
                            
                            pixX=(currentFloorX/20)-floor(currentFloorX/20);
                            pixY=(currentFloorY/20)-floor(currentFloorY/20);
                            
                            texX=floor(loadedMap.assets.floors.textures[lastLayoutFloor].length*pixX);
                            texY=floor(loadedMap.assets.floors.textures[lastLayoutFloor].length*pixY);
                            
                        }
                        
                        
                        //Set first line
                        if(y === start ){
                            
                            lastLightningFloor=tempMap.lighting[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)];
                            
                            lastLayoutFloor=loadedMap.floorLayout[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)];
                            
                            //Set Texture color value
                            if(loadedMap.assets.floors.type[lastLayoutFloor]==="texture"){
                                
                                lastColorFloor=loadedMap.assets.floors.textures[lastLayoutFloor][texX][texY];
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                lastColorFloor=lastLayoutFloor;
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                        //Define Current Varibles
                        {
                            
                            //Set current color for texture
                            if(floorConstants.type[loadedMap.floorLayout[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)]]==="texture"){
                                
                                currentColorFloor=floorConstants.textures[loadedMap.floorLayout[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)]][texX][texY];
                                
                            }
                            
                            //Set current color for everything else
                            else{
                                
                                currentColorFloor=loadedMap.floorLayout[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)];
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                        if(currentColorFloor!==lastColorFloor || tempMap.lighting[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)]!==lastLightningFloor || y+ heightRatio >= maxY || y+heightRatio >= end){
                            
                            //Draw Line
                            if((lastLightningFloor!==1 && loadedMap.floorBox[3])|| lastLayoutFloor!==0){
                                
                                //Empty but lighted
                                if(lastColorFloor===0){
                                    
                                    fill(loadedMap.floorBox[0]*lastLightningFloor,loadedMap.floorBox[1]*lastLightningFloor,loadedMap.floorBox[2]*lastLightningFloor);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                //Different color
                                else{
                                    
                                    //Mono
                                    if(loadedMap.assets.floors.type[lastLayoutFloor]==="mono"){
                                        
                                        fill(loadedMap.assets.floors.color[lastColorFloor][0]*lastLightningFloor,loadedMap.assets.floors.color[lastColorFloor][1]*lastLightningFloor,loadedMap.assets.floors.color[lastColorFloor][2]*lastLightningFloor);
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    //Textured
                                    if(loadedMap.assets.floors.type[lastLayoutFloor]==="texture"){
                                        
                                        fill(loadedMap.assets.floors.color[lastColorFloor][0]*lastLightningFloor,loadedMap.assets.floors.color[lastColorFloor][1]*lastLightningFloor,loadedMap.assets.floors.color[lastColorFloor][2]*lastLightningFloor);
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                }
                                
                                if((lastLayoutFloor !== 0 || lastLightningFloor !== 1) && (lastPosFloor !== y || drawnEnd !== y)){
                                    
                                    //Draw actual line
                                    //line(x,lastPosFloor-1,x,y);
                                    
                                    var l = drawnEnd-lastPosFloor;
                                    
                                    noStroke();
                                    rectMode(CORNER);
                                    rect(x,lastPosFloor-0.2, 1, l+0.4);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            
                            //Set variables for new line
                            lastPosFloor=y;
                            
                            lastLightningFloor=tempMap.lighting[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)];
                            
                            lastLayoutFloor=loadedMap.floorLayout[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)];
                            
                            //Texture
                            if(loadedMap.assets.floors.type[lastLayoutFloor]==="texture"){
                                
                                lastColorFloor=loadedMap.assets.floors.textures[lastLayoutFloor][texX][texY];
                                
                            }
                            
                            //Everything else
                            else{
                                
                                lastColorFloor=lastLayoutFloor;
                                
                            }
                            
                            
                        }
                        
                        
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
        }
        
    };
    
    //Function to draw Ceiling
    var drawCeiling = function(start,end,currentHeight,distance,difX,difY){
        
        //game height ratio we use for iteration so it is pixel perfect
        var heightRatio = game.height/game.realHeight;
        
        //var h = game.camera.height-(currentHeight*(game.height/game.realHeight));
        var h = game.camera.height-(currentHeight*(game.height/game.realHeight))+game.height;
        
        var horizon = game.camera.horizon*(game.height/game.realHeight);
        
        var lastPosCeiling=start;
        var lastLightningCeiling=0;
        var lastLayoutCeiling=0;
        
        if(h < game.height/2 && horizon>-(game.height)/2 && start>end){
            
            var drawnEnd;
            
            //Draw the ceiling from the wall to the top of the screen
            for(var y = start; y >= end; y-=heightRatio){
                
                noStroke();
                
                if(y > end && y-heightRatio<end){
                    
                    drawnEnd = end;
                    
                }
                else{
                    
                    drawnEnd = y;
                    
                }
                
                //Temp height limit
                if(lastPosCeiling > 0){
                    
                    var currentDist= ((2*h - game.height) / ((2.0 * (y-horizon) - game.height)));
                    
                    var scale=(currentDist) / (distance);
                    
                    scale = constrain(scale,0,0.9999);
                    
                    var currentFloorX=ox+(difX*scale);
                    var currentFloorY=oy+(difY*scale);
                    
                    //Draw ceiling
                    {
                            
                            var cPixX;
                            var cPixY;
                            
                            var cTexX;
                            var cTexY;
                            
                            var lastColorCeiling;
                            var currentColorCeiling;
                            
                            if(loadedMap.assets.ceilings.type[loadedMap.ceilingLayout[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)]]==="texture"){
                                
                                var cPixX=(currentFloorX/20)-floor(currentFloorX/20);
                                var cPixY=(currentFloorY/20)-floor(currentFloorY/20);
                                
                                var cTexX=floor(loadedMap.assets.ceilings.textures[lastLayoutCeiling].length*cPixX);
                                var cTexY=floor(loadedMap.assets.ceilings.textures[lastLayoutCeiling].length*cPixY);
                                
                            }
                            
                            
                            //Set first line
                            if(y === start){
                                
                                lastLightningCeiling=tempMap.lighting[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)];
                                
                                lastLayoutCeiling=loadedMap.ceilingLayout[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)];
                                
                                //Set Texture color value
                                if(loadedMap.assets.ceilings.type[lastLayoutCeiling]==="texture"){
                                    
                                    lastColorCeiling=loadedMap.assets.ceilings.textures[lastLayoutCeiling][cTexX][cTexY];
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    lastColorCeiling=lastLayoutCeiling;
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            
                            //Define Current Varibles
                            {
                                
                                //Set current color for texture
                                if(loadedMap.assets.ceilings.type[loadedMap.ceilingLayout[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)]]==="texture"){
                                    
                                    currentColorCeiling=loadedMap.assets.ceilings.textures[loadedMap.ceilingLayout[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)]][cTexX][cTexY];
                                    
                                }
                                
                                //Set current color for everything else
                                else{
                                    
                                    currentColorCeiling=loadedMap.ceilingLayout[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)];
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            if(currentColorCeiling!==lastColorCeiling || tempMap.lighting[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)]!==lastLightningCeiling || y-heightRatio <= minY|| y-heightRatio <= end){
                                
                                //Draw Line
                                if((lastLightningCeiling!==1 && loadedMap.skyBox[3]) || lastLayoutCeiling!==0){
                                    
                                    //Empty but lighted
                                    if(lastColorCeiling===0){
                                        
                                        fill(loadedMap.skyBox[0]*lastLightningCeiling,loadedMap.skyBox[1]*lastLightningCeiling,loadedMap.skyBox[2]*lastLightningCeiling);
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    //Different color
                                    else{
                                        
                                        //Mono
                                        if(loadedMap.assets.ceilings.type[lastLayoutCeiling]==="mono"){
                                            
                                            fill(loadedMap.assets.ceilings.color[lastColorCeiling][0]*lastLightningCeiling,loadedMap.assets.ceilings.color[lastColorCeiling][1]*lastLightningCeiling,loadedMap.assets.ceilings.color[lastColorCeiling][2]*lastLightningCeiling);
                                            
                                        }
                                        
                                        //Textured
                                        if(loadedMap.assets.ceilings.type[lastLayoutCeiling]==="texture"){
                                            
                                            fill(loadedMap.assets.ceilings.color[lastColorCeiling][0]*lastLightningCeiling,loadedMap.assets.ceilings.color[lastColorCeiling][1]*lastLightningCeiling,loadedMap.assets.ceilings.color[lastColorCeiling][2]*lastLightningCeiling);
                                            
                                        }
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    //line(x,game.height-(lastPosCeiling-((game.height*2)/game.realHeight)),x,game.height-y);
                                    
                                    if((lastLayoutCeiling !== 0 || lastLightningCeiling !== 1) && (lastPosCeiling !== y || drawnEnd !== y)){
                                        
                                        //Draw actual line
                                        //line(x,lastPosCeiling+1,x,y);
                                        
                                        
                                        var l = lastPosCeiling-drawnEnd;
                                        
                                        noStroke();
                                        rectMode(CORNER);
                                        rect(x,lastPosCeiling+0.2, 1, -l-0.4);
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                }
                                
                                //Set variables for new line
                                lastPosCeiling=y;
                                
                                lastLightningCeiling=tempMap.lighting[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)];
                                
                                lastLayoutCeiling=loadedMap.ceilingLayout[floor(currentFloorY/20)][floor(currentFloorX/20)];
                                
                                //Texture
                                if(loadedMap.assets.ceilings.type[lastLayoutCeiling]==="texture"){
                                    
                                    lastColorCeiling=loadedMap.assets.ceilings.textures[lastLayoutCeiling][cTexX][cTexY];
                                    
                                }
                                
                                //Everything else
                                else{
                                    
                                    lastColorCeiling=lastLayoutCeiling;
                                    
                                }
                                
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                    
                }
                
                
            }
            
        }
        
        
    };
    
    //Set variables based on shape of wall
    var checkHitWall = function(type){
        
        var lineIntersect = function(x1,y1,x2,y2,sx,sy,dx,dy){
            
            //Return Variables
            var rx;
            var ry;
            var d;
            var wallX;
            var hit = true;
            
            if(abs(dx) < 0.0001){
                
                dx = 0;
                
            }
            
            if(abs(dy) < 0.0001){
                
                dy = 0;
                
            }
            
            //Calc equations of line seg
            
            //Find slope of target
            var b;
            
            if(x1 === x2){
                
                //Vertical line
                b = false;
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                //Regular line
                b = (y2-y1)/(x2-x1);
                
            }
            
            //Find rx
            
            //Check special conditions
            if(dx === 0){
                
                rx = sx;
                
            }
            //If we have a slope on the camera view
            else{
                
                var a = dy/dx;
                
                if(b !== false){
                    
                    rx = (-b*x1 + y1 + a*sx -sy)/(a-b);
                    
                }
                
                else{
                    
                    rx = x1;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            //Find ry
            
            //Based on b
            if(b !== false){
                
                ry = b*(rx-x1)+y1;
                
            }
            //If b is vertical
            else if(dx !== 0){
                
                var a = dy/dx;
                
                ry = a * (x1 - sx) + sy;
                
            }
            
            //If both are vertical (maybe add one if they're lined up?)
            else{
                
                hit = false;
                
            }
            
            //check if the player is facing it
            var k = 0;
            
            if(hit){
                
                k = dx*(rx-sx) + dy*(ry-sy);
                
            }
            
            //Check if it is in the range
            var maxX = max(x1,x2);
            var minX = min(x1,x2);
            
            var maxY = max(y1,y2);
            var minY = min(y1,y2);
            
            if(rx > maxX || rx < minX || ry > maxY || ry < minY || k<0){
                
                hit = false;
                
            }
            
            //Find dist and wallX
            if(hit){
                
                //d
                if(dx !== 0){
                    
                    d = (rx - sx) / ray.dirX;
                    
                }
                else{
                    
                    d = (ry - sy) / ray.dirY;
                    
                }
                
                //wallX
                if(x1!==x2){
                    
                    wallX = (rx - minX) / (maxX-minX);
                    
                }
                
                else{
                    
                    wallX = (ry - minY) / (maxY-minY);
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            return [hit,rx,ry,d,wallX];
            
        };
        
        var circleIntersect = function(cx,cy,sx,sy,dx,dy,radius,x1,y1,x2,y2){
            
            var hit = false;
            var hit2 = false;
            
            //Center of circle
            var center = [cx,cy];
            
            //Origin of ray
            var origin = [sx,sy];
            
            //Direction of ray
            var direction = [dx,dy];
            
            var d = sqrt(sq(dx)+sq(dy));
            
            direction[0] /= d;
            direction[1] /= d;
            
            //Relative origin
            var oc = [(center[0]-origin[0]),(center[1]-origin[1])];
            
            //componenets of the quadratic equation
            var a = 1;
            
            var h = direction[0]*oc[0] + direction[1]*oc[1];
            
            var c = sq(oc[0])+sq(oc[1]) - sq(radius);
            
            //Discriminant
            var discriminant = sq(h) - (a*c);
            
            //Distance the ray travels to intersect the circle
            var t = (h - sqrt(discriminant)) / a;
            var t2 = (h + sqrt(discriminant)) / a;
            
            var direction2 = [direction[0]*t2,direction[1]*t2];
            
            direction[0] *= t;
            direction[1] *= t;
            
            //var int = PVector.add(origin,direction);
            var int = {x:origin[0]+direction[0],y:origin[1]+direction[1]};
            
            //var int = PVector.add(origin,direction);
            var int2 = {x:origin[0]+direction2[0],y:origin[1]+direction2[1]};
            
            if(t < 0 || int.x-ray.mapX < x1 || int.y-ray.mapY < y1 || int.x-ray.mapX > x2 || int.y-ray.mapY > y2 || discriminant < 0){
                
                
                t = -1;
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                hit = true;
                
            }
            
            if(t2< 0 || int2.x-ray.mapX < x1 || int2.y-ray.mapY < y1 || int2.x-ray.mapX > x2 || int2.y-ray.mapY > y2 || discriminant <= 0){
                
                
                t2 = -1;
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                hit2 = true;
                
            }
            
            var tVec = [dx,dy];
            
            var wallX = acos(constrain(int.x-cx,-1,1))/90;
            var wallX2 = acos(constrain(int2.x-cx,-1,1))/90;
            
            //var newTVal = start.dist(int);
            var newTVal = (int.x-sx)*game.camera.normX + (int.y-sy)*game.camera.normY;
            var newTVal2 = (int2.x-sx)*game.camera.normX + (int2.y-sy)*game.camera.normY;
            
            return [hit,int.x,int.y,newTVal*1.25,wallX,hit2,int2.x,int2.y,newTVal2*1.25,wallX2]; 
            
            
        };
        
        //Wall
        if(type === 'wall'){
            
            var returnWalls = {
                
                x:[],
                y:[],
                d:[],
                t:[],
                l:[],
                lerp:[],
                wallX:[],
                hit:false,
                
            };
            
            var doorType;
            
            //Check info about door
            if(loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== 0){
                
                doorType = loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]].type;
                
            }
            
            //If there is no door
            if(loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] === 0 || (loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== 0 && (doorType === 7 || doorType === 8))){
                
                var shape = constrain(loadedMap.shape[ray.mapY][ray.mapX],0,wallShapes.length-1);
                
                var layout = loadedMap.layout[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                
                var trans = loadedMap.assets.walls.transparent[layout];
                //var trans=true;
                
                var rx=[];
                var ry=[];
                var d=[];
                var wallX=[];
                var l=[];
                var lerp=[];
                
                for(var i = 0; i <= wallShapes[shape].length-1; i++){
                    
                    if(wallShapes[shape][i].type === 'line'){
                        
                        var currentLine = wallShapes[shape][i];
                        
                        var r = lineIntersect(ray.mapX + currentLine.x1,ray.mapY + currentLine.y1,ray.mapX + currentLine.x2,ray.mapY + currentLine.y2,ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                        
                        //If we hit a wall
                        if(r[0]){
                            
                            rx.push(r[1]);
                            ry.push(r[2]);
                            d.push(r[3]);
                            wallX.push(r[4]);
                            lerp.push(currentLine.lerp);
                            
                            var ly = constrain(ray.mapY + currentLine.ly,0,tempMap.lighting.length-1);
                            var lx = constrain(ray.mapX + currentLine.lx,0,tempMap.lighting[ly].length-1);
                            
                            l.push(tempMap.lighting[ly][lx]);
                            
                            //stroke(255,0,0);
                            //line(x,200/r[3] + game.height/2,x,-200/r[3] + game.height/2);
                            
                            returnWalls.hit = true;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(wallShapes[shape][i].type === 'circle'){
                        
                        var currentCircle = wallShapes[shape][i];
                        //cx,cy,sx,sy,dx,dy,radius,x1,y1,x2,y2
                        var r = circleIntersect(ray.mapX+currentCircle.cx,ray.mapY+currentCircle.cy,ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY,currentCircle.r,currentCircle.x1,currentCircle.y1,currentCircle.x2,currentCircle.y2);
                        
                        //if(cs){println(r);}
                        
                        //If we hit a wall
                        if(r[0]){
                            
                            rx.push(r[1]);
                            ry.push(r[2]);
                            d.push(r[3]);
                            wallX.push(r[4]);
                            lerp.push(currentCircle.lerp);
                            
                            var ly = constrain(ray.mapY + currentCircle.ly,0,tempMap.lighting.length-1);
                            var lx = constrain(ray.mapX + currentCircle.lx,0,tempMap.lighting[ly].length-1);
                            
                            l.push(tempMap.lighting[ly][lx]);
                            
                            returnWalls.hit = true;
                            
                        }
                        
                        //If we hit a wall
                        if(r[5]){
                            
                            rx.push(r[6]);
                            ry.push(r[7]);
                            d.push(r[8]);
                            wallX.push(r[9]);
                            lerp.push(currentCircle.lerp);
                            
                            var ly = constrain(ray.mapY + currentCircle.ly,0,tempMap.lighting.length-1);
                            var lx = constrain(ray.mapX + currentCircle.lx,0,tempMap.lighting[ly].length-1);
                            
                            l.push(tempMap.lighting[ly][lx]);
                            
                            returnWalls.hit = true;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //Only add the nearest wall if they aren't transparent
                if(!trans){
                    
                    //Find Closest
                    var closestVal = Infinity;
                    var closestI = -1;
                    
                    for(var e = 0; e <= d.length-1; e++){
                        
                        if(d[e] < closestVal){
                            
                            closestVal = d[e];
                            closestI = e;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(closestI>-1){
                        
                        returnWalls.x.push(rx[closestI]);
                        returnWalls.y.push(ry[closestI]);
                        returnWalls.d.push(d[closestI]);
                        returnWalls.t.push(loadedMap.layout[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]);
                        returnWalls.l.push(l[closestI]);
                        returnWalls.lerp.push(lerp[closestI]);
                        returnWalls.wallX.push(wallX[closestI]);
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //If they are, add them all
                else{
                    
                    returnWalls.x=rx.slice();
                    returnWalls.y=ry.slice();
                    returnWalls.d=d.slice();
                    returnWalls.wallX=wallX.slice();
                    returnWalls.lerp = lerp.slice();
                    returnWalls.l= l.slice();
                    
                    for(var e = 0; e <= rx.length-1; e++){
                        
                        returnWalls.t.push(loadedMap.layout[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]);
                        
                        
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            //Ignore the shape if there is a door
            else{
                
                var rt = [];
                var rlerp = [];
                var rlighting = [];
                
                var layout = loadedMap.layout[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                
                var trans = loadedMap.assets.walls.transparent[layout];
                //var trans=true;
                
                var rx=[];
                var ry=[];
                var d=[];
                var wallX=[];
                var l=[];
                var lerp=[];
                
                switch(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]].type){
                    
                    //Slide Left and Right
                    case 0:
                        
                        if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]].lor === 'left'){
                            
                            var doorOpen = tempMap.doorOpen[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                            
                            var wallXOffset = doorOpen;
                            
                            var r = lineIntersect(ray.mapX,ray.mapY + 0.5,ray.mapX + wallXOffset,ray.mapY + 0.5,ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            
                            rt.push(r.slice());
                            
                            rt[rt.length-1][4]+=1-wallXOffset;
                            
                            rlerp.push(0.8);
                            
                            rlighting.push([0,0]);
                            
                        }
                        
                        if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]].lor === 'right'){
                            
                            var doorOpen = tempMap.doorOpen[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                            
                            var wallXOffset = doorOpen;
                            
                            var r = lineIntersect(ray.mapX+1-wallXOffset,ray.mapY + 0.5,ray.mapX + 1,ray.mapY + 0.5,ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            
                            rt.push(r.slice());
                            
                            rt[rt.length-1][4]-=1-wallXOffset;
                            
                            rlerp.push(0.8);
                            
                            rlighting.push([0,0]);
                            
                        }
                        
                        break;
                    
                    
                    //Slide Up and Down
                    case 1:
                        
                        if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]].lor === 'left'){
                            
                            var doorOpen = tempMap.doorOpen[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                            
                            var wallXOffset = doorOpen;
                            
                            var r = lineIntersect(ray.mapX + 0.5,ray.mapY,ray.mapX + 0.5,ray.mapY + wallXOffset,ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            
                            rt.push(r.slice());
                            
                            rt[rt.length-1][4]+=1-wallXOffset;
                            
                            rlerp.push(1);
                            
                            rlighting.push([0,0]);
                            
                        }
                        
                        if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]].lor === 'right'){
                            
                            var doorOpen = tempMap.doorOpen[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                            
                            var wallXOffset = doorOpen; 
                            
                            var r = lineIntersect(ray.mapX + 0.5,ray.mapY+1-doorOpen,ray.mapX + 0.5,ray.mapY + 1,ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            
                            rt.push(r.slice());
                            
                            rt[rt.length-1][4]-=1-wallXOffset;
                            
                            rlerp.push(1);
                            
                            rlighting.push([0,0]);
                            
                        }
                        
                        break;
                    
                    
                    //Full Block
                    case 2:
                        
                        if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]].lor === 'left'){
                            
                            var doorOpen = 1-tempMap.doorOpen[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                            
                            //solidSides(true,true,true,true,[1-doorOpen,1],[0,doorOpen],[0,doorOpen],[1-doorOpen,1],wallXOffset);
                            
                            var r1 = lineIntersect(ray.mapX,ray.mapY,ray.mapX,ray.mapY+1-doorOpen,ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            
                            rt.push(r1.slice());
                            
                            rt[rt.length-1][4]+=doorOpen;
                            
                            rlerp.push(1);
                            
                            if(ox/20 < ray.mapX){
                                
                                
                                rlighting.push([-1,0]);
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,0]);
                             
                            }
                            
                            var r2 = lineIntersect(ray.mapX+1,ray.mapY+doorOpen,ray.mapX+1,ray.mapY+1,ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            
                            rt.push(r2.slice());
                            
                            rt[rt.length-1][4]-=doorOpen;
                            
                            rlerp.push(1);
                            
                            if(ox/20 > ray.mapX+1){
                                
                                
                                rlighting.push([1,0]);
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,0]);
                             
                            }
                            
                            var r3 = lineIntersect(ray.mapX+1,ray.mapY,ray.mapX+doorOpen,ray.mapY,ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            
                            rt.push(r3.slice());
                            
                            rt[rt.length-1][4]-=doorOpen;
                            
                            rlerp.push(0.8);
                            
                            if(oy/20 < ray.mapY){
                                
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,-1]);
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,0]);
                             
                            }
                            
                            var r4 = lineIntersect(ray.mapX,ray.mapY+1,ray.mapX+1-doorOpen,ray.mapY+1,ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            
                            rt.push(r4.slice());
                            
                            rt[rt.length-1][4]+=doorOpen;
                            
                            rlerp.push(0.8);
                            
                            if(oy/20 > ray.mapY+1){
                                
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,1]);
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,0]);
                             
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                        if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]].lor === 'right'){
                            
                            var doorOpen = 1-tempMap.doorOpen[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                            
                            //solidSides(true,true,true,true,[0,doorOpen],[1-doorOpen,1],[1-doorOpen,1],[0,doorOpen],wallXOffset);
                            
                            var r1 = lineIntersect(ray.mapX,ray.mapY+1,ray.mapX,ray.mapY+doorOpen,ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            
                            rt.push(r1.slice());
                            
                            rt[rt.length-1][4]-=doorOpen;
                            
                            rlerp.push(1);
                            
                            if(ox/20 < ray.mapX){
                                
                                
                                rlighting.push([-1,0]);
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,0]);
                             
                            }
                            
                            var r2 = lineIntersect(ray.mapX+1,ray.mapY,ray.mapX+1,ray.mapY+1-doorOpen,ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            
                            rt.push(r2.slice());
                            
                            rt[rt.length-1][4]+=doorOpen;
                            
                            rlerp.push(1);
                            
                            if(ox/20 > ray.mapX+1){
                                
                                
                                rlighting.push([1,0]);
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,0]);
                             
                            }
                            
                            var r3 = lineIntersect(ray.mapX,ray.mapY,ray.mapX+1-doorOpen,ray.mapY,ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            
                            rt.push(r3.slice());
                            
                            rt[rt.length-1][4]+=doorOpen;
                            
                            rlerp.push(0.8);
                            
                            if(oy/20 < ray.mapY){
                                
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,-1]);
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,0]);
                             
                            }
                            
                            var r4 = lineIntersect(ray.mapX + doorOpen,ray.mapY+1,ray.mapX+1,ray.mapY+1,ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            
                            rt.push(r4.slice());
                            
                            rt[rt.length-1][4]-=doorOpen;
                            
                            rlerp.push(0.8);
                            
                            if(oy/20 > ray.mapY+1){
                                
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,1]);
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,0]);
                             
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                        break;
                    
                    
                    //Swing Door bottom
                    case 3:
                        
                        if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]].lor === 'left'){
                            
                            var doorOpen = 1-tempMap.doorOpen[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                            
                            var r = lineIntersect(ray.mapX,ray.mapY+1,ray.mapX+cos(90*doorOpen),ray.mapY+ 1-sin(90*doorOpen),ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            rt.push(r.slice());
                            
                            //Set lighting and lerp
                            if(doorOpen >= 1){
                                
                                rlerp.push(1);
                                
                                if(ox/20 < ray.mapX){
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([-1,0]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                    
                                }
                            }
                            
                            else if(doorOpen <= 0){
                                
                                rlerp.push(0.8);
                                
                                if(oy/20 > ray.mapY+1){
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,1]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                rlerp.push(0.9);
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                        if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]].lor === 'right'){
                            
                            var doorOpen = 1-tempMap.doorOpen[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                            
                            var r = lineIntersect(ray.mapX+1,ray.mapY+1,ray.mapX+1-cos(90*doorOpen),ray.mapY+ 1-sin(90*doorOpen),ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            rt.push(r.slice());
                            
                            //Set lighting and lerp
                            if(doorOpen >= 1){
                                
                                rlerp.push(1);
                                
                                if(ox/20 > ray.mapX+1){
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([1,0]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                    
                                }
                            }
                            
                            else if(doorOpen <= 0){
                                
                                rlerp.push(0.8);
                                
                                if(oy/20 > ray.mapY+1){
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,1]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                rlerp.push(0.9);
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                        break;
                    
                    //Swing Door top
                    case 4:
                        
                        if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]].lor === 'left'){
                            
                            var doorOpen = 1-tempMap.doorOpen[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                            
                            var r = lineIntersect(ray.mapX+1,ray.mapY,ray.mapX+1-cos(90*doorOpen),ray.mapY+ sin(90*doorOpen),ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            rt.push(r.slice());
                            
                            //Set lighting and lerp
                            if(doorOpen >= 1){
                                
                                rlerp.push(1);
                                
                                if(ox/20 > ray.mapX+1){
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([1,0]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                    
                                }
                            }
                            
                            else if(doorOpen <= 0){
                                
                                rlerp.push(0.8);
                                
                                if(oy/20 < ray.mapY){
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,-1]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                rlerp.push(0.9);
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                        if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]].lor === 'right'){
                            
                            var doorOpen = 1-tempMap.doorOpen[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                            
                            var r = lineIntersect(ray.mapX,ray.mapY,ray.mapX+cos(90*doorOpen),ray.mapY+ sin(90*doorOpen),ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            rt.push(r.slice());
                            
                            //Set lighting and lerp
                            if(doorOpen >= 1){
                                
                                rlerp.push(1);
                                
                                if(ox/20 < ray.mapX){
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([-1,0]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                    
                                }
                            }
                            
                            else if(doorOpen <= 0){
                                
                                rlerp.push(0.8);
                                
                                if(oy/20 < ray.mapY){
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,-1]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                rlerp.push(0.9);
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                        break;
                    
                    
                    //Swing Door left
                    case 5:
                        
                        if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]].lor === 'left'){
                            
                            var doorOpen = 1-tempMap.doorOpen[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                            
                            var r = lineIntersect(ray.mapX,ray.mapY,ray.mapX+sin(90*doorOpen),ray.mapY+ cos(90*doorOpen),ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            rt.push(r.slice());
                            
                            //Set lighting and lerp
                            if(doorOpen >= 1){
                                
                                rlerp.push(0.8);
                                
                                if(oy/20 < ray.mapY){
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,-1]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                    
                                }
                            }
                            
                            else if(doorOpen <= 0){
                                
                                rlerp.push(1);
                                
                                if(ox/20 < ray.mapX){
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([-1,0]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                rlerp.push(0.9);
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                        if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]].lor === 'right'){
                            
                            var doorOpen = 1-tempMap.doorOpen[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                            
                            var r = lineIntersect(ray.mapX,ray.mapY+1,ray.mapX+sin(90*doorOpen),ray.mapY+ 1- cos(90*doorOpen),ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            rt.push(r.slice());
                            
                            //Set lighting and lerp
                            if(doorOpen >= 1){
                                
                                rlerp.push(0.8);
                                
                                if(oy/20 > ray.mapY+1){
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,1]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                    
                                }
                            }
                            
                            else if(doorOpen <= 0){
                                
                                rlerp.push(1);
                                
                                if(ox/20 < ray.mapX){
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([-1,0]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                rlerp.push(0.9);
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                        break;
                    
                    //Swing Door right
                    case 6:
                        
                        if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]].lor === 'left'){
                            
                            var doorOpen = 1-tempMap.doorOpen[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                            
                            var r = lineIntersect(ray.mapX+1,ray.mapY+1,ray.mapX+1-sin(90*doorOpen),ray.mapY+1- cos(90*doorOpen),ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            rt.push(r.slice());
                            
                            //Set lighting and lerp
                            if(doorOpen >= 1){
                                
                                rlerp.push(0.8);
                                
                                if(oy/20 > ray.mapY+1){
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,1]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                    
                                }
                            }
                            
                            else if(doorOpen <= 0){
                                
                                rlerp.push(1);
                                
                                if(ox/20 > ray.mapX+1){
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([1,0]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                rlerp.push(0.9);
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                        if(loadedMap.doorDefs[loadedMap.doors[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]].lor === 'right'){
                            
                            var doorOpen = 1-tempMap.doorOpen[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                            
                            var r = lineIntersect(ray.mapX+1,ray.mapY,ray.mapX+1-sin(90*doorOpen),ray.mapY+ cos(90*doorOpen),ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                            rt.push(r.slice());
                            
                            //Set lighting and lerp
                            if(doorOpen >= 1){
                                
                                rlerp.push(0.8);
                                
                                if(oy/20 < ray.mapY){
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,-1]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                    
                                }
                            }
                            
                            else if(doorOpen <= 0){
                                
                                rlerp.push(1);
                                
                                if(ox/20 > ray.mapX+1){
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([1,0]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                rlerp.push(0.9);
                                
                                rlighting.push([0,0]);
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                        break;
                    
                }
                
                for(var i = 0; i <= rt.length-1; i++){
                    
                    //If we hit a wall
                    if(rt[i][0]){
                        
                        rx.push(rt[i][1]);
                        ry.push(rt[i][2]);
                        d.push(rt[i][3]);
                        wallX.push(rt[i][4]);
                        lerp.push(rlerp[i]);
                        
                        var ly = constrain(ray.mapY+rlighting[i][1],0,tempMap.lighting.length-1);
                        var lx = constrain(ray.mapX+rlighting[i][0],0,tempMap.lighting[ly].length-1);
                        
                        l.push(tempMap.lighting[ly][lx]);
                        
                        //stroke(255,0,0);
                        //line(x,200/r[3] + game.height/2,x,-200/r[3] + game.height/2);
                        
                        returnWalls.hit = true;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //Only add the nearest wall if they aren't transparent
                if(!trans){
                    
                    //Find Closest
                    var closestVal = Infinity;
                    var closestI = -1;
                    
                    for(var e = 0; e <= d.length-1; e++){
                        
                        if(d[e] < closestVal){
                            
                            closestVal = d[e];
                            closestI = e;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(closestI>-1){
                        
                        returnWalls.x.push(rx[closestI]);
                        returnWalls.y.push(ry[closestI]);
                        returnWalls.d.push(d[closestI]);
                        returnWalls.t.push(loadedMap.layout[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]);
                        returnWalls.l.push(l[closestI]);
                        returnWalls.lerp.push(lerp[closestI]);
                        returnWalls.wallX.push(wallX[closestI]);
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //If they are, add them all
                else{
                    
                    returnWalls.x=rx.slice();
                    returnWalls.y=ry.slice();
                    returnWalls.d=d.slice();
                    returnWalls.wallX=wallX.slice();
                    returnWalls.lerp = lerp.slice();
                    returnWalls.l= l.slice();
                    
                    for(var e = 0; e <= rx.length-1; e++){
                        
                        returnWalls.t.push(loadedMap.layout[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]);
                        
                        
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            return returnWalls;
            
        }
        
        //Floor and ceiling
        if(type === 'ceiling' || type === 'floor'){
            
            var shape = 0;
            var layout = 0;
            
            var currentHeight = 0;
            var newPrimaryHeight = 0;
            var newSecondaryHeight = 0;
            
            var returnWalls = {
                
                x:[],
                y:[],
                d:[],
                h:[],
                t:[],
                l:[],
                lerp:[],
                wallX:[],
                hit:false,
                
            };
            
            
            if(type === 'ceiling'){
                
                shape = loadedMap.ceilingShape[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                layout = loadedMap.ceilingWallLayout[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                
                currentHeight = ceilingHeight;
                newPrimaryHeight = tempMap.ceilingHeight[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                newSecondaryHeight = tempMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                shape = loadedMap.floorShape[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                layout = loadedMap.floorWallLayout[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                
                currentHeight = floorHeight;
                newPrimaryHeight = tempMap.floorHeight[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                newSecondaryHeight = tempMap.floorSecondaryHeight[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
                
            }
            
            var rx=[];
            var ry=[];
            var d=[];
            var h = [];
            var wallX=[];
            var li = [];
            var lerp=[];
            
            var sorted=[];
            
            for(var i = 0; i <= heightShapes[shape].length-1; i++){
                
                if(heightShapes[shape][i].type === 'line'){
                    
                    var currentLine = heightShapes[shape][i];
                    
                    
                    var r = lineIntersect(ray.mapX + currentLine.x1,ray.mapY + currentLine.y1,ray.mapX + currentLine.x2,ray.mapY + currentLine.y2,ox/20,oy/20,ray.dirX,ray.dirY);
                    
                    //If we hit a wall
                    if(r[0]){
                        
                        rx.push(r[1]);
                        ry.push(r[2]);
                        d.push(r[3]);
                        wallX.push(r[4]);
                        lerp.push(currentLine.lerp);
                    
                        var ly = constrain(ray.mapY + currentLine.ly,0,tempMap.lighting.length-1);
                        var lx = constrain(ray.mapX + currentLine.lx,0,tempMap.lighting[ly].length-1);
                        
                        li.push(tempMap.lighting[ly][lx]);
                        
                        
                        //Check if this line can cause a height change
                        switch(currentLine.h){
                            
                            case 'p':
                                
                                h.push(newPrimaryHeight);
                                
                            break;
                            
                            case 's':
                                
                                h.push(newSecondaryHeight);
                                
                            break;
                            
                            case 'b':
                                
                                h.push('two_sided');
                                
                            break;
                            
                        }
                        
                        sorted.push(false);
                        
                        returnWalls.hit = true;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            //Add all walls for now (later maybe delete ones on equal ints)
            //Find Closest
            
            var checkHeight = currentHeight;
            
            for(var l = 0; l <= d.length-1; l++){
                
                var closestVal = Infinity;
                var closestI = -1;
                
                for(var e = 0; e <= d.length-1; e++){
                    
                    if(d[e] < closestVal && !sorted[e]){
                        
                        closestVal = d[e];
                        closestI = e;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                 
                if(closestI>-1){
                    
                    sorted[closestI] = true;
                    
                    //Check if we change height before we add it
                    if(checkHeight !== h[closestI]){
                        
                        //Set the height for doubles
                        if(h[closestI] === 'two_sided'){
                            
                            if(checkHeight === newPrimaryHeight){
                                
                                h[closestI] = newSecondaryHeight;
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                h[closestI] = newPrimaryHeight;
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                        returnWalls.x.push(rx[closestI]);
                        returnWalls.y.push(ry[closestI]);
                        returnWalls.d.push(d[closestI]);
                        returnWalls.h.push(h[closestI]);
                        returnWalls.t.push(layout);
                        returnWalls.l.push(li[closestI]);
                        returnWalls.lerp.push(lerp[closestI]);
                        returnWalls.wallX.push(wallX[closestI]);
                        
                        checkHeight = h[closestI];
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            return returnWalls;
            
        }
        
        //wallX-=floor(wallX);
        
        return;
    };
    
    //Draw Objects
    var drawObjects =function(d,pd,mi,ma){
        
        noStroke();
        
        var h = game.camera.height;
        
        var horizon = game.camera.horizon*(game.height/game.realHeight);
        
        var hD = horizon;
        
        //var trueWallDistance = dist(ox,oy,floorXWall*20,floorYWall*20);
        
        if(!objectList.setup){
            
            //Draw Objects
            for(var s = 0; s <= objectList.index.length-1; s++){
                
                if(!objectList.drawn[s]){
                    
                    var i = objectList.index[s];
                    
                    var closestPointToLine = objectList.closestPoint[s];
                    
                    var drawn = objectList.drawn[s];
                    //sqrt(sq(objects.cameraDistances[i])+sq(game.camera.height/20)
                    //sqrt(sq(objects.cameraDistances[i])+sq((game.camera.height-(objects.verticalPos[i]/2))/20))
                    var realDistance = objects.cameraDistances[i];
                    var distance = realDistance*1.25;
                    var objectHeight = objectConstants[objects.type[i]].height;  
                    
                    //var distance = objects.cameraDistances[i];
                    
                    if(distance < d+1 && distance >= pd-1 && objects.verticalPos[i]+objectHeight+300>floorHeight && objects.verticalPos[i]+300<ceilingHeight){
                        
                        //There used to be code here to order the objects from farthest to closest, that's now in the updateObjects function for performance
                        
                        //There used to be code here for the detection of intersects, its in a different part of the rayCast function now
                        
                        //dist(player.x,player.y,ray.mapX*20,ray.mapY*20)
                        
                        var objX=-closestPointToLine+objectConstants[objects.type[i]].widths[objects.animation[i]][objects.animationFrame[i]][objects.side[i]]/2;
                        
                        var texPercent=objX/objectConstants[objects.type[i]].widths[objects.animation[i]][objects.animationFrame[i]][objects.side[i]];
                        
                        var texX=floor((objectConstants[objects.type[i]].texture[objects.animation[i]][objects.animationFrame[i]][objects.side[i]].length)*texPercent);
                        
                        for(var l = 0; l <= objectConstants[objects.type[i]].texture[objects.animation[i]][objects.animationFrame[i]][objects.side[i]][texX].length-1; l++){
                            
                            if(objectConstants[objects.type[i]].lit){
                                
                                //Set Color
                                fill(objectConstants[objects.type[i]].colors[objectConstants[objects.type[i]].texture[objects.animation[i]][objects.animationFrame[i]][[objects.side[i]]][texX][l][0]][0],objectConstants[objects.type[i]].colors[objectConstants[objects.type[i]].texture[objects.animation[i]][objects.animationFrame[i]][[objects.side[i]]][texX][l][0]][1],objectConstants[objects.type[i]].colors[objectConstants[objects.type[i]].texture[objects.animation[i]][objects.animationFrame[i]][[objects.side[i]]][texX][l][0]][2],objects.transparency[i]);
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                //Set Color
