                                fill(objectConstants[objects.type[i]].colors[objectConstants[objects.type[i]].texture[objects.animation[i]][objects.animationFrame[i]][[objects.side[i]]][texX][l][0]][0]*tempMap.lighting[floor(objects.y[i]/20)][floor(objects.x[i]/20)],objectConstants[objects.type[i]].colors[objectConstants[objects.type[i]].texture[objects.animation[i]][objects.animationFrame[i]][[objects.side[i]]][texX][l][0]][1]*tempMap.lighting[floor(objects.y[i]/20)][floor(objects.x[i]/20)],objectConstants[objects.type[i]].colors[objectConstants[objects.type[i]].texture[objects.animation[i]][objects.animationFrame[i]][[objects.side[i]]][texX][l][0]][2]*tempMap.lighting[floor(objects.y[i]/20)][floor(objects.x[i]/20)],objects.transparency[i]);
                                
                            }
                            
                            var vD = h / (distance/20);
                            
                            var texture = objectConstants[objects.type[i]].texture[objects.animation[i]][objects.animationFrame[i]][[objects.side[i]]][texX][l];
                            
                            var topLit = objects.verticalPos[i] + texture[1] + 300;
                            
                            var bottomLit = objects.verticalPos[i] - texture[2] + 300;
                            
                            var top = (-((topLit*(game.height/game.realHeight))-game.height/2)/(distance/20))+game.height/2+hD+vD;
                            
                            var bottom = (-((bottomLit*(game.height/game.realHeight))-game.height/2)/(distance/20))+game.height/2+hD+vD;
                            
                            if(top<ma && bottom>mi){
                                
                                top = constrain(top,mi,ma);
                                bottom = constrain(bottom,mi,ma);
                                
                                //line(x,top,x,bottom);
                                
                                var lo = top-bottom;
                                var mo = bottom + lo/2;
                                
                                rectMode(CORNER);
                                
                                rect(x,bottom-0.002,1,lo+0.004);
                                
                            }
                        }
                        
                        //Mark the drawn ones
                        objectList.drawn[s] = true;
                        
                    }
                    
                    /*
                    else if(objects.cameraDistances[i] >= d+1){
                        
                        //Break out of the loop if we reach the wall
                        break;
                        
                    }
                    */
                    
                }
                
            }
            
        }
        
    };
    
    //Check height collisions for hitscan
    var checkHitScanHeight = function(floorHeight,ceilingHeight,x,y){
        
        //Check the distance the ray travels before it hits the floor, then find the coord of the floor pixel same for the ceiling
        if(dz === 0){dz = 0.0001;}
        
        var hdx = dx/dist(0,0,dx,dy);
        var hdy = dy/dist(0,0,dx,dy);
        
        fht = (floorHeight-ih)/(20*dz);
        fhfx = ox + hdx*fht;
        fhfy = oy + hdy*fht;
        
        cht = (ceilingHeight - ih)/(20*dz);
        chfx = ox + hdx*cht;
        chfy = oy + hdy*cht;
        
        //Now see if the floor is hit or the ceiling
        if(fht>0){
            
            ht = fht;
            hfx = fhfx;
            hfy = fhfy;
            hfz = floorHeight-game.realHeight/2;
            
            hfc = 'floor';
            
        }
        
        
        if(cht>0){
            
            ht = cht;
            hfx = chfx;
            hfy = chfy;
            hfz = ceilingHeight-game.realHeight/2;
            
            hfc = 'ceiling';
            
        }
        
        var trueWallDist = dist(ox,oy,x,y);
        
        //We've hit the ground instead of a wall
        if(ht<trueWallDist){
            
            //Distance the ray traveled
            hitScanReturn.t=ht;
            
            //Where it stopped on the gorund or ceiling or wall
            hitScanReturn.x=hfx;
            hitScanReturn.y=hfy;
            hitScanReturn.z=hfz;
            
            //Set the normals
            hitScanReturn.nx=0;
            hitScanReturn.ny=0;
            hitScanReturn.nz=fht/abs(fht);
            
            //Whether it hit a ceiling or wall
            hitScanReturn.fc=hfc;
            
            hitScanReturn.mapX = floor(hfx/20);
            hitScanReturn.mapY = floor(hfy/20);
                
            //return hitScanReturn;
            
        }
        
        else{
            
            hfc = 'neither';
            
        }
        
        
        return ht<trueWallDist;
        
    };
    
    /**DDA Algorithm**/
    
    //Set the first side
    if(ray.sideDistX < ray.sideDistY){
        
        ray.side = 0;
        
    }
    
    else{
        
        ray.side = 1;
        
    } 
    
    //Before the Algorithm, on the first iteration, check if youre in a block
    //Check if ray has hit a wall
    if((!hs && loadedMap.layout[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]>0) || (hs && loadedMap.boundaries[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]>0)){
        
        var w = checkHitWall('wall');
        
        wallVars.hit=w.hit;
        wallVars.x=w.x.slice();
        wallVars.y=w.y.slice();
        wallVars.d=w.d.slice();
        wallVars.t=w.t.slice();
        wallVars.l=w.l.slice();
        wallVars.lerp=w.lerp.slice();
        wallVars.wallX=w.wallX.slice();
        
    }
    
    //Check if the floor changed height
    if((loadedMap.floorShape[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] === 0 && tempMap.floorHeight[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== floorHeight) || (loadedMap.floorShape[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== 0 && (tempMap.floorHeight[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== floorHeight||tempMap.floorSecondaryHeight[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== floorHeight))){
        
        var f = checkHitWall('floor');
        
        floorVars.hit=f.hit;
        floorVars.x=f.x.slice();
        floorVars.y=f.y.slice();
        floorVars.d=f.d.slice();
        floorVars.h=f.h.slice();
        floorVars.t=f.t.slice();
        floorVars.l=f.l.slice();
        floorVars.lerp=f.lerp.slice();
        floorVars.wallX=f.wallX.slice();
        
    }
    
    
    //Check if the ceiling changed height
    if((loadedMap.ceilingShape[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] === 0 && tempMap.ceilingHeight[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== ceilingHeight) || (loadedMap.ceilingShape[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== 0 && (tempMap.ceilingHeight[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== ceilingHeight||tempMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== ceilingHeight))){
        
        var c = checkHitWall('ceiling');
        
        ceilingVars.hit=c.hit;
        ceilingVars.x=c.x.slice();
        ceilingVars.y=c.y.slice();
        ceilingVars.d=c.d.slice();
        ceilingVars.h=c.h.slice();
        ceilingVars.t=c.t.slice();
        ceilingVars.l=c.l.slice();
        ceilingVars.lerp=c.lerp.slice();
        ceilingVars.wallX=c.wallX.slice();
        
    }
    
    //Set some variables for the floor
    
    //Use DDA Algoritm
    while(!wallVars.hit && !floorVars.hit && !ceilingVars.hit){
        
        //jump to next map square
        if(ray.sideDistX < ray.sideDistY){
            
            ray.sideDistX += ray.deltaDistX;
            
            ray.mapX += ray.stepX;
            
            ray.side = 0;
            
        }
        
        else{
            
            ray.sideDistY += ray.deltaDistY;
            
            ray.mapY += ray.stepY;
            
            ray.side = 1;
            
            ray.lastDist+=ray.deltaDistY;
            
        }
        
        //Calculate distance projected on camera (not the oblique distance!)
        if(ray.side === 0){
            
            ray.perpWallDist = (ray.mapX - ox/20 + (1 - ray.stepX) / 2) / ray.dirX;
            
            ray.wallX = ray.posY + ray.perpWallDist * ray.dirY;
            
        }
        
        else{
            
            ray.perpWallDist = (ray.mapY - oy/20 + (1 - ray.stepY) / 2) / ray.dirY;
            
            ray.wallX = ray.posX + ray.perpWallDist * ray.dirX;
            
        }
        
        //Stop it if the thing leaves the bounds
        if(ray.mapY < 0 || ray.mapX < 0 || ray.mapY > loadedMap.layout.length - 1 || ray.mapX > loadedMap.layout[constrain(ray.mapY,0,loadedMap.layout.length - 1)].length - 1){
            
            return;
            
        }
        
        //Check if the floor changed height
        if((loadedMap.floorShape[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] === 0 && tempMap.floorHeight[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== floorHeight) || (loadedMap.floorShape[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== 0 && (tempMap.floorHeight[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== floorHeight||tempMap.floorSecondaryHeight[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== floorHeight))){
            
            var f = checkHitWall('floor');
            
            floorVars.hit=f.hit;
            floorVars.x=f.x.slice();
            floorVars.y=f.y.slice();
            floorVars.d=f.d.slice();
            floorVars.h=f.h.slice();
            floorVars.t=f.t.slice();
            floorVars.l=f.l.slice();
            floorVars.lerp=f.lerp.slice();
            floorVars.wallX=f.wallX.slice();
            
        }
        
        //Check if the ceiling changed height
        if((loadedMap.ceilingShape[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] === 0 && tempMap.ceilingHeight[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== ceilingHeight) || (loadedMap.ceilingShape[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== 0 && (tempMap.ceilingHeight[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== ceilingHeight||tempMap.ceilingSecondaryHeight[ray.mapY][ray.mapX] !== ceilingHeight))){
            
            var c = checkHitWall('ceiling');
            
            ceilingVars.hit=c.hit;
            ceilingVars.x=c.x.slice();
            ceilingVars.y=c.y.slice();
            ceilingVars.d=c.d.slice();
            ceilingVars.h=c.h.slice();
            ceilingVars.t=c.t.slice();
            ceilingVars.l=c.l.slice();
            ceilingVars.lerp=c.lerp.slice();
            ceilingVars.wallX=c.wallX.slice();
            
        }
        
        //if(cs){println(ray.mapX + ', ' + ray.mapY);}
        
        //Check if ray has hit a wall
        if((!hs && loadedMap.layout[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]>0) || (hs && loadedMap.boundaries[ray.mapY][ray.mapX]>0)){
            
            var w = checkHitWall('wall');
            
            wallVars.hit=w.hit;
            wallVars.x=w.x.slice();
            wallVars.y=w.y.slice();
            wallVars.d=w.d.slice();
            wallVars.t=w.t.slice();
            wallVars.l=w.l.slice();
            wallVars.lerp=w.lerp.slice();
            wallVars.wallX=w.wallX.slice();
            
        }
        
    }
    
    /**Organize the Draw Order**/
    
    if(wallVars.hit || floorVars.hit || ceilingVars.hit){
        
        //All of our unsorted walls that need to be sorted and drawn
        var allUnsorted = {x:[],y:[],d:[],t:[],l:[],lerp:[],wallX:[],type:[],f:[],c:[],s:[]};
        
        //Whether that index has been sorted
        var sorted = [];
        
        //Add walls to unsorted
        var layout = loadedMap.layout[ray.mapY][ray.mapX];
        
        var trans = loadedMap.assets.walls.transparent[layout];
        
        if(wallVars.hit){
            
            for(var i = 0; i<= wallVars.d.length-1; i++){
                
                allUnsorted.x.push(wallVars.x[i]);
                allUnsorted.y.push(wallVars.y[i]);
                allUnsorted.d.push(wallVars.d[i]);
                allUnsorted.t.push(wallVars.t[i]);
                allUnsorted.l.push(wallVars.l[i]);
                allUnsorted.lerp.push(wallVars.lerp[i]);
                allUnsorted.wallX.push(wallVars.wallX[i]);
                
                allUnsorted.type.push('wall');
                allUnsorted.f.push(0);
                allUnsorted.c.push(0);
                allUnsorted.s.push(trans);
                
                sorted.push(false);
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Add floors to unsorted
        if(floorVars.hit){
            
            
            for(var i = 0; i<= floorVars.d.length-1; i++){
                
                allUnsorted.x.push(floorVars.x[i]);
                allUnsorted.y.push(floorVars.y[i]);
                allUnsorted.d.push(floorVars.d[i]);
                allUnsorted.f.push(floorVars.h[i]);
                allUnsorted.t.push(floorVars.t[i]);
                allUnsorted.l.push(floorVars.l[i]);
                allUnsorted.lerp.push(floorVars.lerp[i]);
                allUnsorted.wallX.push(floorVars.wallX[i]);
                
                allUnsorted.type.push('floor');
                allUnsorted.c.push(0);
                allUnsorted.s.push(true);
                
                sorted.push(false);
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Add ceilings to unsorted
        
        if(ceilingVars.hit){
            
            for(var i = 0; i<= ceilingVars.d.length-1; i++){
                
                allUnsorted.x.push(ceilingVars.x[i]);
                allUnsorted.y.push(ceilingVars.y[i]);
                allUnsorted.d.push(ceilingVars.d[i]);
                allUnsorted.c.push(ceilingVars.h[i]);
                allUnsorted.t.push(ceilingVars.t[i]);
                allUnsorted.l.push(ceilingVars.l[i]);
                allUnsorted.lerp.push(ceilingVars.lerp[i]);
                allUnsorted.wallX.push(ceilingVars.wallX[i]);
                
                allUnsorted.type.push('ceiling');
                allUnsorted.f.push(0);
                //allUnsorted.c.push(0);
                allUnsorted.s.push(true);
                
                sorted.push(false);
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Reset old objects
        var wallHit = wallVars.hit; 
        var floorHit = floorVars.hit; 
        var ceilingHit = ceilingVars.hit; 
        
        wallVars = {};
        floorVars = {};
        ceilingVars = {};
        
        //Floor height and ceiling height ot be passed to recursion
        var fh = floorHeight;
        var ch = ceilingHeight;
        
        var lMinY = minY;
        var lMaxY = maxY;
        
        //Fine tune definitions
        var h = game.camera.height;
        var horizon = game.camera.horizon*(game.height/game.realHeight);
        
        //Sort walls based on their distance
        var allSorted = {x:[],y:[],d:[],t:[],l:[],lerp:[],wallX:[],type:[],f:[],c:[],s:[],ty:[],by:[],minY:[],maxY:[],fp:[],cp:[]};
        
        for(var l = 0; l <= allUnsorted.d.length-1; l++){
            
            var closestVal = Infinity;
            var closestI = -1;
            
            for(var e = 0; e <= allUnsorted.d.length-1; e++){
                
                if(allUnsorted.d[e] < closestVal && !sorted[e]){
                    
                    closestVal = allUnsorted.d[e];
                    closestI = e;
                    
                }
                
                //Figure out the priority of equal distances
                else if(allUnsorted.d[e] === closestVal && !sorted[e]){
                    
                    switch(allUnsorted.type[e]){
                        
                        case 'wall':
                            
                            if(allUnsorted.type[closestI] === 'floor' && allUnsorted.f[closestI]<fh){
                                
                                closestVal = allUnsorted.d[e];
                                closestI = e;
                                
                            }
                            
                            if(allUnsorted.type[closestI] === 'ceiling' && allUnsorted.c[closestI]>ch){
                                
                                closestVal = allUnsorted.d[e];
                                closestI = e;
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                        case 'floor':
                            
                            if(allUnsorted.type[closestI] === 'wall' && allUnsorted.f[e]>fh){
                                
                                closestVal = allUnsorted.d[e];
                                closestI = e;
                                
                            }
                            
                            if(allUnsorted.type[closestI] === 'ceiling' && allUnsorted.f[e]<fh){
                                
                                closestVal = allUnsorted.d[e];
                                closestI = e;
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                        case 'ceiling':
                            
                            if(allUnsorted.type[closestI] === 'wall' && allUnsorted.c[e]<ch){
                                
                                closestVal = allUnsorted.d[e];
                                closestI = e;
                                
                            }
                            
                            if(allUnsorted.type[closestI] === 'floor' && allUnsorted.c[e]>ch){
                                
                                closestVal = allUnsorted.d[e];
                                closestI = e;
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            if(closestI > -1){
                
                allSorted.x.push(allUnsorted.x[closestI]);
                allSorted.y.push(allUnsorted.y[closestI]);
                allSorted.d.push(allUnsorted.d[closestI]);
                allSorted.t.push(allUnsorted.t[closestI]);
                allSorted.l.push(allUnsorted.l[closestI]);
                allSorted.lerp.push(allUnsorted.lerp[closestI]);
                allSorted.wallX.push(allUnsorted.wallX[closestI]);
                allSorted.type.push(allUnsorted.type[closestI]);
                allSorted.f.push(allUnsorted.f[closestI]);
                allSorted.c.push(allUnsorted.c[closestI]);
                allSorted.s.push(allUnsorted.s[closestI]);
                allSorted.fp.push(fh);
                allSorted.cp.push(ch);
                
                sorted[closestI] = true;
                
                var vD = h / allUnsorted.d[closestI];
                
                switch(allUnsorted.type[closestI]){
                    
                    case 'wall':
                        
                        var tty = (-((fh*(game.height/game.realHeight))-game.height/2)/allUnsorted.d[closestI])+game.height/2+horizon+vD;
                        var tby = (-((ch*(game.height/game.realHeight))-game.height/2)/allUnsorted.d[closestI])+game.height/2+horizon+vD;
                        
                        var ty = max(tty,tby);
                        var by = min(tty,tby);
                        
                        allSorted.ty.push(ty);
                        allSorted.by.push(by);
                        
                        allSorted.maxY.push(min(lMaxY,ty));
                        allSorted.minY.push(max(lMinY,by));
                        
                        allSorted.c[allSorted.c.length-1] = ch;
                        allSorted.f[allSorted.f.length-1] = fh;
                        
                        lMaxY = min(lMaxY,ty);
                        lMinY = max(lMinY,by);
                        
                        if(!trans){
                            
                            break;
                            
                        }
                        
                        break;
                    
                    case 'floor':
                        
                        var tty = (-((fh*(game.height/game.realHeight))-game.height/2)/allUnsorted.d[closestI])+game.height/2+horizon+vD;
                        var tby = (-((allUnsorted.f[closestI]*(game.height/game.realHeight))-game.height/2)/allUnsorted.d[closestI])+game.height/2+horizon+vD;
                        
                        var ty = max(tty,tby);
                        var by = min(tty,tby);
                        
                        var cy = (-((ch*(game.height/game.realHeight))-game.height/2)/allUnsorted.d[closestI])+game.height/2+horizon+vD;
                        
                        allSorted.ty.push(ty);
                        allSorted.by.push(by);
                        
                        allSorted.maxY.push(min(lMaxY,by));
                        allSorted.minY.push(max(lMinY,cy));
                        
                        allSorted.c[allSorted.c.length-1] = ch;
                        
                        lMaxY = min(lMaxY,by);
                        lMinY = max(lMinY,cy);
                        
                        fh = allUnsorted.f[closestI];
                        
                        break;
                    
                    case 'ceiling':
                        
                        var tty = (-((allUnsorted.c[closestI]*(game.height/game.realHeight))-game.height/2)/allUnsorted.d[closestI])+game.height/2+horizon+vD;
                        var tby = (-((ch*(game.height/game.realHeight))-game.height/2)/allUnsorted.d[closestI])+game.height/2+horizon+vD;
                        
                        var ty = max(tty,tby);
                        var by = min(tty,tby);
                        
                        var fy = (-((fh*(game.height/game.realHeight))-game.height/2)/allUnsorted.d[closestI])+game.height/2+horizon+vD;
                        
                        allSorted.ty.push(ty);
                        allSorted.by.push(by);
                        
                        allSorted.maxY.push(min(lMaxY,fy));
                        //allSorted.maxY.push(lMaxY);
                        allSorted.minY.push(max(lMinY,ty));
                        
                        allSorted.f[allSorted.c.length-1] = fh;
                        
                        lMinY = max(lMinY,ty);
                        lMaxY = min(lMaxY,fy);
                        
                        ch = allUnsorted.c[closestI];
                        
                        break;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Reset old object
        allUnsorted = {};
        
        var cmx = ray.mapX;
        var cmy = ray.mapY;
        
        //Recurse if needed
        //jump to next map square
        if(ray.sideDistX < ray.sideDistY){
            
            ray.sideDistX += ray.deltaDistX;
            
            ray.mapX += ray.stepX;
            
            ray.side = 0;
            
        }
        
        else{
            
            ray.sideDistY += ray.deltaDistY;
            
            ray.mapY += ray.stepY;
            
            ray.side = 1;
            
            ray.lastDist+=ray.deltaDistY;
            
        }
        
        //Recurse
        if(ray.mapX > -1 && ray.mapY > -1 && ray.mapX < loadedMap.layout[constrain(ray.mapY,0,loadedMap.layout.length-1)].length && ray.mapY < loadedMap.layout.length && ((hs && !wallHit) || (!hs && (trans || (!wallHit && lMinY<lMaxY))))){
            
            var ld = allSorted.d[allSorted.d.length-1];
            
            if(allSorted.d.length === 0){
                
                ld = priorDist;
                
            }
            
            hitScanReturn = drawRayCast(x,ox,oy,dx,dy,ray.mapX,ray.mapY,lMinY,lMaxY,fh,ch,ld,depth,hs,dz,ih);
            
        }
        
        //Current floor and ceiling height through the loop
        var fh = floorHeight;
        var ch = ceilingHeight;
        
        var fy = -Infinity;
        var cy = Infinity;
        
        //Draw the walls
        for(var i = allSorted.d.length-1; i >= 0; i--){
            
            var m = constrain(i-1,0,allSorted.d.length-1);
            
            var mi;
            var ma;
            var pd;
            
            if(i === 0){
                
                var mi = minY;
                var ma = maxY;
                pd = priorDist;
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                var mi = allSorted.minY[m];
                var ma = allSorted.maxY[m];
                pd = allSorted.d[m];
                
            }
            
            var vD = h / allSorted.d[i];
            
            switch(allSorted.type[i]){
                
                //Wall
                case 'wall':
                    
                    var tty = allSorted.ty[i];
                    var tby = allSorted.by[i];
                    
                    var ty = max(tty,tby);
                    var by = min(tty,tby);
                    
                    if(!hs){
                        
                        //ty,by,minY,maxY,d,t,l,lerp,wallX
                        drawWall(x,ty,by,mi,ma,allSorted.d[i],allSorted.t[i],allSorted.l[i],allSorted.lerp[i],allSorted.wallX[i]);
                        
                        //start,end,currentHeight,distance,difX,difY
                        if(minY<maxY && allSorted.d[i] !== pd){
                            
                            drawFloor(constrain(ty,minY,maxY),constrain(ma,minY,maxY),allSorted.fp[i],allSorted.d[i],(allSorted.x[i]*20-ox),(allSorted.y[i]*20-oy));
                            
                            drawCeiling(constrain(by,minY,maxY),constrain(mi,minY,maxY),allSorted.cp[i],allSorted.d[i],(allSorted.x[i]*20-ox),(allSorted.y[i]*20-oy));
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    else{
                        
                        var heightHit = checkHitScanHeight(allSorted.f[i],allSorted.c[i],allSorted.x[i]*20,allSorted.y[i]*20);
                        
                        //We hit a floor or ceiling
                        if(heightHit && ((hitScanReturn.fc === 'floor' && dz<0)||(hitScanReturn.fc === 'ceiling' && dz>0))){
                            
                            var dVal = allSorted.d[i];
                            
                            if(allSorted.d.indexOf(dVal) === allSorted.d.lastIndexOf(dVal)){
                                
                                return hitScanReturn;
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                        //We hit the wall
                        else{
                            
                            //Distance the ray traveled
                            hitScanReturn.t=dist(ox,oy,allSorted.x[i]*20,allSorted.y[i]*20);
                            
                            //Where it stopped on the gorund or ceiling or wall
                            hitScanReturn.x=allSorted.x[i]*20;
                            hitScanReturn.y=allSorted.y[i]*20;
                            hitScanReturn.z=ih + hitScanReturn.t * dz*20 - 300;
                            
                            //Set the normals
                            hitScanReturn.nx=0;
                            hitScanReturn.ny=0;
                            hitScanReturn.nz=0;
                            
                            if(ray.side === 0){
                                
                                hitScanReturn.nx = -dx/abs(dx);
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                hitScanReturn.ny = -dy/abs(dy);
                                
                            }
                            
                            //Whether it hit a ceiling or wall
                            hitScanReturn.fc=hfc;
                            
                            hitScanReturn.mapX = cmx;
                            hitScanReturn.mapY = cmy;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                
                case 'floor':
                    
                    var tty = allSorted.ty[i];
                    var tby = allSorted.by[i];
                    
                    var ty = max(tty,tby);
                    var by = min(tty,tby);
                    
                    if(!hs){
                        
                        if(allSorted.fp[i]<allSorted.f[i]){
                            
                            //ty,by,minY,maxY,d,t,l,lerp,wallX
                            drawWall(x,ty,by,mi,ma,allSorted.d[i],allSorted.t[i],allSorted.l[i],allSorted.lerp[i],allSorted.wallX[i]);
                            
                        }
                        
                        var startY = by;
                        
                        if(allSorted.f[i]>allSorted.fp[i]){
                            
                            startY = ty;
                            
                        }
                        
                        if(minY<maxY){
                            
                            drawFloor(constrain(startY,minY,maxY),constrain(ma,minY,maxY),allSorted.fp[i],allSorted.d[i],(allSorted.x[i]*20-ox),(allSorted.y[i]*20-oy));
                            
                            drawCeiling(constrain(allSorted.minY[i],minY,maxY),constrain(mi,minY,maxY),ch,allSorted.d[i],(allSorted.x[i]*20-ox),(allSorted.y[i]*20-oy));
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    else{
                        
                        var heightHit = checkHitScanHeight(allSorted.fp[i],ch,allSorted.x[i]*20,allSorted.y[i]*20);
                        
                        //We hit a floor or ceiling
                        if(heightHit && dz<0){
                            
                            return hitScanReturn;
                            
                        }
                        
                        //We hit the wall
                        else{
                            
                            var t = dist(ox,oy,allSorted.x[i]*20,allSorted.y[i]*20);
                            
                            var fz = ih + dz*t*20;
                            
                            if(fz<allSorted.f[i]){
                                
                                //Distance the ray traveled
                                hitScanReturn.t=dist(ox,oy,allSorted.x[i]*20,allSorted.y[i]*20);
                                
                                //Where it stopped on the gorund or ceiling or wall
                                hitScanReturn.x=allSorted.x[i]*20;
                                hitScanReturn.y=allSorted.y[i]*20;
                                hitScanReturn.z=ih + hitScanReturn.t * dz*20 - 300;
                                
                                //Set the normals
                                hitScanReturn.nx=0;
                                hitScanReturn.ny=0;
                                hitScanReturn.nz=0;
                                
                                if(ray.side === 0){
                                    
                                    hitScanReturn.nx = -dx/abs(dx);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    hitScanReturn.ny = -dy/abs(dy);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                //Whether it hit a ceiling or wall
                                hitScanReturn.fc=hfc;
                                
                                hitScanReturn.mapX = cmx;
                                hitScanReturn.mapY = cmy;
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                
                case 'ceiling':
                    
                    var tty = allSorted.ty[i];
                    var tby = allSorted.by[i];
                    
                    var ty = max(tty,tby);
                    var by = min(tty,tby);
                    
                    if(!hs){
                        
                        if(allSorted.cp[i]>allSorted.c[i]){
                            
                            //ty,by,minY,maxY,d,t,l,lerp,wallX
                            drawWall(x,ty,by,mi,ma,allSorted.d[i],allSorted.t[i],allSorted.l[i],allSorted.lerp[i],allSorted.wallX[i]);
                            
                        }
                        
                        var startY = ty;
                        
                        if(allSorted.c[i]<allSorted.cp[i]){
                            
                            startY = by;
                            
                        }
                        
                        if(minY<maxY){
                            
                            drawFloor(constrain(allSorted.maxY[i],minY,maxY),constrain(ma,minY,maxY),fh,allSorted.d[i],(allSorted.x[i]*20-ox),(allSorted.y[i]*20-oy));
                            
                            drawCeiling(constrain(startY,minY,maxY),constrain(mi,minY,maxY),allSorted.cp[i],allSorted.d[i],(allSorted.x[i]*20-ox),(allSorted.y[i]*20-oy));
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    else{
                        
                        var heightHit = checkHitScanHeight(fh,allSorted.cp[i],allSorted.x[i]*20,allSorted.y[i]*20);
                        
                        //We hit a floor or ceiling
                        if(heightHit&&dz>0){
                            
                            return hitScanReturn;
                            
                        }
                        
                        //We hit the wall
                        else{
                            
                            var t = dist(ox,oy,allSorted.x[i]*20,allSorted.y[i]*20);
                            
                            var fz = ih + dz*t*20;
                            
                            if(fz>allSorted.c[i]){
                                
                                //Distance the ray traveled
                                hitScanReturn.t=dist(ox,oy,allSorted.x[i]*20,allSorted.y[i]*20);
                                
                                //Where it stopped on the gorund or ceiling or wall
                                hitScanReturn.x=allSorted.x[i]*20;
                                hitScanReturn.y=allSorted.y[i]*20;
                                hitScanReturn.z=ih + hitScanReturn.t * dz*20 - 300;
                                
                                //Set the normals
                                hitScanReturn.nx=0;
                                hitScanReturn.ny=0;
                                hitScanReturn.nz=0;
                                
                                if(ray.side === 0){
                                    
                                    hitScanReturn.nx = -dx/abs(dx);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    hitScanReturn.ny = -dy/abs(dy);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                //Whether it hit a ceiling or wall
                                hitScanReturn.fc=hfc;
                                
                                hitScanReturn.mapX = cmx;
                                hitScanReturn.mapY = cmy;
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                    
                
            }
            
            drawObjects(allSorted.d[i]*20,pd*20,mi,ma);
            
            ch = allSorted.cp[i];
            fh = allSorted.fp[i];
            
        }
        
    }
    
    /**Return the variables we need for hitscan**/
    
    if(hs){
        
        return hitScanReturn;
        
    }
    
};

//Check Collision for hit-scan weapons and functions
var hitScan=function(angle,positionX,positionY,dz,detecting,positionZ,range){
    
    var dx = sin(angle);
    var dy = -cos(angle);
    
    var targetTemp = {
        
        x:[],
        y:[],
        z:[],
        
        type:[],
        
        height:[],
        width:[],
        
        dist:[],
        
        drawn:[],
        
        real:[],
        
    };
    
    if(detecting === 'enemies'){
        
        for(var i = 0; i <= enemies.x.length-1; i++){
            
            targetTemp.drawn.push(false);
            
        }
        
        for(var i = 0; i <= enemies.x.length-1; i++){
            
            if(!enemies.dud[i] && enemies.inView[i]){
                
                var lastSmallest = Infinity;
                var lastI = 0;
                
                for(var s = 0; s <= enemies.x.length-1; s++){
                    
                    if(enemies.distance[s]<lastSmallest && !targetTemp.drawn[s] && !enemies.dud[s] && enemies.inView[s]){
                        
                        lastSmallest = enemies.distance[s];
                        lastI = s;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                targetTemp.drawn[lastI] = true;
                
                targetTemp.x.push(enemies.x[lastI]);
                targetTemp.y.push(enemies.y[lastI]);
                targetTemp.z.push(enemies.verticalPos[lastI]);
                targetTemp.type.push(enemies.type[lastI]);
                targetTemp.height.push(enemyConstants[enemies.type[lastI]].height);
                targetTemp.width.push(enemyConstants[enemies.type[lastI]].width);
                targetTemp.dist.push(enemies.distance[lastI]);
                targetTemp.real.push(lastI);
                
            }
            
        }
        
        objectList.drawn = [];
        objectList.closestPoint = [];
        objectList.index = [];
        
        
        objectList.setup = true;
        
    }
    
    if(detecting === 'player'){
        
        targetTemp.x.push(player.x);
        targetTemp.y.push(player.y);
        targetTemp.z.push((player.height+player.floorHeight+300));
        targetTemp.type.push(0);
        targetTemp.height.push(-player.floorHeight);
        targetTemp.width.push(player.width);
        targetTemp.dist.push(dist(positionX,positionY,player.x,player.y));
        targetTemp.real.push(0);
        
    }
    
    //var hit = drawRayCast(0,positionX,positionY,sin(angle),-cos(angle),0,0,positionX,positionY,false,1,false,0,primaryOrSecondary(positionX,positionY,'floor'),primaryOrSecondary(positionX,positionY,'ceiling'),-4,game.height+4,floor(positionX/20),floor(positionY/20),true,dz,positionZ+game.realHeight/2);
    
    //x,ox,oy,dx,dy,mapX,mapY,minY,maxY,floorHeight,ceilingHeight,priorDist,depth,hs,dz,ih
    
    var hit = drawRayCast(0,positionX,positionY,sin(angle),-cos(angle),floor(positionX/20),floor(positionY/20),-4,game.height+4,primaryOrSecondary(positionX,positionY,'floor'),primaryOrSecondary(positionX,positionY,'ceiling'),0,0,true,dz,positionZ+game.realHeight/2);
    
    //What we will return
    var returnValues = {
        
        t:dist(positionX,positionY,hit.x,hit.y),
        
        x:hit.x,
        y:hit.y,
        z:hit.z,
        
        nx:hit.nx,
        ny:hit.ny,
        nz:hit.nz,
        
        fc:hit.fc,
        
        hits:[],
        
        mapX:hit.mapX,
        mapY:hit.mapY
        
    };
    
    //Now check what we hit
    for(var i = 0; i <= targetTemp.x.length-1; i++){
        
        //Ray Next Position
        var rayNextX=positionX+dx;
        var rayNextY=positionY+dy;
        
        //Check if the line intersects
        var closestPointToLine = (rayNextX-positionX) * targetTemp.y[i] + (positionY-rayNextY)*targetTemp.x[i] + (positionX-rayNextX) * positionY + (rayNextY-positionY) * positionX;
        
        var t = dist(positionX,positionY,hit.x,hit.y);
        
        var endShot = (positionZ + dz*targetTemp.dist[i]*20);
        var targetZ = targetTemp.z[i]-300;
        
        if(abs(closestPointToLine)<targetTemp.width[i]/2 && targetTemp.dist[i] < t && endShot >= targetZ && endShot <= targetZ + targetTemp.height[i] && targetTemp.dist[i] <= range){
            
            returnValues.hits.push(targetTemp.real[i]);
            
            //Set the impact point to the first enemy
            if(returnValues.hits.length-1 === 0){
                
                returnValues.x = positionX + dx*targetTemp.dist[i];
                returnValues.y = positionY + dy*targetTemp.dist[i];
                returnValues.z = (positionZ + dz*targetTemp.dist[i]*20);
                returnValues.fc = 'enemy';
                
                returnValues.nx = 0;
                returnValues.ny = 0;
                returnValues.nz = 0;
                
                returnValues.t = targetTemp.dist[i];
                
            }
            
        }
        
    }
    
    return returnValues;
    
};

//Pathfinding

var n = 0;

var removeTarget = false;

var feeler = {
    
    //Squares that can be reached from reached tiles
    open:{
        
        //Square position
        x:[],
        y:[],
        
        //Current F position
        f:[],
        
        //Direction it came from to get here
        d:[],
        
        //How many steps it's taken to get here
        g:[],
        
        //Whether this has been removed from the list
        dud:[],
        
    },
    
    //Squares that have been reached
    closed:{
        
        //Square position
        x:[],
        y:[],
        
        //Current F position
        f:[],
        
        //Direction it came from to get here
        d:[],
        
        //How many steps it's taken to get here
        g:[],
        
        //Whether this has been removed from the list
        dud:[],
        
    },
    
    //Current square the feeler is on
    s:0,
    
    //Final shortest route from target to start
    pathX:[],
    pathY:[],
    
    //How many loops the two while loops are allowed to do
    maxLoops:500,
    
};

var addToList = function(x,y,f,d,g,type){
    
    if(type === 0){
        
        var dud = false;
        var t = 0;
        
        for(var i = 0; i <= feeler.open.dud.length-1; i++){
            
            if(feeler.open.dud[i] && !dud){
                
                dud = true;
                t = i;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        if(dud){
            
            feeler.open.x[t]=x;
            feeler.open.y[t]=y;
            feeler.open.f[t]=f;
            feeler.open.d[t]=d;
            feeler.open.g[t]=g;
            feeler.open.dud[t]=false;
            
        }
        
        else{
            
            feeler.open.x.push(x);
            feeler.open.y.push(y);
            feeler.open.d.push(d);
            feeler.open.f.push(f);
            feeler.open.g.push(g);
            feeler.open.dud.push(false);
            
        }
        
    }
    
    else{
        
        var dud = false;
        var t = 0;
        
        for(var i = 0; i <= feeler.closed.dud.length-1; i++){
            
            if(feeler.closed.dud[i] && !dud){
                
                dud = true;
                t = i;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        if(dud){
            
            feeler.closed.x[t]=x;
            feeler.closed.y[t]=y;
            feeler.closed.f[t]=f;
            feeler.closed.d[t]=d;
            feeler.closed.g[t]=g;
            feeler.closed.dud[t]=false;
            
        }
        
        else{
            
            feeler.closed.x.push(x);
            feeler.closed.y.push(y);
            feeler.closed.d.push(d);
            feeler.closed.f.push(f);
            feeler.closed.g.push(g);
            feeler.closed.dud.push(false);
            
        }
        
    }
    
};

var pathFinding = function(startX,startY,goalX,goalY){
    
    //Reset All Variables
    {
        
        feeler.open.x = [];
        feeler.open.y = [];
        feeler.open.f = [];
        feeler.open.d = [];
        feeler.open.g = [];
        feeler.open.dud = [];
        
        feeler.closed.x = [];
        feeler.closed.y = [];
        feeler.closed.f = [];
        feeler.closed.d = [];
        feeler.closed.g = [];
        feeler.closed.dud = [];
        
        feeler.s = 0;
        
        feeler.pathX = [];
        feeler.pathY = [];
        
    }
    
    //Set start
    feeler.open.x.push(startX);
    feeler.open.y.push(startY);
    feeler.open.d.push(0);
    feeler.open.f.push(0);
    feeler.open.g.push(0);
    feeler.open.dud.push(false);
    
    //Local number of calls
    var local = 0;
    
    //Current number of steps to get there
    feeler.g = 0;
    
    var room = loadedMap.boundaries;
    
    //While loop
    var found = false;
    
    var noSolution = false;
    
    removeTarget = false;
    
    while(!found){
        
        //Set s to lowest value
        {
            
            var lowestValue = "none";
            
            var allDuds = true;
            
            for(var i = 0; i <= feeler.open.f.length-1; i++){
                
                if((feeler.open.f[i] < lowestValue || lowestValue === "none") && !feeler.open.dud[i]){
                    
                    lowestValue = feeler.open.f[i];
                    
                    feeler.s = i;
                    
                    allDuds = false;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            if(allDuds){
                
                found = true;
                
                noSolution = true;
                
                removeTarget = true;
                
            }
            
            if(feeler.open.x[feeler.s] === goalX && feeler.open.y[feeler.s] === goalY){
                
                found = true;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Remove s from open list and add to closed
        {
            
            feeler.open.dud[feeler.s]=true;
            
            addToList(feeler.open.x[feeler.s],feeler.open.y[feeler.s],feeler.open.f[feeler.s],feeler.open.d[feeler.s],feeler.open.g[feeler.s],1);
            
        }
        
        //Check squares adjacent to s
        {
            
            var tempX = feeler.open.x[feeler.s];
            var tempY = feeler.open.y[feeler.s];
            var tempG = feeler.open.g[feeler.s];
            
            //Up
            if(room[tempY-1][tempX] === 0 && abs(tempMap.floorHeight[tempY-1][tempX]-tempMap.floorHeight[tempY][tempX]) <= 100){
                
                //Set h variable
                var h = abs(goalX-tempX) + abs(goalY-(tempY-1));
                
                //Find if the tile is in the closed list
                var closed = false;
                
                for(var i = 0; i <= feeler.closed.x.length-1; i++){
                    
                    if(feeler.closed.x[i] === tempX && feeler.closed.y[i] === tempY-1){
                        
                        closed = true;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //Do stuff with it if it's not
                if(!closed){
                    
                    //Find if it's in the open list
                    var open = false;
                    
                    for(var i = 0; i <= feeler.open.x.length-1; i++){
                        
                        if(feeler.open.x[i] === tempX && feeler.open.y[i] === tempY-1){
                            
                            open = true;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(open){
                        
                        if(tempG + 1 + h < feeler.open.f[i]){
                            
                            feeler.open.d[i] = 1;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    else{
                        
                        addToList(tempX,tempY-1,tempG + 1 + h,1,tempG + 1,0);
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            //Down
            if(room[tempY+1][tempX] === 0 && abs(tempMap.floorHeight[tempY+1][tempX]-tempMap.floorHeight[tempY][tempX]) <= 100){
                
                //Set h variable
                var h = abs(goalX-tempX) + abs(goalY-(tempY+1));
                
                //Find if the tile is in the closed list
                var closed = false;
                
                for(var i = 0; i <= feeler.closed.x.length-1; i++){
                    
                    if(feeler.closed.x[i] === tempX && feeler.closed.y[i] === tempY+1){
                        
                        closed = true;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //Do stuff with it if it's not
                if(!closed){
                    
                    //Find if it's in the open list
                    var open = false;
                    
                    for(var i = 0; i <= feeler.open.x.length-1; i++){
                        
                        if(feeler.open.x[i] === tempX && feeler.open.y[i] === tempY+1){
                            
                            open = true;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(open){
                        
                        if(tempG + 1 + h < feeler.open.f[i]){
                            
                            feeler.open.d[i] = 3;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    else{
                        
                        addToList(tempX,tempY+1,tempG + 1 + h,3,tempG + 1,0);
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            //Left
            if(room[tempY][tempX-1] === 0 && abs(tempMap.floorHeight[tempY][tempX-1]-tempMap.floorHeight[tempY][tempX]) <= 100){
                
                //Set h variable
                var h = abs(goalX-(tempX-1)) + abs(goalY-tempY);
                
                //Find if the tile is in the closed list
                var closed = false;
                
                for(var i = 0; i <= feeler.closed.x.length-1; i++){
                    
                    if(feeler.closed.x[i] === tempX-1 && feeler.closed.y[i] === tempY){
                        
                        closed = true;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //Do stuff with it if it's not
                if(!closed){
                    
                    //Find if it's in the open list
                    var open = false;
                    
                    for(var i = 0; i <= feeler.open.x.length-1; i++){
                        
                        if(feeler.open.x[i] === tempX-1 && feeler.open.y[i] === tempY){
                            
                            open = true;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(open){
                        
                        if(tempG + 1 + h < feeler.open.f[i]){
                            
                            feeler.open.d[i] = 4;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    else{
                        
                        addToList(tempX-1,tempY,tempG + 1 + h,4,tempG + 1,0);
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            //Right
            if(room[tempY][tempX+1] === 0 && abs(tempMap.floorHeight[tempY][tempX+1]-tempMap.floorHeight[tempY][tempX]) <= 100){
                
                //Set h variable
                var h = abs(goalX-tempX+1) + abs(goalY-(tempY));
                
                //Find if the tile is in the closed list
                var closed = false;
                
                for(var i = 0; i <= feeler.closed.x.length-1; i++){
                    
                    if(feeler.closed.x[i] === tempX+1 && feeler.closed.y[i] === tempY){
                        
                        closed = true;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //Do stuff with it if it's not
                if(!closed){
                    
                    //Find if it's in the open list
                    var open = false;
                    
                    for(var i = 0; i <= feeler.open.x.length-1; i++){
                        
                        if(feeler.open.x[i] === tempX+1 && feeler.open.y[i] === tempY){
                            
                            open = true;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(open){
                        
                        if(tempG + 1 + h < feeler.open.f[i]){
                            
                            feeler.open.d[i] = 2;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    else{
                        
                        addToList(tempX+1,tempY,tempG + 1 + h,2,tempG + 1,0);
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Check if the goal is found
        for(var i = 0; i <= feeler.open.f.length-1; i++){
            
            if(feeler.open.x[i] === goalX && feeler.open.y[i] === goalY){
                
                found = true;
                
                feeler.s = i;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        if(n>feeler.maxLoops){
            
            found = true;
            
            noSolution = true;
            
            if(local === n){
                
                removeTarget = true;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        n++;
        
        local++;
        
        /*
        println("n: " + n);
        println("x: " + feeler.open.x);
        println("y: " + feeler.open.y);
        println("f: " + feeler.open.f);
        println("s: " + feeler.s);
        
        println("---");
        */
        
    }
    
    //Path Map
    var pathMap = [];
    
    for(var y = 0; y <= room.length-1; y++){
        
        pathMap.push([]);
        
        for(var x = 0; x <= room[y].length-1; x++){
            
            pathMap[y].push(0);
            
        }
        
    }
    
    for(var i =0; i <= feeler.open.d.length-1; i++){
        
        pathMap[feeler.open.y[i]][feeler.open.x[i]] = feeler.open.d[i];
        
    }
    
    for(var i =0; i <= feeler.closed.d.length-1; i++){
        
        pathMap[feeler.closed.y[i]][feeler.closed.x[i]] = feeler.closed.d[i];
        
    }
    
    //Path
    feeler.pathX = [];
    feeler.pathY = [];
    
    var returned = false;
    
    var x = feeler.open.x[feeler.s];
    var y = feeler.open.y[feeler.s];
    
    var n = 0;
    
    while(!returned){
        
        if(!returned){
            
            feeler.pathX.push(x);
            feeler.pathY.push(y);
            
        }
        
        switch(pathMap[y][x]){
            
            case 0:
                
                returned = true;
                
                break;
                
            case 1:
                
                y+=1;
                
                break;
                
            case 2:
                
                x-=1;
                
                break;
                
            case 3:
                
                y-=1;
                
                break;
                
            case 4:
                
                x+=1;
                
                break;           
            
        }
        
        //Just in case
        if(n>feeler.maxLoops){
            
            returned = true;
            
        }
        
        n++;
        
    }
    
    //Too lazy to change how this works from my old implementation so I'm just plugging the version I prefer into the return 
    var path = [];
    
    if(n<=feeler.maxLoops){
        
        for(var i = 0; i <= feeler.pathX.length-1; i++){
            
            path.push([feeler.pathX[i],feeler.pathY[i]]);
            
        }
        
    }
    
    if(noSolution){
        
        path = [];
        
    }
    
    return path;
    
};

//Draw and give functionality to buttons
var button = function(x,y,width,height,id,selected){
    
    ////Draw the button
    
    //Set button to be more visible when selected and when the mouse is hovering over it
    var highlightOffset = 0;
    
    var mouseInRange = mouseX > x && mouseX < x+width && mouseY > y && mouseY < y+height;
    
    if(mouseInRange){
        
        highlightOffset = 100;
        
    }
    
    var selectOffset = 0;
    
    if(selected){
        
        selectOffset = 50;
        
    }
    
    //Rectangle of the button
    fill(186-selectOffset, 180-selectOffset, 180-selectOffset,175 + highlightOffset + selectOffset);
    
    stroke(5,5,5);
    
    rect(x,y,width,height);
    
    //Specific symbols or text in button
    switch(id){
        
    }
    
    strokeWeight(1);
    
    textSize(12);
    
    ////Function of buttons
    if(mouseIsPressed && mouseInRange && !controls.toggles.button){
        
        switch(id){
            
            
        }
        
        controls.toggles.button = true;
        
    }
    
    fill(30, 30, 30);
    
    //return whether a button is being hovered over
    return mouseInRange;
    
};

//How the world is drawn
var drawWorld=function(){
    
    if(game.cam===2){
        
        strokeCap(SQUARE);
        
        //Test skybox elements
        
        fill(90, 219, 40);
        //ellipse(300-(player.angle/360)*1200,20+(game.camera.horizon * (game.height/game.realHeight)),10,10);
        //println((player.angle/360));
        fill(90, 219, 40);
        //ellipse(0+360-player.angle,20,10,10);
        
        fill(250, 140, 175);
        //ellipse(90-player.angle,20,10,10);
        fill(250, 140, 175);
        //ellipse(90+360-player.angle,20,10,10);
        
        fill(146, 140, 250);
        //ellipse(180-player.angle,20,10,10);
        fill(146, 140, 250);
        //ellipse(180+360-player.angle,20,10,10);
        
        fill(153, 0, 0);
        //ellipse(270-player.angle,20,10,10);
        fill(153, 0, 0);
        //ellipse(270+360-player.angle,20,10,10);
        
        
        //FloorBox
        fill(loadedMap.floorBox[0], loadedMap.floorBox[1], loadedMap.floorBox[2]);
        
        noStroke();
        
        var horizon = game.camera.horizon*(game.height/game.realHeight);
        
        rect(0,game.height/2+horizon,game.width,game.height/2 - horizon);
        
        for(var x = 0; x < game.width+1; x++){
            
            var cameraX=2*x/game.width-1;
            
            var dX=game.camera.dirX+game.camera.planeX*cameraX;
            var dY=game.camera.dirY+game.camera.planeY*cameraX;
            
            //First iteration of raycast for each vertical stripe
            objectList.setup = true;
            
            //x,ox,oy,dx,dy,mapX,mapY,minY,maxY,floorHeight,ceilingHeight,depth,hs,dz
            
            drawRayCast(x,game.camera.x,game.camera.y,dX,dY,floor(game.camera.x/20),floor(game.camera.y/20),-10,game.height+10,primaryOrSecondary(game.camera.x,game.camera.y,'floor'),primaryOrSecondary(game.camera.x,game.camera.y,'ceiling'),0,false,0);
            
            if(x === game.width/2){
                
                //println('');
                
            }
            
            
        }
        
        rectMode(CORNER);
        
        stroke(255, 0, 0,50);
        //line(game.width/2,0,game.width/2,game.height);
        //x,game.camera.x,game.camera.y,player.dirX,player.dirY,player.planeX,player.planeY,game.camera.x,game.camera.y,false,1,false,0,primaryOrSecondary(game.camera.x,game.camera.y,'floor'),primaryOrSecondary(game.camera.x,game.camera.y,'ceiling'),-4,game.height+4,floor(game.camera.x/20),floor(game.camera.y/20),false
    }
    
};

//How the HUD is drawn
var drawMenu=function(){
    
    rectMode(CORNER);
    
    //Draw Weapon
    if(game.cam>=2){
        
        //Draw the hitscan lines
        for(var i = 0; i <= hitScanLines.x1.length-1; i++){
            
            stroke(hitScanLines.color[i][0],hitScanLines.color[i][1],hitScanLines.color[i][2],hitScanLines.color[i][3]);
            
            strokeWeight(hitScanLines.weight[i]);
            
            line(hitScanLines.x1[i],hitScanLines.y1[i],hitScanLines.x2[i],hitScanLines.y2[i]);
            
        }
        
        //Only try to draw if the player is armed
        if(player.armed){
            
            //Don't change while switching
            if(weaponSway.switchFactor===1){
                
                drawnWeapon=drawnWeapon;
                
            }
            
            //Update if not
            else{
                
                drawnWeapon=player.projectile;
                
            }
            
            //Remember old matrix
            pushMatrix();
            
            //Translate weapon position based on variables
            //translate(-300+weaponSway.posX + weaponSway.turnX + weaponSway.strafeX + weaponSway.walkX + weaponSway.switchX + weaponSway.reloadX + -weaponSway.recoilY/1.5,-300+weaponSway.posY + weaponSway.turnY + weaponSway.strafeY + weaponSway.walkY + weaponSway.switchY + weaponSway.reloadY + weaponSway.recoilY);
            translate(weaponSway.posX + weaponSway.turnX + weaponSway.strafeX + weaponSway.walkX + weaponSway.switchX + weaponSway.reloadX + -weaponSway.recoilY/1.5,weaponSway.posY + weaponSway.turnY + weaponSway.strafeY + weaponSway.walkY + weaponSway.switchY + weaponSway.reloadY + weaponSway.recoilY);
            var lightLevel=tempMap.lighting[floor(player.y/20)][floor(player.x/20)];
            
            switch(drawnWeapon){
                
                //Sword
                case 0:
                    
                    if(player.cooldown<=2){
                        
                        stroke(148*lightLevel, 148*lightLevel, 148*lightLevel);
                        
                        fill(145*lightLevel, 145*lightLevel, 145*lightLevel);
                        
                        rect(-70,-40,35,75);
                        
                        fill(117*lightLevel, 115*lightLevel, 115*lightLevel);
                        
                        rect(-110,-60,120,25);
                        
                        fill(199*lightLevel, 199*lightLevel, 199*lightLevel);
                        
                        rect(-80,-60,55,-280);
                        
                        triangle(-80,-340,-25,-340,-52.5,-390);
                        
                        line(-52.5,-60,-52.5,-290);
                        
                        fill(117*lightLevel, 115*lightLevel, 115*lightLevel);
                        
                        ellipse(-52,35,50,50);
                        
                    }
                    
                    else{
                        
                        pushMatrix();
                        
                        rotate(-45 - player.cooldown*2);
                        
                        stroke(148*lightLevel, 148*lightLevel, 148*lightLevel);
                        
                        fill(145*lightLevel, 145*lightLevel, 145*lightLevel);
                        
                        rect(-70,-40,35,75);
                        
                        fill(117*lightLevel, 115*lightLevel, 115*lightLevel);
                        
                        rect(-110,-60,120,25);
                        
                        fill(199*lightLevel, 199*lightLevel, 199*lightLevel);
                        
                        rect(-80,-60,55,-280);
                        
                        triangle(-80,-340,-25,-340,-52.5,-390);
                        
                        line(-52.5,-60,-52.5,-290);
                        
                        fill(117*lightLevel, 115*lightLevel, 115*lightLevel);
                        
                        ellipse(-52,35,50,50);
                        
                        popMatrix();
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                    
                
                //Flare Gun
                case 1:
                    
                    //Steady
                    if(player.cooldown<=2){
                    //if(false){
                        
                        //Gun
                        fill(225*lightLevel, 23*lightLevel, 23*lightLevel);
                        
                        stroke(112*lightLevel, 110*lightLevel, 110*lightLevel);
                        
                        strokeWeight(2);
                        
                        //Muzzle
                        ellipse(0,-30,45,45);
                        
                        //Tube
                        noStroke();
                        quad(25,30,55,25,55,55,25,60);
                        
                        //Top
                        quad(15,50,55,25,15,-45,-22,-35);
                        
                        //Side
                        //quad(25,30,25,60,-15,-15,-15,-40);
                        
                        stroke(112*lightLevel, 110*lightLevel, 110*lightLevel);
                        
                        line(-12,-15,15,50);
                        
                        line(55,25,20,-35);
                        
                        //Grip
                        quad(30,110,60,105,55,55,25,60);
                        
                        quad(30,110,10,95,10,35,25,60);
                        
                        //Back
                        arc(36,40,50,50,-60,185);
                        
                    }
                    
                    //Open Break
                    else if(player.cooldown>2 && player.cooldown<=25){
                        
                        pushMatrix();
                        
                        translate(0,40);
                        
                        //Gun
                        fill(225*lightLevel, 23*lightLevel, 23*lightLevel);
                        
                        stroke(112*lightLevel, 110*lightLevel, 110*lightLevel);
                        
                        strokeWeight(2);
                        
                        //Muzzle
                        ellipse(-40,60,45,40);
                        
                        //Tube
                        noStroke();
                        //quad(25,30,55,25,55,55,25,60);
                        
                        //Top
                        quad(-38,80,43,28,10,-2,-61,54);
                        
                        stroke(112*lightLevel, 110*lightLevel, 110*lightLevel);
                        
                        //Side
                        //quad(25,30,25,60,-15,-15,-15,-40);
                        fill(55*lightLevel, 23*lightLevel, 23*lightLevel);

        fill(237, 216, 23);

        rect(15,game.realHeight-35,5,15);

        triangle(90,game.realHeight-35,110,game.realHeight-35,100,game.realHeight-20);
        
        //The actual numbers
        
        textSize(25);
        
        text(floor(player.health),30,game.realHeight-20);
        
        text("I    " + floor(player.suit),80,game.realHeight-20);
        
        //Ammo Count
        if(projectileConstants[player.projectile].type!=="melee"){
            
            if(projectileConstants[player.projectile].reloadable){
                
                text(floor(player.clip[player.projectile]),game.realWidth-106,game.realHeight-20);
                
                text("I " + floor(player.ammo[projectileConstants[player.projectile].ammo]),game.realWidth-62,game.realHeight-20);
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                text(floor(player.ammo[projectileConstants[player.projectile].ammo]),game.realWidth-62,game.realHeight-20);
                
            }
            
        }
        
        
    }
    
};

//Process the player's controls
var interact = function(){
    
    //Set variables
    player.move=false;
    
    //Reset scan lines
    hitScanLines = {
        
        x1:[],
        x2:[],
        y1:[],
        y2:[],
        
        color:[],
        
        weight:[],
        
    };
    
    //Player
    {
    
    //Move the player using WASD
    {
        
        //Forward
        if(keys[87]){
            
            if(game.cam !== 0){
                
                var a  = [-sin(player.angle)*-player.acceleration*game.deltaF,cos(player.angle)*-player.acceleration*game.deltaF];
                
                if(!player.canJump || player.overSpeed){
                    
                    a[0] /= 2;
                    a[1] /= 2;
                    
                }
                
                //When the player is blast jumping, prevent them from moving more in the direction they are flying
                if(player.overSpeed && dist(player.speedX,player.speedY,0,0) > 0){
                    
                    //total speed
                    var vt = dist(player.speedX,player.speedY,0,0);
                    
                    //dot product of the velocity and accelration vectors
                    var dot = a[0] * player.speedX + a[1] * player.speedY;
                    
                    //scaling factor for projecting a onto v
                    var factor = dot/sq(vt);
                    
                    //project a onto v
                    var c = [factor*player.speedX, factor*player.speedY];
                    
                    //Subtract the projection from the acceleration
                    a[0] -= c[0];
                    a[1] -= c[1];
                    
                }
                
                player.speedX+=a[0];
                
                player.speedY+=a[1];
                
                player.move=true;
                
                //Sway to Up and Down
                if(weaponSway.walkY>=-weaponSway.maxWalk && weaponSway.walkY<=weaponSway.maxWalk){
                    
                    weaponSway.walkY+=player.maxSpeed*weaponSway.walkFactorY*game.deltaF;
                    
                }
                
                //Change direction of sway
                if(weaponSway.walkY<-weaponSway.maxWalk || weaponSway.walkY>weaponSway.maxWalk){
                    
                    weaponSway.walkFactorY*=-1;
                    
                    if(weaponSway.walkY<-weaponSway.maxWalk){
                        
                        weaponSway.walkY=-weaponSway.maxWalk;
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(weaponSway.walkY>weaponSway.maxWalk){
                        
                        weaponSway.walkY=weaponSway.maxWalk;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //Sway to Left and Right
                if(weaponSway.walkX>=-weaponSway.maxWalk*2 && weaponSway.walkX<=weaponSway.maxWalk*2){
                    
                    weaponSway.walkX+=player.maxSpeed*weaponSway.walkFactorX*game.deltaF;
                    
                }
                
                //Change direction of sway
                if(weaponSway.walkX<-weaponSway.maxWalk*2 || weaponSway.walkX>weaponSway.maxWalk*2){
                    
                    weaponSway.walkFactorX*=-1;
                    
                    if(weaponSway.walkX<-weaponSway.maxWalk*2){
                        
                        weaponSway.walkX=-weaponSway.maxWalk*2;
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(weaponSway.walkX>weaponSway.maxWalk*2){
                        
                        weaponSway.walkX=weaponSway.maxWalk*2;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                game.camera.y -= player.maxSpeed*3*game.deltaF;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Backward
        if(keys[83]){
            
            if(game.cam !== 0){
                
                var a  = [-sin(player.angle)*player.acceleration*game.deltaF,cos(player.angle)*player.acceleration*game.deltaF];
                
                if(!player.canJump || player.overSpeed){
                    
                    a[0] /= 2;
                    a[1] /= 2;
                    
                }
                
                //When the player is blast jumping, prevent them from moving more in the direction they are flying
                if(player.overSpeed && dist(player.speedX,player.speedY,0,0) > 0){
                    
                    //total speed
                    var vt = dist(player.speedX,player.speedY,0,0);
                    
                    //dot product of the velocity and accelration vectors
                    var dot = a[0] * player.speedX + a[1] * player.speedY;
                    
                    //scaling factor for projecting a onto v
                    var factor = dot/sq(vt);
                    
                    //project a onto v
                    var c = [factor*player.speedX, factor*player.speedY];
                    
                    //Subtract the projection from the acceleration
                    a[0] -= c[0];
                    a[1] -= c[1];
                    
                }
                
                player.speedX+=a[0];
                
                player.speedY+=a[1];
                
                player.move=true;
                
                //Sway to Up and Down
                if(weaponSway.walkY>=-weaponSway.maxWalk && weaponSway.walkY<=weaponSway.maxWalk){
                    
                    weaponSway.walkY-=player.maxSpeed*weaponSway.walkFactorY*game.deltaF;
                    
                }
                
                //Change direction of sway
                if(weaponSway.walkY<-weaponSway.maxWalk || weaponSway.walkY>weaponSway.maxWalk){
                    
                    weaponSway.walkFactorY*=-1;
                    
                    if(weaponSway.walkY<-weaponSway.maxWalk){
                        
                        weaponSway.walkY=-weaponSway.maxWalk;
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(weaponSway.walkY>weaponSway.maxWalk){
                        
                        weaponSway.walkY=weaponSway.maxWalk;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //Sway to Left and Right
                if(weaponSway.walkX>=-weaponSway.maxWalk*2 && weaponSway.walkX<=weaponSway.maxWalk*2){
                    
                    weaponSway.walkX-=player.maxSpeed*weaponSway.walkFactorX*game.deltaF;
                    
                }
                
                //Change direction of sway
                if(weaponSway.walkX<-weaponSway.maxWalk*2 || weaponSway.walkX>weaponSway.maxWalk*2){
                    
                    weaponSway.walkFactorX*=-1;
                    
                    if(weaponSway.walkX<-weaponSway.maxWalk*2){
                        
                        weaponSway.walkX=-weaponSway.maxWalk*2;
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(weaponSway.walkX>weaponSway.maxWalk*2){
                        
                        weaponSway.walkX=weaponSway.maxWalk*2;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                game.camera.y += player.maxSpeed*3*game.deltaF;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Reset Walking Sway
        if(!keys[87] && !keys[83]){
            
            if(weaponSway.walkY<0){
                
                weaponSway.walkY+=player.maxSpeed/2;
                
            }
            
            if(weaponSway.walkY>0){
                
                weaponSway.walkY-=player.maxSpeed/2;
                
            }
            
            if(weaponSway.walkX<0){
                
                weaponSway.walkX+=player.maxSpeed/2;
                
            }
            
            if(weaponSway.walkX>0){
                
                weaponSway.walkX-=player.maxSpeed/2;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Left
        if(keys[65]){
            
            if(game.cam !== 0 || player.overSpeed){
                
                var a  = [-sin(player.angle+90)*player.acceleration*game.deltaF,cos(player.angle+90)*player.acceleration*game.deltaF];
                
                if(!player.canJump){
                    
                    a[0] /= 2;
