                    a[1] /= 2;
                    
                }
                
                //When the player is blast jumping, prevent them from moving more in the direction they are flying
                if(player.overSpeed && dist(player.speedX,player.speedY,0,0) > 0){
                    
                    //total speed
                    var vt = dist(player.speedX,player.speedY,0,0);
                    
                    //dot product of the velocity and accelration vectors
                    var dot = a[0] * player.speedX + a[1] * player.speedY;
                    
                    //scaling factor for projecting a onto v
                    var factor = dot/sq(vt);
                    
                    //project a onto v
                    var c = [factor*player.speedX, factor*player.speedY];
                    
                    //Subtract the projection from the acceleration
                    a[0] -= c[0];
                    a[1] -= c[1];
                    
                }
                
                player.speedX+=a[0];
                
                player.speedY+=a[1];
                
                player.move=true;
                
                //Sway to left
                if(weaponSway.strafeX>-weaponSway.maxStrafe){
                    
                    weaponSway.strafeX-=player.maxSpeed*game.deltaF;
                    
                }
                
                //Sway Down
                if(weaponSway.strafeY<weaponSway.maxStrafe/2){
                    
                    weaponSway.strafeY+=(player.maxSpeed/2)*game.deltaF;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                game.camera.x -= player.maxSpeed*3*game.deltaF;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        else if(weaponSway.strafeX<0){
            
            weaponSway.strafeX+=player.maxSpeed;
            
        }
        
        //Right
        if(keys[68]){
            
            if(game.cam !== 0){
                
                var a  = [-sin(player.angle+90)*-player.acceleration*game.deltaF,cos(player.angle+90)*-player.acceleration*game.deltaF];
                
                if(!player.canJump || player.overSpeed){
                    
                    a[0] /= 2;
                    a[1] /= 2;
                    
                }
                
                //When the player is blast jumping, prevent them from moving more in the direction they are flying
                if(player.overSpeed && dist(player.speedX,player.speedY,0,0) > 0){
                    
                    //total speed
                    var vt = dist(player.speedX,player.speedY,0,0);
                    
                    //dot product of the velocity and accelration vectors
                    var dot = a[0] * player.speedX + a[1] * player.speedY;
                    
                    //scaling factor for projecting a onto v
                    var factor = dot/sq(vt);
                    
                    //project a onto v
                    var c = [factor*player.speedX, factor*player.speedY];
                    
                    //Subtract the projection from the acceleration
                    a[0] -= c[0];
                    a[1] -= c[1];
                    
                }
                
                player.speedX+=a[0];
                
                player.speedY+=a[1];
                
                player.move=true;
                
                //Sway to right
                if(weaponSway.strafeX<weaponSway.maxStrafe){
                    
                    weaponSway.strafeX+=player.maxSpeed*game.deltaF;
                    
                }
                
                //Sway Down
                if(weaponSway.strafeY<weaponSway.maxStrafe/2){
                    
                    weaponSway.strafeY+=player.maxSpeed/2*game.deltaF;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                game.camera.x += player.maxSpeed*3*game.deltaF;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        else if(weaponSway.strafeX>0){
            
            weaponSway.strafeX-=player.maxSpeed;
            
        }
        
        if(!keys[68] && !keys[65] && weaponSway.strafeY>0){
            
            weaponSway.strafeY-=player.maxSpeed/2;
            
        }
        
    }

    //Turn using arrow keys
    {
        
        if(keys[37]){
            
            player.angle-=player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF;
            
            
            //both camera direction and camera plane must be rotated
            var oldDirX = player.dirX;
            player.dirX = player.dirX * cos(-player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF) - player.dirY * sin(-player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF);
            player.dirY = oldDirX * sin(-player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF) + player.dirY * cos(-player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF);
            
            var oldPlaneX = player.planeX;
            player.planeX = player.planeX * cos(-player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF) - player.planeY * sin(-player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF);
            player.planeY = oldPlaneX * sin(-player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF) + player.planeY * cos(-player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF);
            
            //Sway to right
            if(weaponSway.turnX<weaponSway.maxTurn){
                
                weaponSway.turnX+=player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Reset Sway
        else if(weaponSway.turnX>0){
            
            weaponSway.turnX-=player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF;
            
        }
        
        if(keys[39]){
            
            player.angle+=player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF;
            
            //both camera direction and camera plane must be rotated
            var oldDirX = player.dirX;
            player.dirX = player.dirX * cos(player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF) - player.dirY * sin(player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF);
            player.dirY = oldDirX * sin(player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF) + player.dirY * cos(player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF);
            
            var oldPlaneX = player.planeX;
            player.planeX = player.planeX * cos(player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF) - player.planeY * sin(player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF);
            player.planeY = oldPlaneX * sin(player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF) + player.planeY * cos(player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF);
            
            //Sway to left
            if(weaponSway.turnX>-weaponSway.maxTurn){
                
                weaponSway.turnX-=player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Reset Sway
        else if(weaponSway.turnX<0){
            
            weaponSway.turnX+=player.turnSpeed*game.deltaF;
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Shoot
    
    if(((!mouseLocked && keys[38]) || (mouseLocked && mouseIsPressed)) && player.cooldown<=0 && (player.clip[[player.projectile]]>0 || projectileConstants[player.projectile].type==="melee") && (projectileConstants[player.projectile].reloadType!=="charging" || player.clip[[player.projectile]]>=projectileConstants[player.projectile].clip) && player.armed){
        
        //Stop Reloading
        player.reloading=false;
        
        player.reloadTime=0;
        
        //Shoot
        if(projectileConstants[player.projectile].type==="projectile"){
            
            for(var s = 0; s <= projectileConstants[player.projectile].shots-1; s++){
                
                var dud=false;
                var dudNum=0;
                
                //Find duds
                {
                    
                    //Variables for search
                    var i=0;
                    var search=true;
                    
                    //Search
                    while(search){
                        
                        if(projectiles.dud[i]){
                            
                            dudNum=i;
                            
                            dud=true;
                            
                            search=false;
                            
                        }
                        
                        i++;
                        
                        if(i>=projectiles.dud.length-1){
                            
                            search=false;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                var angleShot=player.angle+random(-projectileConstants[player.projectile].deviation,projectileConstants[player.projectile].deviation);
                var vAngleShot=player.vAngle+random(-projectileConstants[player.projectile].deviation,projectileConstants[player.projectile].deviation);
                
                var realAngle = angleShot - 90;
                
                var vx = (sin(angleShot)*projectileConstants[player.projectile].speed)*cos(vAngleShot);
                var vy = (cos(angleShot)*-projectileConstants[player.projectile].speed)*cos(vAngleShot);
                var vz = sin(vAngleShot)*projectileConstants[player.projectile].speed;
                
                //Create Projectile
                if(!dud){
                    
                    projectiles.x.push(player.x);
                    projectiles.y.push(player.y);
                    
                    projectiles.speedX.push(vx);
                    projectiles.speedY.push(vy);
                    
                    projectiles.vix.push(vx);
                    projectiles.viy.push(vy);
                    projectiles.viz.push(vz);
                    
                    projectiles.animation.push(0);
                    
                    projectiles.animationFrame.push(0);
                    
                    projectiles.verticalPos.push(player.height - ((player.height - player.floorHeight)/6)); 
                    
                    projectiles.verticalVelocity.push(vz);
                    
                    projectiles.fuse.push(0);
                    
                    projectiles.angle.push(angleShot);
                    
                    projectiles.type.push(player.projectile);
                    
                    projectiles.friendly.push(true);
                    
                    projectiles.dud.push(false);
                    
                }
                
                
                else{
                    
                    
                    projectiles.x[dudNum]=player.x;
                    projectiles.y[dudNum]=player.y;
                    
                    projectiles.speedX[dudNum]=vx;
                    projectiles.speedY[dudNum]=vy;
                    
                    projectiles.vix[dudNum]=vx;
                    projectiles.viy[dudNum]=vy;
                    projectiles.viz[dudNum]=vz;
                    
                    projectiles.animation[dudNum]=0;
                    
                    projectiles.animationFrame[dudNum]=0;
                    projectiles.verticalPos[dudNum]=player.height - (-player.floorHeight/6);
                    
                    projectiles.verticalVelocity[dudNum]=vz;
                    
                    projectiles.fuse[dudNum]=0;
                    
                    projectiles.angle[dudNum]=angleShot;
                    
                    projectiles.type[dudNum]=player.projectile;
                    
                    projectiles.friendly[dudNum]=true;
                    
                    projectiles.dud[dudNum]=false;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            //Move offscreen
            weaponSway.recoilY-=weaponSway.recoilSpeed;
            
            weaponSway.recoilFactor=-1;
            
            if(weaponSway.recoilY<-weaponSway.maxRecoil){
                
                weaponSway.recoilY=-weaponSway.maxRecoil;
                
                weaponSway.recoilFactor=1;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        if(projectileConstants[player.projectile].type==="hitScan" || projectileConstants[player.projectile].type==="melee"){
            
            //Move offscreen
            weaponSway.recoilY-=weaponSway.recoilSpeed;
            
            weaponSway.recoilFactor=-1;
            
            if(weaponSway.recoilY<-weaponSway.maxRecoil){
                
                weaponSway.recoilY=-weaponSway.maxRecoil;
                
                weaponSway.recoilFactor=1;
                
            }
            
            var enemiesHurt=[];
            
            for(var e=0; e<=enemies.x.length-1; e++){
                
                enemiesHurt.push(0);
                
            }
            
            for(var shot=1; shot<=projectileConstants[player.projectile].shots; shot++){
                
                var angleShot=player.angle+random(-projectileConstants[player.projectile].deviation/2,projectileConstants[player.projectile].deviation/2);
                var vAngleShot=player.vAngle+random(-projectileConstants[player.projectile].deviation,projectileConstants[player.projectile].deviation);
                
                var realAngle = angleShot - 90;
                
                var dz = sin(vAngleShot);
                
                var range = Infinity;
                
                if(projectileConstants[player.projectile].type==="melee"){
                    
                    range = projectileConstants[player.projectile].range;
                    
                }
                
                //Find what enemy it hit
                var hitScanValue=hitScan(angleShot,player.x,player.y,dz,'enemies',player.height,range);
                
                var enemy='none';
                
                if(hitScanValue.hits.length > 0){
                    
                    enemy = hitScanValue.hits[0];
                    
                }
                
                else{
                    
                    enemy = 'none';
                    
                }
                
                //Apply damage if enemy is hit
                if(enemy!=='none'){
                    
                    enemiesHurt[enemy]+=projectileConstants[player.projectile].damage;
                    
                }
                
                var lineAngle=angleShot-player.angle;
                
                var halfFOV=60;
                
                var lineX = (lineAngle/halfFOV)*(width/2);
                
                var vLineAngle=vAngleShot-player.vAngle;
                
                var lineY = -(vLineAngle/halfFOV)*(height/2);
                
                var rx = constrain(random(-3,3),-3 + 4*hitScanValue.nx,3 + 4*hitScanValue.nx);
                var ry = constrain(random(-3,3),-3 + 4*hitScanValue.ny,3 + 4*hitScanValue.ny);
                var rz = constrain(random(-60,60),-60 + 80*hitScanValue.nz,60 + 80*hitScanValue.nz);
                
                //Draw hitscan line
                if(range > hitScanValue.t){
                    
                    //Create a particle and draw a line
                    switch(player.projectile){
                        
                        //Gumball Machine
                        case 3:
                            
                            createParticle(hitScanValue.x+hitScanValue.nx,hitScanValue.y+hitScanValue.ny,rx,ry,hitScanValue.z+hitScanValue.nz,rz,floor(random(9,14)));
                            
                            hitScanLines.color.push([249, 246, 238,100]);
                            hitScanLines.weight.push(3);
                            
                            break;
                        
                        //Shotgun
                        case 6:
                            
                            createParticle(hitScanValue.x+hitScanValue.nx,hitScanValue.y+hitScanValue.ny,rx,ry,hitScanValue.z+hitScanValue.nz,rz,6);
                            
                            hitScanLines.color.push([134, 240, 34,255]);
                            hitScanLines.weight.push(3);
                            
                            break;
                        
                        //Gattling Laser
                        case 7:
                            
                            createParticle(hitScanValue.x+hitScanValue.nx,hitScanValue.y+hitScanValue.ny,0,0,hitScanValue.z+hitScanValue.nz,0,7);
                            
                            hitScanLines.color.push([230, 100, 34,255]);
                            hitScanLines.weight.push(5);
                            
                            break;
                        
                        default:
                            
                            createParticle(hitScanValue.x+hitScanValue.nx,hitScanValue.y+hitScanValue.ny,0,0,hitScanValue.z+hitScanValue.nz,0,1);
                            
                            hitScanLines.color.push([228, 208, 10,255]);
                            hitScanLines.weight.push(3);
                            
                    }
                    
                    var gunX=weaponSway.posX + weaponSway.turnX + weaponSway.strafeX + weaponSway.walkX + weaponSway.switchX + weaponSway.reloadX + -weaponSway.recoilY/1.5;
                    
                    var gunY=weaponSway.posY + weaponSway.turnY + weaponSway.strafeY + weaponSway.walkY + weaponSway.switchY + weaponSway.reloadY + weaponSway.recoilY;
                    
                    if((projectileConstants[player.projectile].type !== 'melee')){
                        
                        hitScanLines.x1.push(game.realWidth/2+lineX);
                        hitScanLines.y1.push(game.realHeight/2+lineY);
                        hitScanLines.x2.push(gunX+projectileConstants[player.projectile].shootX);
                        hitScanLines.y2.push(gunY+projectileConstants[player.projectile].shootY);
                        
                        
                    }
                    
                    else if (enemy.length === 0){
                        
                        playSoundEffect(19);
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            for(var e=0; e<=enemies.x.length-1; e++){
                
                if(enemiesHurt[e]>0){
                    
                    hurt(1,e,enemiesHurt[e],projectileConstants[player.projectile].knockback,atan2(enemies.y[e]-player.y,enemies.x[e]-player.x),atan(((enemies.verticalPos[e]-player.height))/dist(enemies.x[e],enemies.y[e],player.x,player.y)));
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            
        }
        
        //Remove ammo
        if(projectileConstants[player.projectile].type!=="melee"){
            
            if(projectileConstants[player.projectile].reloadable){
                
                player.clip[[player.projectile]]-=projectileConstants[player.projectile].consumption;
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                player.ammo[projectileConstants[player.projectile].ammo]-=projectileConstants[player.projectile].consumption;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Fix negative ammo
        if(player.clip[[player.projectile]] < 0){
            
            player.clip[[player.projectile]] = 0;
            
        }
        
        if(player.ammo[projectileConstants[player.projectile].ammo]<0){
            
            player.ammo[projectileConstants[player.projectile].ammo] = 0;
            
        }
        
        //Play Sound Effect
        switch(player.projectile){
            
            case 0:
                
                playSoundEffect(0);
                
                break;
            
            case 5:
                
                playSoundEffect(1);
                
                break;
            
            case 3:
                
                playSoundEffect(2);
                
                break;
                
            case 6:
                
                playSoundEffect(3);
                
                break;
                
            case 7:
                
                playSoundEffect(4);
                
                break;
                
            case 8:
                
                playSoundEffect(5);
                
                break;
                
            case 2:
                
                playSoundEffect(20);
                
                break;
            
            
        }
        
        if(!game.semiPause){
            
            //Add to temp lighting
            tempMap.lighting[floor(player.y/20)][floor(player.x/20)] += projectileConstants[player.projectile].lighting/2;
            
            for(var y = constrain(floor(player.y/20)-1,0,tempMap.lighting.length-1); y <= constrain(floor(player.y/20)+1,0,tempMap.lighting.length-1);y++){
                
                for(var x = constrain(floor(player.x/20)-1,0,tempMap.lighting[y].length-1); x <= constrain(floor(player.x/20)+1,0,tempMap.lighting[y].length-1);x++){
                    
                    tempMap.lighting[y][x] += projectileConstants[player.projectile].lighting/2;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Alert enemies
        for(var e = 0; e <= enemies.state.length-1; e++){
            
            if((enemies.state[e] === 'hunting' || enemies.state[e] === 'alert') && enemies.distance[e] <= projectileConstants[player.projectile].audibleRange){
                
                enemies.state[e] = 'hunting';
                
                enemies.target[e] = [player.x,player.y];
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Set cooldown
        player.cooldown=projectileConstants[player.projectile].cooldown;
        
    }
     
    //Reload
    if(keys[82] && !player.reloading && player.ammo[projectileConstants[player.projectile].ammo]>0 && player.clip[player.projectile]<projectileConstants[player.projectile].clip && player.armed){
        
        player.reloading=true;
        
        //Move offscreen
        weaponSway.reloadY+=weaponSway.reloadSpeed;
        
        weaponSway.reloadFactor=1;
        
        if(weaponSway.reloadY>weaponSway.maxReload){
            
            weaponSway.reloadY=weaponSway.maxReload;
            
            weaponSway.reloadFactor=-1;
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Switch weapons
    if(keys[16]){
        
        if(!controls.toggles.change && player.armed && player.cooldown <= 0){
            
            //Stop Reload
            {
                
                player.reloading=false;
                
                player.reloadTime=0;
                
            }
            
            //Switch Weapon
            {
                
                var start=player.projectile;
                
                var num=player.projectile;
                
                var found=false;
                
                //Change the number
                
                num++;
                
                //Constrain
                if(num>projectileConstants.length-1){
                    
                    num=0;
                    
                }
                
                if(num<0){
                    
                    num = projectileConstants.length-1;
                    
                }
                
                while(num!==start && !found){
                    
                    if(player.weapons[num]){
                        
                        player.projectile=num;
                        
                        found=true;
                        
                        //Move offscreen
                        weaponSway.switchY+=weaponSway.switchSpeed;
                        
                        weaponSway.switchFactor=1;
                        
                        if(weaponSway.switchY>weaponSway.maxSwitch){
                            
                            weaponSway.switchY=weaponSway.maxSwitch;
                            
                            weaponSway.switchFactor=-1;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    else{
                        
                        num++;
                        
                        //Constrain
                        if(num>projectileConstants.length-1){
                            
                            num=0;
                            
                        }
                        
                        if(num<0){
                            
                            num = projectileConstants.length-1;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            //Toggle Control
            controls.toggles.change=true;
            
        }
        
    }
    
    else if(keys[17]){
        
        if(!controls.toggles.change && player.armed && player.cooldown <= 0){
            
            //Stop Reload
            {
                
                player.reloading=false;
                
                player.reloadTime=0;
                
            }
            
            //Switch Weapon
            {
                
                var start=player.projectile;
                
                var num=player.projectile;
                
                var found=false;
                
                //Change the number
                
                
                num--;
                
                
                
                //Constrain
                if(num>projectileConstants.length-1){
                    
                    num=0;
                    
                }
                
                if(num<0){
                    
                    num = projectileConstants.length-1;
                    
                }
                
                while(num!==start && !found){
                    
                    if(player.weapons[num]){
                        
                        player.projectile=num;
                        
                        found=true;
                        
                        //Move offscreen
                        weaponSway.switchY+=weaponSway.switchSpeed;
                        
                        weaponSway.switchFactor=1;
                        
                        if(weaponSway.switchY>weaponSway.maxSwitch){
                            
                            weaponSway.switchY=weaponSway.maxSwitch;
                            
                            weaponSway.switchFactor=-1;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    else{
                        
                        //Change the number
                        num--;
                        
                        //Constrain
                        if(num>projectileConstants.length-1){
                            
                            num=0;
                            
                        }
                        
                        if(num<0){
                            
                            num = projectileConstants.length-1;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            //Toggle Control
            controls.toggles.change=true;
            
        }
        
    }
    
    else{
        
        controls.toggles.change=false;
        
    }
    
    //Swap to Sword
    if(keys[49] && player.projectile !== 0){
        
        player.cooldown = 0;
        
        player.projectile=0;
        
        //Move offscreen
        weaponSway.switchY+=weaponSway.switchSpeed;
        
        weaponSway.switchFactor=1;
        
        if(weaponSway.switchY>weaponSway.maxSwitch){
            
            weaponSway.switchY=weaponSway.maxSwitch;
            
            weaponSway.switchFactor=-1;
            
        }
        
        
    }
    
    //Swap to flare/pistol, shotgun/laser shotgun, gumball/laser, firework/launcher
    for(var n = 0; n <= 3; n++){
        
        if(keys[50+n] && player.projectile !== n+1 && (player.weapons[n+1]||player.weapons[n+5])){
            
            player.cooldown = 0;
            
            if(player.weapons[n+1]){
                
                player.projectile=n+1;
                
            }
            
            else{
                
                player.projectile=n+5;
                
            }
            //Move offscreen
            weaponSway.switchY+=weaponSway.switchSpeed;
            
            weaponSway.switchFactor=1;
            
            if(weaponSway.switchY>weaponSway.maxSwitch){
                
                weaponSway.switchY=weaponSway.maxSwitch;
                
                weaponSway.switchFactor=-1;
                
            }
            
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Interact
    if(keys[69]){
        
        var angleShot=player.angle;
        
        var vAngleShot=player.vAngle;
        
        var dz = sin(vAngleShot);
        
        var range = 30;
        
        //Find what enemy it hit
        var hitScanValue=hitScan(angleShot,player.x,player.y,dz,'enemies',player.height,range);
        
        if(range > hitScanValue.t && hitScanValue.nz === 0){
            
            var x = hitScanValue.mapX;
            var y = hitScanValue.mapY;
            
            tempMap.doorActivated[y][x] = true;
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Break the cursor
    if(keys[85] || game.cam < 2){
        
        mouseLocked = false;
        
    }
    
    //Jump
    if(keys[32] && player.canJump){
        
        player.vertSpeed=player.jumpSpeed;
        
        player.canJump = false;
        
    }
    
    //Crouch
    if(keys[67]){
        
        player.floorHeight=player.defaultFloorHeight/2;
        
    }
    
    else{
        
        player.floorHeight=player.defaultFloorHeight;
        
    }
    
    }
    
    player.cooldown-=game.deltaF;
    
};

//Create explosion
var explosion=function(impactX,impactY,impactZ,normalX,normalY,range,damage,knockback,type,friendly){
    
    //Set explosion type vars
    var limitType = [[1,1],[1.5,1.5],[4,4],[6,6],[5,5]];
    
    var xLimit = limitType[type][0];
    
    var yLimit = limitType[type][1];
    
    var boundX = [-xLimit,xLimit];
    var boundY = [-yLimit,yLimit];
    
    if(normalX === 1){
        
        boundX = [0,xLimit];
        
        
    }
    
    else if(normalX === -1){
        
        
        boundX = [-xLimit,0];
        
    }
    
    if(normalY === 1){
        
        boundY = [0,yLimit];
        
        
    }
    
    else if(normalY === -1){
        
        
        boundY = [-yLimit,0];
        
    }
    
    var partType = [[10,15],[4,7],[7,8],[10,11],[6,8]];
    
    var minPart = partType[type][0];
    
    var maxPart = partType[type][1];
    
    var colorNum = 0;
    
    //Play Sound
    switch(type){
        
        case 1:
            
            
            break;
        
        
        case 2:
            
            
            break;
            
        default:
            
            if(range>0){
                
                playSoundEffect(10);
                
            }
    }
    
    //Make particles
    
    switch(type){
        
        case 2:
            
            createParticle(impactX+normalX*5,impactY+normalY*5,random(boundX[0],boundX[1]),random(boundY[0],boundY[1]),impactZ,random(0,80),18);
            
            break;
            
        case 3:
            
            colorNum = floor(random(0,4))*2;
            
            break;
            
        default:
            
            if(range>0){
                
                createParticle(impactX+normalX*5,impactY+normalY*5,0,0,impactZ,0,41);
                
            }
            
        
    }
    
    for(var p = 0; p <= random(minPart,maxPart); p++){
        
        switch(type){
            
            case 1:
                
                createParticle(impactX+random(-range/2,range/2),impactY+random(-range/2,range/2),random(boundX[0],boundX[1]),random(boundY[0],boundY[1]),impactZ,random(-30,30),1);
                
                createParticle(impactX+random(-range/2,range/2),impactY+random(-range/2,range/2),random(boundX[0],boundX[1]),random(boundY[0],boundY[1]),impactZ,random(-30,30),5);
                
            break;
            
            case 2:
                
                createParticle(impactX+random(-range/2,range/2),impactY+random(-range/2,range/2),random(boundX[0],boundX[1]),random(boundY[0],boundY[1]),impactZ,random(-80,80),16);
                
                createParticle(impactX+random(-range/2,range/2),impactY+random(-range/2,range/2),random(boundX[0]/4,boundX[1]/4),random(boundY[0]/4,boundY[1]/4),impactZ,random(-10,10),17);
                
                break;
            
            case 3:
                
                createParticle(impactX+random(-range/20,range/20),impactY+random(-range/20,range/20),0,0,impactZ,0,22);
                
                createParticle(impactX,impactY,random(boundX[0],boundX[1]),random(boundY[0],boundY[1]),impactZ,random(-80,80),23 + colorNum);
                
                createParticle(impactX+random(-range/2,range/2),impactY+random(-range/2,range/2),random(boundX[0]/4,boundX[1]/4),random(boundY[0]/4,boundY[1]/4),impactZ+random(-range/2,range/2)*20,random(-10,10),17);
                
                createParticle(impactX+random(-range/2,range/2),impactY+random(-range/2,range/2),random(boundX[0]/4,boundX[1]/4),random(boundY[0]/4,boundY[1]/4),impactZ+random(-range/2,range/2)*20,random(-10,10),20);
                
                createParticle(impactX+random(-range/2,range/2),impactY+random(-range/2,range/2),random(boundX[0]/4,boundX[1]/4),random(boundY[0]/4,boundY[1]/4),impactZ+random(-range/2,range/2)*20,random(-10,10),19);
                
            break;
            
            case 4:
                
                for(var n = 0; n <= 3; n++){
                    createParticle(impactX+random(-range/2,range/2),impactY+random(-range/2,range/2),random(boundX[0],boundX[1]),random(boundY[0],boundY[1]),impactZ,random(-30,30),1);
                }
                
                createParticle(impactX+random(-range/2,range/2),impactY+random(-range/2,range/2),random(boundX[0],boundX[1]),random(boundY[0],boundY[1]),impactZ,random(-30,30),8);
                
            break;
            
            default:
                
                if(range>0){
                    
                    createParticle(impactX+random(-range/2,range/2),impactY+random(-range/2,range/2),random(boundX[0],boundX[1]),random(boundY[0],boundY[1]),impactZ,random(-30,30),40);
                    
                    createParticle(impactX+random(-range/2,range/2),impactY+random(-range/2,range/2),random(boundX[0],boundX[1]),random(boundY[0],boundY[1]),impactZ,random(-30,30),42);
                    
                }
                
            break;
        
        }
        
    }
    
    //Check enemies
    for(var e = 0; e <= enemies.x.length-1; e++){
        
        if(dist(impactX,impactY,enemies.x[e],enemies.y[e])<=range && !enemies.dud[e]){
            
            var eHeight = enemies.verticalPos[e] + enemyConstants[enemies.type[e]].height/2;
            
            var dt = constrain(damage * ((range - dist(impactX,impactY,enemies.x[e],enemies.y[e])+enemyConstants[enemies.type[e]].width)/range),damage*0.2,damage);
            
            hurt(1,e,dt,knockback,atan2(enemies.y[e]-impactY,enemies.x[e]-impactX),atan(((eHeight-impactZ))/(dist(impactX,impactY,player.x,player.y))));
            
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Check player
    if(dist(impactX,impactY,player.x,player.y)<=range){
        
        var pHeight = player.height+player.floorHeight/2;
        
        var dt = constrain(damage * ((range - dist(impactX,impactY,player.x,player.y)+player.width)/range),damage*0.2,damage);
        
        if(!friendly){
            
            hurt(0,0,dt,knockback,atan2(player.y-impactY,player.x-impactX),atan(((player.height-impactZ))/dist(impactX,impactY,player.x,player.y)));
            
        }
        
        else{
            
            hurt(0,0,dt/3,knockback,atan2(player.y-impactY,player.x-impactX),atan(((player.height-impactZ))/dist(impactX,impactY,player.x,player.y)));
            
        }
        
    }
    
};


//Map specific scripts
var mapScripts = function(){
    
    //Map specific actions
    switch(loadedMap.name){
        
        case 'mines_1':
            
            if(player.height<-4000){
                
                player.health = 0;
                
            }
            
            if(player.height>=2000 && !game.triggers[8].includes(false)){
                
                game.map++;
                
                loadMap();
                
                return;
                
            }
            
            break;
        
        case 'mines_2':
            
            if(player.height<=-3000){
                
                player.health = 0;
                
            }
            
            for(var i = 0; i <= enemies.health.length; i++){
                
                if(enemies.verticalPos[i]<=-3000 && !enemies.dud[i]){
                    
                    hurt(1,i,enemies.health[i],0,0,0);
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            if(player.height<=-2000 && !game.triggers[10].includes(false)){
                
                game.map++;
                
                loadMap();
                
                return;
                
            }
            
            //Trigger explosions when you encounter dynamite guy
            if(!game.triggers[8].includes(false)){
                
                //impactX,impactY,impactZ,normalX,normalY,range,damage,knockback,type,friendly
                
                if(mapVars[0][1]<7){
                    
                    if(mapVars[0][0] <= 0){
                        
                        switch(mapVars[0][1]){
                            
                            case 0:
                                
                                //m
                                explosion(1520,1034,-200,0,1,20,0,0,0,false);
                                explosion(1480,1034,-500,0,1,20,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                //l
                                explosion(1569,1027,-600,0,1,15,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                //r
                                explosion(1410,1027,-600,0,1,15,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                break;
                            
                            case 1:
                                
                                
                                //m
                                explosion(1420,1034,-100,0,1,20,0,0,0,false);
                                explosion(1500,1034,-700,0,1,20,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                //l
                                explosion(1549,1027,-500,0,1,15,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                //r
                                explosion(1430,1027,-300,0,1,15,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                
                                break;
                            
                            case 2:
                                
                                
                                //m
                                explosion(1460,1034,-300,0,1,20,0,0,0,false);
                                explosion(1535,1034,-600,0,1,20,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                //l
                                explosion(1550,1027,-100,0,1,15,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                //r
                                explosion(1420,1027,-700,0,1,15,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                
                                break;
                            
                            case 3:
                                
                                
                                //m
                                explosion(1520,1034,-200,0,1,20,0,0,0,false);
                                explosion(1480,1034,-500,0,1,20,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                //l
                                explosion(1569,1027,-600,0,1,15,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                //r
                                explosion(1410,1027,-600,0,1,15,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                
                                break;
                            
                            case 4:
                                
                                
                                //m
                                explosion(1420,1034,-100,0,1,20,0,0,0,false);
                                explosion(1500,1034,-700,0,1,20,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                //l
                                explosion(1549,1027,-500,0,1,15,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                //r
                                explosion(1430,1027,-300,0,1,15,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                
                                break;
                            
                            case 5:
                                
                                
                                //m
                                explosion(1520,1034,-200,0,1,20,0,0,0,false);
                                explosion(1480,1034,-500,0,1,20,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                //l
                                explosion(1569,1027,-600,0,1,15,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                //r
                                explosion(1410,1027,-600,0,1,15,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                
                                
                                break;
                            
                            case 6:
                                
                                
                                //m
                                explosion(1420,1034,-100,0,1,20,0,0,0,false);
                                explosion(1500,1034,-700,0,1,20,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                //l
                                explosion(1549,1027,-500,0,1,15,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                //r
                                explosion(1430,1027,-300,0,1,15,0,0,0,false);
                                
                                
                                break;
                            
                        }
                        
                        mapVars[0][0] = 7;
                        
                        mapVars[0][1]++;
                        
                    }
                    
                    else{
                        
                        mapVars[0][0] -= game.deltaF;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
        break;
        
        case 'mines_3':
            
            
            if(player.height<=-4000){
                
                player.health = 0;
                
            }
            
            for(var i = 0; i <= enemies.health.length; i++){
                
                if(enemies.verticalPos[i]<=-3000 && !enemies.dud[i]){
                    
                    hurt(1,i,enemies.health[i],0,0,0);
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            if(player.height<=-2000 && !game.triggers[9].includes(false)){
                
                game.map++;
                
                loadMap();
                
                return;
                
            }
            
            
            break;
            
        case 'mines_4':
            
            //Non boss stuff
            
            if(player.height<=-4000){
                
                player.health = 0;
                
            }
            
            if(player.height>=2000 && !game.triggers[2].includes(false)){
                
                game.map++;
                
                loadMap();
                
                return;
                
            }
            
            //Set vars based on target
            if(mapVars[1][0] === 'init'){
                
                for(var i = 0; i <= enemies.type.length-1; i++){
                    
                    switch(enemies.type[i]){
                        
                        case 9:
                            
                            mapVars[1][3] = i;
                            
                            break;
                        
                        case 10:
                            
                            mapVars[1][7] = i;
                            
                            break;
                        
                        case 11:
                            
                            mapVars[1][11] = i;
                            
                            break;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                player.height=10000;
                
                mapVars[1][0] = 'intro';
                
            }
            
            //Create plaintext vars (only useful for constants)
            var hi = mapVars[1][3];
            var li = mapVars[1][7];
            var ri = mapVars[1][11];
            
            var leftHandDead = enemies.dud[li];
            var rightHandDead = enemies.dud[ri];
            
            var heightOffset = 100;
            var idleTime = 150;
            var reelTime = 60;
            var attackTime = [40,6,60,120];
            var projCooldown = 3;
            
            //attacking index
            var ai = -1;
            
            //Do one last check for if the king is dead
            if(enemies.dud[hi]){
                
                mapVars[1][0] = 'dead';
                
            }
            
            //Mode based behavior
            switch(mapVars[1][0]){
                
                case 'intro':
                    
                    {
                        
                        //Mode timer
                        mapVars[1][1] -= game.deltaF;
                        
                        //Intro Start timer
                        mapVars[1][2] -= game.deltaF;
                        
                        //Stay down at first
                        if(mapVars[1][2] > 0){
                            
                            mapVars[1][6]=-3000+heightOffset;
                            mapVars[1][10]=-3000+heightOffset;
                            mapVars[1][14]=-3000+heightOffset;
                            
                        }
                        
                        //Pop up
                        else{
                            
                            mapVars[1][6] = 40*sin((millis()/1000)*180)+heightOffset;
                            mapVars[1][10] = 20*sin((millis()/1000)*180)+heightOffset;
                            mapVars[1][14] = 20*sin((millis()/1000)*180)+heightOffset;
                            
                        }
                        
                        if(mapVars[1][1]<=0){
                            
                            mapVars[1][0] = 'idle';
                            
                            mapVars[1][1] = idleTime;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                
                case 'idle':
                    
                    {
                        
                        //First see if we needed to swap hands
                        if((player.x<330 || rightHandDead) && !leftHandDead){
                            
                            mapVars[1][15] = 'left';
                            
                        }
                        
                        if((player.x>390 || leftHandDead) && !rightHandDead){
                            
                            mapVars[1][15] = 'right';
                            
                        }
                        
                        //Bobbing motion
                        mapVars[1][6] = 40*sin((millis()/1000)*180)+heightOffset;
                        mapVars[1][10] = 20*sin((millis()/1000)*180)+heightOffset;
                        mapVars[1][14] = 20*sin((millis()/1000)*180)+heightOffset;
                        
                        //Based on active hand we set target positions
                        var activeDisp = 0;
                        var idleDisp = 0;
                        var perpInv = 1;
                        
                        if(mapVars[1][15] === 'right'){
                            
                            activeDisp = 4;
                            
                        }
                        
                        else{
                            
                            idleDisp = 4;
                            
                            perpInv = -1;
                            
                        }
                        
                        //Start vector math
                        
                        //Unit vector from skull to player
                        var pvd = dist(player.x,player.y,enemies.x[hi],enemies.y[hi]);
                        
                        var pvx = (player.x - enemies.x[hi])/pvd;
                        var pvy = (player.y - enemies.y[hi])/pvd;
                        
                        //Perpendicular vector
                        var opvx = pvy*perpInv;
                        var opvy = -pvx*perpInv;
                        
                        //Set the targets
                        var al = pvd/2;
                        var il = 20;
                        var iw = 35;
                        
                        //Active
                        mapVars[1][8+activeDisp]=enemies.x[hi] + pvx*al;
                        mapVars[1][9+activeDisp]=enemies.y[hi] + pvy*al;
                        
                        //Passive
                        mapVars[1][8+idleDisp]=enemies.x[hi] + pvx*il + opvx*iw;
                        mapVars[1][9+idleDisp]=enemies.y[hi] + pvy*il + opvy*iw;
                        
                        //Swap to attack
                        
                        //Mode timer
                        mapVars[1][1] -= game.deltaF;
                        
                        if(mapVars[1][1]<=0){
                            
                            mapVars[1][0] = 'reel';
                            
                            mapVars[1][1] = reelTime;
                            
                            //reel uses the left right variable to tell which one is going to be attacking, we swap these if there's both hands still, but we keep the single useful hand the same so it can swap to the attack position
                            if(!leftHandDead && !rightHandDead){
                                
                                if(mapVars[1][15] === 'right'){
                                    
                                    mapVars[1][15] = 'left';
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    mapVars[1][15] = 'right';
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            //Pick attack
                            mapVars[1][16] = floor(random(0,3));
                            
                            //Skull Attack
                            if(leftHandDead && rightHandDead){
                                
                                mapVars[1][16] = 3;
                                
                            }
                            //mapVars[1][16] = 2;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                
                case 'reel':
                    
                    {
                        
                        //Bobbing motion
                        mapVars[1][6] = 40*sin((millis()/1000)*180)+heightOffset;
                        mapVars[1][10] = 20*sin((millis()/1000)*180)+heightOffset;
                        mapVars[1][14] = 20*sin((millis()/1000)*180)+heightOffset;
                        
                        //Based on active hand we set target positions
                        var blockDisp = 0;
                        var attackDisp = 0;
                        var perpInv = 1;
                        
                        if(mapVars[1][15] === 'left'){
                            
                            blockDisp = 4;
                            
                        }
                        
                        else{
                            
                            attackDisp = 4;
                            
                            perpInv = -1;
                            
                        }
                        
                        //Start vector math
                        
                        //Unit vector from skull to player
                        var pvd = dist(player.x,player.y,enemies.x[hi],enemies.y[hi]);
                        
                        var pvx = (player.x - enemies.x[hi])/pvd;
                        var pvy = (player.y - enemies.y[hi])/pvd;
                        
                        //Perpendicular vector
                        var opvx = pvy*perpInv;
                        var opvy = -pvx*perpInv;
                        
                        //Set the targets
                        var al = pvd/2;
                        var il = 20;
                        var iw = 35;
                        
                        //Block
                        mapVars[1][8+blockDisp]=enemies.x[hi] + pvx*al;
                        mapVars[1][9+blockDisp]=enemies.y[hi] + pvy*al;
                        
                        ai = mapVars[1][7+attackDisp];
                        
                        switch(mapVars[1][16]){
                            
                            //Swipe
                            case 0:
                                
                                //Save distance
                                mapVars[1][17] = pvd;
                                
                                //Change to slap hand
                                enemies.animation[ai] = 1;
                                
                                //Hold hand back
                                mapVars[1][8+attackDisp]=enemies.x[hi] + pvd*(-perpInv);
                                mapVars[1][9+attackDisp]=enemies.y[hi];
                                
                                mapVars[1][10+attackDisp] -= 100;
                                
                                break;
                            
                            //Punch
                            case 1:
                                
                                //Save distance
                                mapVars[1][18] = player.x;
                                mapVars[1][19] = player.y;
                                
                                mapVars[1][20] = enemies.x[ai];
                                mapVars[1][21] = enemies.y[ai];
                                
                                //Change to fist hand
                                enemies.animation[ai] = 2;
                                
                                var il = -10;
                                var iw = 55;
                                
                                //Hold hand back
                                mapVars[1][8+attackDisp]=enemies.x[hi] + pvx*il + opvx*iw;
                                mapVars[1][9+attackDisp]=enemies.y[hi] + pvy*il + opvy*iw;
                                
                                mapVars[1][10+attackDisp] -= 100;
                                
                                break;
                            
                            //Projectile
                            case 2:
                                
                                //Change to open hand
                                enemies.animation[ai] = 0;
                                
                                var il = 30;
                                var iw = 50;
                                
                                //Hold hand back
                                mapVars[1][8+attackDisp]=enemies.x[hi] + pvx*il + opvx*iw;
                                mapVars[1][9+attackDisp]=enemies.y[hi] + pvy*il + opvy*iw;
                                
                                break;
                            
                            //Head only
                            case 3:
                                
                                //Track player
                                mapVars[1][4] = player.x;
                                
                                //Save distance
                                mapVars[1][17] = pvd;
                                
                                //Hijack unused var for whcih way it should move
                                mapVars[1][18] = enemies.speedX[hi]/abs(enemies.speedX[hi]);
                                
                                
                                break;
                            
                        }
                        
                        //Swap to attack
                        
                        //Mode timer
                        mapVars[1][1] -= game.deltaF;
                        
                        if(mapVars[1][1]<=0){
                            
                            mapVars[1][0] = 'attack';
                            
                            mapVars[1][1] = attackTime[mapVars[1][16]];
                            
                            enemies.animation[hi] = 1;
                            enemies.vertSpeed[ai] = 0;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                    
                
                case 'attack':
                    
                    {
                        
                        //Bobbing motion
                        mapVars[1][6] = 40*sin((millis()/1000)*180)+heightOffset;
                        mapVars[1][10] = 20*sin((millis()/1000)*180)+heightOffset;
                        mapVars[1][14] = 20*sin((millis()/1000)*180)+heightOffset;
                        
                        //Based on active hand we set target positions
                        var blockDisp = 0;
                        var attackDisp = 0;
                        var perpInv = 1;
                        
                        if(mapVars[1][15] === 'left'){
                            
                            blockDisp = 4;
                            
                        }
                        
                        else{
                            
                            attackDisp = 4;
                            
                            perpInv = -1;
                            
                        }
                        
                        //Start vector math
                        
                        //Unit vector from skull to player
                        var pvd = dist(player.x,player.y,enemies.x[hi],enemies.y[hi]);
                        
                        var pvx = (player.x - enemies.x[hi])/pvd;
                        var pvy = (player.y - enemies.y[hi])/pvd;
                        
                        //Perpendicular vector
                        var opvx = pvy*perpInv;
                        var opvy = -pvx*perpInv;
                        
                        //Set the targets
                        var al = pvd/2;
                        
                        //Block
                        mapVars[1][8+blockDisp]=enemies.x[hi] + pvx*al;
                        mapVars[1][9+blockDisp]=enemies.y[hi] + pvy*al;
                        
                        ai = mapVars[1][7+attackDisp];
                        
                        if(enemies.dud[ai] && mapVars[1][16] !== 3){
                            
                            mapVars[1][1] = 0;
                            
                        }
                        
                        var attackProg = attackTime[mapVars[1][16]]-mapVars[1][1];
                        
                        switch(mapVars[1][16]){
                            
                            //Swipe
                            case 0:
                                
                                //Change to slap hand
                                enemies.animation[ai] = 1;
                                
                                var currentAngle = 180 * (attackProg/attackTime[mapVars[1][16]]) -180;
                                
                                var tf = 1;
                                
                                if(mapVars[1][15] === 'right'){
                                    
                                    //currentAngle += 180;
                                    tf = -1;
                                    
                                }
                                
                                enemies.x[ai] = enemies.x[hi] + mapVars[1][17] * cos(currentAngle) * tf;
                                enemies.y[ai] = enemies.y[hi] + mapVars[1][17] * sin(currentAngle);
                                
                                enemies.angle[ai] = tf*currentAngle + 180;
                                
                                //Check if the player gets hit 
                                if(dist(player.x,player.y,enemies.x[ai],enemies.y[ai]) < player.width/2 + enemyConstants[enemies.type[ai]].width/2){
                                    
                                    //Stop attacking
                                    mapVars[1][1] = 0;
                                    
                                    //var hurt=function(type,num,damage,knockback,angle,vAngle){
                                    hurt(0,0,enemyConstants[enemies.type[ai]].damage,5,tf*currentAngle + 90,45);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                break;
                            
                            //Punch
                            case 1:
                                
                                //Change to fist hand
                                enemies.animation[ai] = 2;
                                
                                enemies.x[ai] = mapVars[1][20] + (mapVars[1][18]-mapVars[1][20])*(attackProg/attackTime[mapVars[1][16]]);
                                enemies.y[ai] = mapVars[1][21] + (mapVars[1][19]-mapVars[1][21])*(attackProg/attackTime[mapVars[1][16]]);
                                
                                var currentAngle = atan2(mapVars[1][18]-mapVars[1][20],mapVars[1][21]-mapVars[1][19])-90;
                                
                                //Check if the player gets hit 
                                if(dist(player.x,player.y,enemies.x[ai],enemies.y[ai]) < player.width/2 + enemyConstants[enemies.type[ai]].width/2){
                                    
                                    //Stop attacking
                                    mapVars[1][1] = 0;
                                    
                                    //var hurt=function(type,num,damage,knockback,angle,vAngle){
                                    hurt(0,0,enemyConstants[enemies.type[ai]].damage*1.5,8,currentAngle,0);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                break;
                            
                            //Projectile
                            case 2:
                            case 3:
                                
                                //Change to open hand
                                enemies.animation[ai] = 3;
                                
                                if(mapVars[1][16]===3){
                                    
                                    ai = hi;
                                    
                                    enemies.speedX[ai] = 0;
                                    
                                    enemies.speedY[ai] = 0;
                                    
                                    enemies.vertSpeed[ai] = 0;
                                    
                                    enemies.angle[ai]=-atan2(player.x-enemies.x[ai],player.y-enemies.y[ai])+180;
                                    
                                    var currentAngle = -mapVars[1][18]*360 * (attackProg/attackTime[mapVars[1][16]]) +90;
                                    
                                    enemies.x[ai] = constrain(player.x + mapVars[1][17] * cos(currentAngle),110,610);
                                    enemies.y[ai] = constrain(player.y + mapVars[1][17] * sin(currentAngle),125,470);
                                    
                                }
                                
                                if(mapVars[1][22] <= 0){
                                    
                                    var proj =15;
                                    var dz = random(-0.1,0.1);
                                    
                                    var dud=false;
                                    var dudNum=0;
                                    
                                    //Find duds
                                    {
                                        
                                        //Variables for search
                                        var d=0;
                                        var search=true;
                                        //Search
                                        while(search){
                                            
                                            if(projectiles.dud[d]){
                                                
                                                dudNum=d;
                                                
                                                dud=true;
                                                
                                                search=false;
                                                
                                            }
                                            
                                            d++;
                                            
                                            if(d>=projectiles.dud.length-1){
                                                
                                                search=false;
                                                
                                            }
                                            
                                        }
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    var angleShot=enemies.angle[ai]+random(-projectileConstants[proj].deviation,projectileConstants[proj].deviation);
                                    var vAngleShot=0+random(-projectileConstants[proj].deviation,projectileConstants[proj].deviation);
                                    
                                    var realAngle = angleShot - 90;
                                    
                                    var x = enemies.x[ai];
                                    var y = enemies.y[ai];
                                    var z = enemies.verticalPos[ai] + enemyConstants[enemies.type[ai]].height/2 - 450;
                                    
                                    var vx = (sin(angleShot)*projectileConstants[proj].speed)*cos(vAngleShot);
                                    var vy = (cos(angleShot)*-projectileConstants[proj].speed)*cos(vAngleShot);
                                    var vz = dz*projectileConstants[proj].speed;
                                    
                                    //Create Projectile
                                    if(!dud){
                                        
                                        projectiles.x.push(x);
                                        projectiles.y.push(y);
                                        
                                        projectiles.speedX.push(vx);
                                        projectiles.speedY.push(vy);
                                        
                                        projectiles.vix.push(vx);
                                        projectiles.viy.push(vy);
                                        projectiles.viz.push(vz);
                                        
                                        projectiles.animation.push(0);
                                        
                                        projectiles.animationFrame.push(0);
                                        
                                        projectiles.verticalPos.push(z);
                                        //projectiles.verticalPos.push(0); 
                                        
                                        projectiles.verticalVelocity.push(vz);
                                        
                                        projectiles.fuse.push(0);
                                        
                                        projectiles.angle.push(angleShot);
                                        
                                        projectiles.type.push(proj);
                                        
                                        projectiles.friendly.push(false);
                                        
                                        projectiles.dud.push(false);
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    else{
                                        
                                        projectiles.x[dudNum]=x;
                                        projectiles.y[dudNum]=y;
                                        
                                        projectiles.speedX[dudNum]=vx;
                                        projectiles.speedY[dudNum]=vy;
                                        
                                        projectiles.vix[dudNum]=vx;
                                        projectiles.viy[dudNum]=vy;
                                        projectiles.viz[dudNum]=vz;
                                        
                                        projectiles.animation[dudNum]=0;
                                        
                                        projectiles.animationFrame[dudNum]=0;
                                        projectiles.verticalPos[dudNum]=z;
                                        
                                        projectiles.verticalVelocity[dudNum]=vz;
                                        
                                        projectiles.fuse[dudNum]=0;
                                        
                                        projectiles.angle[dudNum]=angleShot;
                                        
                                        projectiles.type[dudNum]=proj;
                                        
                                        projectiles.friendly[dudNum]=false;
                                        
                                        projectiles.dud[dudNum]=false;
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    mapVars[1][22] = projCooldown;
                                    
                                    //Make particles
                                    for(var p = 0; p <= random(1,2); p++){
                                        
                                        var xSpeed = random(-2,2);
                                        var ySpeed = random(-2,2);
                                        
                                        createParticle(x+random(-7,7),y+random(-7,7),xSpeed,ySpeed,z,random(-10,10),19);
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    mapVars[1][22] -= game.deltaF;
                                    
                                }
                                
                                break;
                            
                        } 
                        
                        //Swap to idle
                        
                        //Mode timer
                        mapVars[1][1] -= game.deltaF;
                        
                        if(mapVars[1][1]<=0){
                            
                            mapVars[1][0] = 'idle';
                            
                            mapVars[1][1] = idleTime;
                            
                            if(!leftHandDead || !rightHandDead){
                                
                                if(mapVars[1][15] === 'right'){
                                    
                                    mapVars[1][15] = 'left';
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    mapVars[1][15] = 'right';
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            enemies.animation[mapVars[1][7]]=0;
                            enemies.animation[mapVars[1][11]]=0;
                            
                            enemies.animation[hi] = 0;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                
                case 'dead':
                    
                    mapVars[1][8]=317;
                    mapVars[1][9]=466;
                    mapVars[1][10]=-5000;
                    enemies.animation[li] = 2;
                    
                    mapVars[1][12]=400;
                    mapVars[1][13]=466;
                    mapVars[1][14]=-5000;
                    enemies.animation[ri] = 2;
                    
                    break;
                
            }
            
            //Move toward target positions
            if(mapVars[1][0] !== 'init'){
                
                //Plaintext target vars
                var hx = mapVars[1][4];
                var hy = mapVars[1][5];
                var hz = mapVars[1][6];
                //var hd = dist(hx,hy,hz/20,enemies.x[hi],enemies.y[hi],enemies.verticalPos[hi]/20);
                
                var lx = mapVars[1][8];
                var ly = mapVars[1][9];
                var lz = mapVars[1][10];
                //var ld = dist(lx,ly,lz/20,enemies.x[li],enemies.y[li],enemies.verticalPos[li]/20);
                
                var rx = mapVars[1][12];
                var ry = mapVars[1][13];
                var rz = mapVars[1][14];
                //var rd = dist(rx,ry,rz/20,enemies.x[ri],enemies.y[ri],enemies.verticalPos[ri]/20);
                
                var ia = [hi,li,ri];
                var xa = [hx,lx,rx];
                var ya = [hy,ly,ry];
                var za = [hz,lz,rz];
                
                //Loop through the head and hands
                for(var i = 0; i <= 2; i++){
                    
                    var ti = ia[i];
                    var tx = xa[i];
                    var ty = ya[i];
                    var tz = za[i];
                    
                    if((mapVars[1][0] !== 'attack' || ti !== ai) || mapVars[1][16] === 2){
                        
                        var acc = 0.01;
                        
                        //Move toward target 
                        enemies.speedX[ti] += acc*(tx-enemies.x[ti]);
                        enemies.speedY[ti] += acc*(ty-enemies.y[ti]);
                        enemies.vertSpeed[ti] += acc*((tz-enemies.verticalPos[ti]));
                        
                        //Slow down
                        enemies.speedX[ti] *= 0.9;
                        enemies.speedY[ti] *= 0.9;
                        enemies.vertSpeed[ti] *= 0.9;
                        
                        //Cap the ends of the speed
                        var minSpeed = 0.1;
                        var maxSpeed = 20;
                        
                        var tSpeed = dist(0,0,0,enemies.speedX[ti],enemies.speedY[ti],enemies.vertSpeed[ti]);
                        
                        if(tSpeed > maxSpeed){
