                            enemies.speedX[ti] = (enemies.speedX[ti]/tSpeed)*maxSpeed;
                            enemies.speedY[ti] = (enemies.speedY[ti]/tSpeed)*maxSpeed;
                            enemies.vertSpeed[ti] = (enemies.vertSpeed[ti]/tSpeed)*maxSpeed;
                            
                        }
                        
                        /*
                        if(tSpeed < minSpeed){
                            
                            enemies.speedX[ti] = (enemies.speedX[ti]/tSpeed)*minSpeed;
                            enemies.speedY[ti] = (enemies.speedY[ti]/tSpeed)*minSpeed;
                            enemies.vertSpeed[ti] = (enemies.vertSpeed[ti]/tSpeed)*minSpeed*20;
                            
                        }
                        */
                        
                        //Stop once youre about to cross over the limit
                        var hMinX = min(enemies.x[ti],enemies.x[ti]+enemies.speedX[ti]);
                        var hMaxX = max(enemies.x[ti],enemies.x[ti]+enemies.speedX[ti]);
                        
                        if(constrain(tx,hMinX,hMaxX) === tx && tSpeed < 1){
                            
                            enemies.x[ti] = tx;
                            enemies.speedX[ti] = 0;
                            
                        }
                        
                        var hMinY = min(enemies.y[ti],enemies.y[ti]+enemies.speedY[ti]);
                        var hMaxY = max(enemies.y[ti],enemies.y[ti]+enemies.speedY[ti]);
                        
                        if(constrain(ty,hMinY,hMaxY) === ty && tSpeed < 1){
                            
                            enemies.y[ti] = ty;
                            enemies.speedY[ti] = 0;
                            
                        }
                        
                        var hMinZ = min(enemies.verticalPos[ti],enemies.verticalPos[ti]+enemies.vertSpeed[ti]);
                        var hMaxZ = max(enemies.verticalPos[ti],enemies.verticalPos[ti]+enemies.vertSpeed[ti]);
                        
                        if(constrain(tz,hMinZ,hMaxZ) === tz && tSpeed < 1){
                            
                            enemies.verticalPos[ti] = tz;
                            enemies.vertSpeed[ti] = 0;
                            
                        }
                        
                        //Rotate to face player
                        if(mapVars[1][0] !== 'attack' || (mapVars[1][16] === 2 && ti ===ai)){
                            
                            var angle = enemies.angle[ti];
                            
                            var dAngle = -1*(atan2(player.x-enemies.x[i],player.y-enemies.y[i]));
                            
                            var dif = angle-dAngle;
                            
                            var shortDistance = (dif + 180) % 360;
                            
                            shortDistance -= 180; 
                            
                            if(shortDistance < 0){
                                
                                enemies.angle[ti]-=0.5;
                                
                            }
                            
                            else{
                                
                                enemies.angle[ti]+=0.5;
                                
                            }
                            
                            if(enemies.angle[ti] < 0){
                                
                                enemies.angle[ti] = 360+enemies.angle[ti];
                                
                            }
                            
                            if(enemies.angle[ti] >360){
                                
                                enemies.angle[ti] = enemies.angle[ti]-360;
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            if(enemies.animation[hi] === 2){
                
                enemies.animationTime[hi] -= game.deltaF;
                
                if(enemies.animationTime[hi] < 0){
                    
                    enemies.animation[hi] = 0;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            //Health bar Logic
            if(mapVars[1][0] !== 'intro' || mapVars[1][1] < 30){
                
                //Set some constants
                var health = enemies.health[hi];
                
                var maxHealth = enemyConstants[loadedMap.enemies.type[hi]].health;
                
                var healthLevel = constrain(health/maxHealth,0,1);
                
                //mapVars[1][23] - level | mapVars[1][24] - last health | mapVars[1][25] - timer | mapVars[1][26] - target
                
                //Lag bar logic
                
                //If he takes damage
                if(healthLevel !== mapVars[1][24]){
                    
                    //Check to see if we can move the target yet
                    if(mapVars[1][25]>0 || mapVars[1][26] === mapVars[1][23]){
                        
                        mapVars[1][26] = healthLevel;
                        
                        mapVars[1][25] = 20;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //Reduce the value if we have waited long enough and there is a target below the value
                if(mapVars[1][25] <= 0){
                    
                    //Move down to reach target
                    if(mapVars[1][23]>mapVars[1][26]){
                        
                        mapVars[1][23]-=0.005;
                        
                        mapVars[1][23] = constrain(mapVars[1][23],mapVars[1][26],1);
                        
                    }
                    
                    //Check if target is above actual health when we have reached it
                    else if(mapVars[1][23] > healthLevel){
                        
                        mapVars[1][26] = healthLevel;
                        
                        mapVars[1][25] = 20;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //Decrease timer otherwise
                else{
                    
                    mapVars[1][25]--;
                    
                }
                
                //Set the last health level
                mapVars[1][24] = healthLevel;
                
                //Set vars for drawing
                var margin = width/120;
                
                var offSet = 0;
                
                var transparency = 255;
                
                if(mapVars[1][0] === 'intro'){
                    
                    var percent = constrain((mapVars[1][1]/30),0,1);
                    
                    offSet = percent*height/10;
                    
                }
                
                if(mapVars[1][0] === 'dead'){
                    
                    transparency = 255 + mapVars[1][1];
                    
                    mapVars[1][1]-=2;
                    
                }
                
                //Background
                fill(54, 48, 48,transparency);
                
                strokeWeight(2);
                
                stroke(8, 8, 8,transparency);
                
                rect(width/6 - margin,height/20 - margin-offSet,(width*(2/3)) + margin*2,height/25 + margin*2,5);
                
                fill(8, 8, 8,transparency);
                
                rect(width/6,height/20-offSet,(width*(2/3)),height/25);
                
                //Lag Bar
                fill(238, 220, 91,transparency);
                
                rect(width/6,height/20-offSet,(width*(2/3))*mapVars[1][23],height/25);
                
                //Main Bar
                fill(212, 21, 21,transparency);
                
                rect(width/6,height/20-offSet,(width*(2/3))*healthLevel,height/25);
                
                //Name
                fill(235, 235, 235,transparency);
                
                textAlign(LEFT,CENTER);
                
                textFont(createFont("serif Bold"));
                
                textSize(15);
                
                text("The Skeleton King", width/6,height/20-offSet-height/50);
                
                textFont(createFont("sans-serif"));
                
                textAlign(LEFT,BASELINE);
                
            }
            
            break;
        
    }
    
    //Map specific lighting
    for(var y = 0; y <= loadedMap.dynamicLighting.length-1; y++){
        
        for(var x = 0; x <= loadedMap.dynamicLighting[y].length-1;x++){
            
            //Which tile value
            switch(loadedMap.dynamicLighting[y][x]){
                
                //1
                case 1:
                    
                    //Which map 
                    switch(loadedMap.name){
                        
                        case 'mines_1':
                        case 'mines_2':
                        case 'mines_3':
                        case 'mines_4':
                            
                            if(game.globalTimer%10 <5){
                                
                                tempMap.lighting[y][x]/=1.015;
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                    
                //2
                case 2:
                    
                    //Which map 
                    switch(loadedMap.name){
                        
                        case 'mines_1':
                        case 'mines_3':
                            
                            if(tempMap.floorHeight[y][x]>600){
                                
                                tempMap.lighting[y][x]=0.1;
                                
                            }
                            
                            else if(tempMap.floorHeight[y][x]>300){
                                
                                tempMap.lighting[y][x]*=(1-(tempMap.floorHeight[y][x]-300)/300);
                                
                                tempMap.lighting[y][x]=constrain(tempMap.lighting[y][x],0.2,1);
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                        case 'mines_4':
                            
                            if(tempMap.floorHeight[y][x]>300){
                                
                                tempMap.lighting[y][x]*=(600-tempMap.floorHeight[y][x])/300;
                                
                                //tempMap.lighting[y][x]=constrain(tempMap.lighting[y][x],0.2,1);
                                
                            }
                            
                            if(tempMap.floorHeight[y][x]>600){
                                
                                tempMap.lighting[y][x]=0;
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                    }
                    
                break;
                
                case 3:
                    
                    //Which map 
                    switch(loadedMap.name){
                        
                        
                        case 'mines_4':
                            
                            if(mapVars[1][0]==='intro' && player.height>1600){
                                
                                tempMap.lighting[y][x]*=1-((player.height-1600)/(8400));
                                
                            }
                            
                        break;
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
            }
            
        }
        
    }
    
};


//How things move and interact
var physics=function(){
    
    //Reset number of pathfinding calls
    n = 0;
    
    if(!game.semiPause){
        
        //Enemy AI
        
        for(var i = 0; i <= enemies.x.length-1; i++){
            
            if(enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].moveType !== 0){
                
                var inZone = false;
                
                var y = floor(enemies.y[i]/20);
                var x = floor(enemies.x[i]/20);
                
                var zone = loadedMap.loadZone[y][x];
                var overlap = loadedMap.loadOverlap[y][x];
                
                inZone = (player.zones[0] === zone || player.zones[0] === overlap) || (player.zones[1] === zone || player.zones[1] === overlap);
                
                if(!enemies.dud[i] && inZone){
                    
                    //What type of enemy
                    switch(enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].moveType){
                        
                        //Inanimate Prop
                        case 0:
                            
                            
                            
                            break;
                            
                        //Basic movement
                        case 1:
                            
                            //Moving enemy
                            if(enemies.moving[i]){
                                
                                //Wait 
                                enemies.moveTime[i]--;
                                
                                //Stop moving
                                if(enemies.moveTime[i]<=0 || enemies.distance[i]<enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].range){
                                    
                                    //Stop the enemy
                                    enemies.speedX[i]=0;
                                    enemies.speedY[i]=0;
                                    
                                    //Set new angle
                                    if(enemies.distance[i]>enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].detectRange){
                                        
                                        enemies.angle[i]=random(0,360);
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    //When in range
                                    else{
                                        
                                        enemies.angle[i]=-1*(atan2(player.x-enemies.x[i],player.y-enemies.y[i])-180);
                                        
                                    } 
                                    
                                    //Reset move time
                                    enemies.moveTime[i]=enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].moveWaitAverage+random(-enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].moveWaitAverage*enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].waitWeight,enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].moveWaitAverage*enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].waitWeight);
                                    
                                    //Set enemies to not move
                                    enemies.moving[i]=false;
                                    
                                    //Reduce cooldown
                                    enemies.cooldown[i]-=game.deltaF;
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    enemies.speedX[i]=sin(enemies.angle[i])*enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].speed;
                                    enemies.speedY[i]=-cos(enemies.angle[i])*enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].speed;
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            //Not moving enemy
                            else{
                                
                                //Wait 
                                enemies.moveTime[i]-=game.deltaF;
                                
                                //Stop moving
                                if(enemies.moveTime[i]<=0){
                                    
                                    //Move the enemy
                                    //When out of player's range
                                    enemies.speedX[i]=sin(enemies.angle[i])*enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].speed;
                                    enemies.speedY[i]=-cos(enemies.angle[i])*enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].speed;
                                    
                                    //Reset move time
                                    enemies.moveTime[i]=enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].moveWaitAverage+random(-enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].moveWaitAverage*enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].waitWeight,enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].moveWaitAverage*enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].waitWeight);
                                    
                                    //Make enemy moving
                                    enemies.moving[i]=true;
                                    
                                    enemies.animation[i] = 1;
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                            
                            //Attack Player
                            if(enemies.distance[i]<=enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].range){
                                
                                
                                //Attack and reset cooldown
                                if(enemies.cooldown[i]<=0){
                                    
                                    hurt(0,0,enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].damage,0,0);
                                    
                                    enemies.cooldown[i]=enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].attackWait;
                                    
                                }
                                
                                else{
                                    
                                    enemies.cooldown[i]-=game.deltaF;
                                    
                                }
                                
                                
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                            
                        //Search for player
                        case 2:
                            
                            //Difference in heights for hitscan
                            var difZ = ((player.height+player.floorHeight/2+300)-(enemies.verticalPos[i]+enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].height/2))/20;
                            
                            //dz value for hitscan
                            var dz = difZ/dist(enemies.x[i],enemies.y[i],player.x,player.y);
                            
                            //Angle between player and enemy
                            var da = -1*(atan2(player.x-enemies.x[i],player.y-enemies.y[i])-180);
                            
                            //Check if enemy can see player
                            var r = hitScan(da,enemies.x[i],enemies.y[i],dz,'none',(enemies.verticalPos[i]+enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].height/2)-300,Infinity);
                            
                            //Bool for if the enemy can see the player
                            var playerInLine = dist(enemies.x[i],enemies.y[i],r.x,r.y) >= enemies.distance[i];
                            
                            //createParticle(r.x,r.y,0,0,r.z,0,1);
                            
                            //Check from the top of the enemy again
                            if(!playerInLine){
                                
                                //Difference in heights for hitscan
                                difZ = ((player.height+300)-(enemies.verticalPos[i]+enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].height))/20;
                                
                                //dz value for hitscan
                                dz = difZ/dist(enemies.x[i],enemies.y[i],player.x,player.y);
                                
                                //Check if enemy can see player
                                r = hitScan(da,enemies.x[i],enemies.y[i],dz,'none',(enemies.verticalPos[i]+enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].height)-300,Infinity);
                                
                                //Bool for if the enemy can see the player
                                playerInLine = dist(enemies.x[i],enemies.y[i],r.x,r.y) >= enemies.distance[i];
                                
                                //createParticle(r.x,r.y,0,0,r.z,0,1);
                                
                            }
                            
                            
                            
                            //calculate difference in angle between enemy and player and where it is looking
                            var a = da - enemies.angle[i];
                            
                            a = abs((a+180)%360 -180);
                            
                            //println(enemies.state[i]);
                            //var states = ['alert','ambush','idle','hunting','attacking'];
                            switch(enemies.state[i]){
                                
                                //Start only able to see player if they are in front of you
                                case 'alert':
                                    
                                    if(playerInLine && a <= enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].fov){
                                        
                                        enemies.state[i] = 'attacking';
                                        
                                        enemies.angle[i] = da;
                                        
                                        enemies.moving[i] = false;
                                        
                                        enemies.moveTime[i] = 20;
                                        
                                        enemies.cooldown[i] = enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].attackWait;
                                        
                                        enemies.lastSeen[i] = [player.x,player.y,-1*(atan2(player.speedX,player.speedY)-180)];
                                        
                                        enemies.animation[i] = 0;
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    //Idle Animation
                                    enemies.animationTime[i]-=game.deltaF;
                                    
                                    if(enemies.animationTime[i]<= 0){
                                        
                                        if(enemies.animationFrame[i] < objectConstants[enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].object].texture[enemies.animation[i]].length-1){
                                            
                                            enemies.animationFrame[i]++;
                                            
                                        }
                                        
                                        else{
                                            
                                            enemies.animationFrame[i]=0;
                                            
                                        }
                                        
                                        enemies.animationTime[i] = enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].animationTimes[0];
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    break;
                                
                                //Always track the player until they break line of sight if you're attacking
                                case 'attacking':
                                    
                                    //println(dzt);
                                    
                                    if(playerInLine){
                                        
                                        enemies.moveTime[i]--;
                                        
                                        enemies.lastSeen[i] = [player.x,player.y,-1*(atan2(player.speedX,player.speedY)-180)];
                                        
                                        //Move toward player
                                        if(enemies.moving[i]){
                                            
                                            //Stop to attack if possible
                                            if(enemies.moveTime[i] <= 0 || enemies.distance[i] <= enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].range){
                                                
                                                enemies.moveTime[i]=enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].moveWaitAverage+random(-enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].moveWaitAverage*enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].waitWeight,enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].moveWaitAverage*enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].waitWeight);
                                                
                                                enemies.cooldown[i] = enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].attackWait;
                                                
                                                enemies.moving[i] = false;
                                                
                                                //Stop the enemy
                                                enemies.speedX[i]=0;
                                                enemies.speedY[i]=0;
                                                
                                                if(enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].flying){
                                                    
                                                    enemies.vertSpeed[i] = 0;
                                                    
                                                }
                                                
                                                enemies.animation[i] = 0;
                                                
                                                enemies.animationFrame[i] = 0;
                                                
                                                enemies.animationTime[i] = enemies.cooldown[i] - enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].animationTimes[2];
                                                
                                            }
                                            
                                            else{
                                                
                                                //Move in direction
                                                enemies.speedX[i]=sin(enemies.angle[i])*enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].speed;
                                                enemies.speedY[i]=-cos(enemies.angle[i])*enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].speed;
                                                
                                                if(enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].flying){
                                                    
                                                    //enemies.vertSpeed[i] = dzt *enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].speed*20; 
                                                    
                                                }
                                                
                                                //Walk Animation
                                                enemies.animation[i] = 1;
                                                
                                                enemies.animationTime[i]-=game.deltaF;
                                                
                                                if(enemies.animationTime[i]<= 0){
                                                    
                                                    if(enemies.animationFrame[i] < objectConstants[enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].object].texture[enemies.animation[i]].length-1){
                                                        
                                                        enemies.animationFrame[i]++;
                                                        
                                                    }
                                                    
                                                    else{
                                                        
                                                        enemies.animationFrame[i]=0;
                                                        
                                                    }
                                                    
                                                    enemies.animationTime[i] = enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].animationTimes[1];
                                                    
                                                }
                                                
                                            }
                                            
                                        }
                                        
                                        //Attack if possible
                                        else{
                                            
                                            enemies.angle[i] = da;
                                            
                                            //Start moving again
                                            if(enemies.moveTime[i] <= 0 && enemies.distance[i] >= enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].range){
                                                
                                                enemies.moveTime[i]=enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].moveWaitAverage+random(-enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].moveWaitAverage*enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].waitWeight,enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].moveWaitAverage*enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].waitWeight);
                                                
                                                enemies.moving[i] = true;
                                                
                                                enemies.angle[i] = da + random(-enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].directness,enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].directness);
                                                
                                                if(enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].flying){
                                                    
                                                    var dzt = (dz + sin(random(-enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].directness/2,enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].directness)));
                                                    
                                                    enemies.vertSpeed[i] = dzt *enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].speed*10; 
                                                    
                                                }
                                                
                                                enemies.animation[i] = 1;
                                                
                                                enemies.animationFrame[i] = 0;
                                                
                                                enemies.animationTime[i] = enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].animationTimes[1];
                                                
                                            }
                                            
                                            //Attack
                                            else{
                                                
                                                switch(enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].attackType){
                                                    
                                                    case 'range':
                                                        
                                                        //Attack and reset cooldown
                                                        if(enemies.cooldown[i]<=0){
                                                            
                                                            var proj = enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].projectile;
                                                            
                                                            for(var s = 0; s <= projectileConstants[proj].shots-1; s++){
                                                                
                                                                var dud=false;
                                                                var dudNum=0;
                                                                
                                                                //Find duds
                                                                {
                                                                    
                                                                    //Variables for search
                                                                    var d=0;
                                                                    var search=true;
                                                                    //Search
                                                                    while(search){
                                                                        
                                                                        if(projectiles.dud[d]){
                                                                            
                                                                            dudNum=d;
                                                                            
                                                                            dud=true;
                                                                            
                                                                            search=false;
                                                                            
                                                                        }
                                                                        
                                                                        d++;
                                                                        
                                                                        if(d>=projectiles.dud.length-1){
                                                                            
                                                                            search=false;
                                                                            
                                                                        }
                                                                        
                                                                    }
                                                                    
                                                                }
                                                                
                                                                var angleShot=enemies.angle[i]+random(-projectileConstants[proj].deviation,projectileConstants[proj].deviation);
                                                                var vAngleShot=0+random(-projectileConstants[proj].deviation,projectileConstants[proj].deviation);
                                                                
                                                                var realAngle = angleShot - 90;
                                                                
                                                                var x = enemies.x[i];
                                                                var y = enemies.y[i];
                                                                var z = enemies.verticalPos[i] + enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].height/2 - 300;
                                                                
                                                                switch(enemies.type[i]){
                                                                    
                                                                    case 3:
                                                                        
                                                                        if(s === 0){
                                                                            
                                                                            x+= 5 * cos(angleShot);
                                                                            y+= 5 * sin(angleShot);
                                                                            
                                                                        }
                                                                        
                                                                        else{
                                                                            
                                                                            x-= 5 * cos(angleShot);
                                                                            y-= 5 * sin(angleShot);
                                                                            
                                                                        }
                                                                        
                                                                        break;
                                                                    
                                                                    case 5:
                                                                        
                                                                        z -= enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].height/4;
                                                                        
                                                                        angleShot += (s-1)*15;
                                                                        
                                                                        break;
                                                                    
                                                                    case 6:
                                                                        
                                                                        x+= 5 * cos(angleShot);
                                                                        y+= 5 * sin(angleShot);
                                                                        
                                                                        z += enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].height/3;
                                                                        
                                                                        break;
                                                                    
                                                                }
                                                                
                                                                var vx = (sin(angleShot)*projectileConstants[proj].speed)*cos(vAngleShot);
                                                                var vy = (cos(angleShot)*-projectileConstants[proj].speed)*cos(vAngleShot);
                                                                var vz = dz*projectileConstants[proj].speed;
                                                                
                                                                //Create Projectile
                                                                if(!dud){
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.x.push(x);
                                                                    projectiles.y.push(y);
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.speedX.push(vx);
                                                                    projectiles.speedY.push(vy);
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.vix.push(vx);
                                                                    projectiles.viy.push(vy);
                                                                    projectiles.viz.push(vz);
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.animation.push(0);
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.animationFrame.push(0);
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.verticalPos.push(z);
                                                                    //projectiles.verticalPos.push(0); 
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.verticalVelocity.push(vz);
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.fuse.push(0);
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.angle.push(angleShot);
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.type.push(enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].projectile);
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.friendly.push(false);
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.dud.push(false);
                                                                    
                                                                }
                                                                
                                                                else{
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.x[dudNum]=x;
                                                                    projectiles.y[dudNum]=y;
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.speedX[dudNum]=vx;
                                                                    projectiles.speedY[dudNum]=vy;
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.vix[dudNum]=vx;
                                                                    projectiles.viy[dudNum]=vy;
                                                                    projectiles.viz[dudNum]=vz;
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.animation[dudNum]=0;
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.animationFrame[dudNum]=0;
                                                                    projectiles.verticalPos[dudNum]=z;
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.verticalVelocity[dudNum]=vz;
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.fuse[dudNum]=0;
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.angle[dudNum]=angleShot;
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.type[dudNum]=enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].projectile;
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.friendly[dudNum]=false;
                                                                    
                                                                    projectiles.dud[dudNum]=false;
                                                                    
                                                                }
                                                                
                                                                
                                                            }
                                                            
                                                            enemies.cooldown[i]=enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].attackWait; 
                                                            
                                                            //Reset attack animation
                                                            enemies.animationTime[i] = enemies.cooldown[i] - enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].animationTimes[2];
                                                            
                                                            enemies.animationFrame[i] = 0;
                                                            
                                                            enemies.animation[i] = 0;
                                                            
                                                        }
                                                        
                                                        else{
                                                            
                                                            enemies.cooldown[i]-=game.deltaF;
                                                            
                                                            if(enemies.cooldown[i] <= enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].attackWait/2 && enemies.cooldown[i]<=enemies.moveTime[i]){
                                                                
                                                                enemies.animation[i] = 2;
                                                                
                                                            }
                                                            
                                                            else{
                                                                
                                                                enemies.animation[i] = 0; 
                                                                
                                                            }
                                                            
                                                            //Animate attack
                                                            if(enemies.animationTime[i]<=0){
                                                                
                                                                enemies.animationFrame[i] = 1;
                                                                
                                                            }
                                                            
                                                            enemies.animationTime[i]-=game.deltaF;
                                                            
                                                        }
                                                        
                                                        break;
                                                    
                                                    case 'melee':
                                                        
                                                        //Melee enemies don't pause to shoot
                                                        if(enemies.distance[i] > enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].range){
                                                            
                                                            enemies.moveTime[i]=0;
                                                            
                                                        }
                                                        
                                                        else{
                                                            
                                                            //Attack and reset cooldown
                                                            if(enemies.cooldown[i]<=0){
                                                                
                                                                enemies.cooldown[i]=enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].attackWait;
                                                                
                                                                hurt(0,0,enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].damage,0,0,0);
                                                                
                                                                //Reset attack animation
                                                                enemies.animationTime[i] = enemies.cooldown[i] - enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].animationTimes[2];
                                                                
                                                                enemies.animationFrame[i] = 0;
                                                                
                                                                enemies.animation[i] = 0;
                                                                
                                                            }
                                                            
                                                            else{
                                                                
                                                                enemies.cooldown[i]-=game.deltaF;
                                                                
                                                                if(enemies.cooldown[i] <= enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].attackWait/2){
                                                                    
                                                                    enemies.animation[i] = 2;
                                                                    
                                                                }
                                                                
                                                                else{
                                                                    
                                                                    enemies.animation[i] = 0; 
                                                                    
                                                                }
                                                                
                                                                //Animate attack
                                                                if(enemies.animationTime[i]<=0){
                                                                    
                                                                    enemies.animationFrame[i] = 1;
                                                                    
                                                                }
                                                                
                                                                enemies.animationTime[i]-=game.deltaF;
                                                                
                                                            }
                                                            
                                                        }
                                                        
                                                        break;
                                                    
                                                }
                                                
                                            }
                                            
                                        } 
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    else{
                                        
                                        enemies.state[i] = 'hunting';
                                        
                                        enemies.target[i] = enemies.lastSeen[i].slice();
                                        
                                        enemies.animation[i] = 1;
                                        
                                        enemies.cooldown[i] = enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].attackWait;
                                    }
                                    
                                    break;
                                
                                //Find player if they've left line of sight (extra fov is granted)
                                case 'hunting':
                                    
                                    //They can see a bit better when they're searching
                                    if(playerInLine && a <= enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].fov *1.5){
                                        
                                        enemies.state[i] = 'attacking';
                                        
                                        enemies.angle[i] = da;
                                        
                                        enemies.cooldown[i] = enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].attackWait/2;
                                        
                                        enemies.path[i] = [];
                                        
                                        enemies.next[i] = [];
                                        
                                        enemies.target[i] = [];
                                        
                                        enemies.animation[i] = 0;
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    else{
                                        
                                        //Move to where we last saw the player
                                        if(enemies.target[i].length>0){
                                            
                                            enemies.target[i][0] = floor(enemies.target[i][0]/20)*20+10;
                                            enemies.target[i][1] = floor(enemies.target[i][1]/20)*20+10;
                                            
                                            //enemies.angle[i] = -1*(atan2(enemies.target[i][0]-enemies.x[i],enemies.target[i][1]-enemies.y[i])-180);
                                            
                                            //Distance from target
                                            var td = dist(enemies.x[i],enemies.y[i],enemies.target[i][0],enemies.target[i][1]);
                                            
                                            //Stop searching once you're there
                                            if(td<3){
                                                
                                                enemies.speedX[i]=0;
                                                enemies.speedY[i]=0;
                                                
                                                enemies.angle[i] = enemies.target[i][2];
                                                
                                                enemies.target[i] = [];
                                                
                                                enemies.animation[i] = 0;
                                                
                                            }
                                            
                                            //How we walk to the target
                                            else{
                                                
                                                //Make sure you're in walking animation
                                                enemies.animation[i] = 1;
                                                
                                                //Check if we can see the target
                                                var th = hitScan(-1*(atan2(enemies.target[i][0]-enemies.x[i],enemies.target[i][1]-enemies.y[i])-180),enemies.x[i],enemies.y[i],0,'none',enemies.verticalPos[i]+(enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].height/3)-300,Infinity);
                                                
                                                //enemies.next[i]=[];
                                                
                                                var nextStepInPath = [];
                                                
                                                //If we can't see the target, pathfind to it
                                                if(dist(enemies.x[i],enemies.y[i],th.x,th.y)<td){
                                                    
                                                    var finalStep = enemies.path[i][0];
                                                    
                                                    if((enemies.path[i].length <= 1 || (floor(enemies.target[i][0]/20) !== finalStep[0] || floor(enemies.target[i][1]/20) !== finalStep[1])) && n<feeler.maxLoops){
                                                        
                                                        enemies.path[i] = pathFinding(floor(enemies.x[i]/20),floor(enemies.y[i]/20),floor(enemies.target[i][0]/20),floor(enemies.target[i][1]/20));
                                                        
                                                        //Remove target if we can't pathfind to it 
                                                        if(removeTarget){
                                                            
                                                            enemies.target[i] = [];
                                                            
                                                        }
                                                        
                                                        if(enemies.path[i].length > 0){
                                                            
                                                            enemies.next[i]=[enemies.path[i].length-1,enemies.path[i].length-2];
                                                            
                                                        }
                                                        
                                                    }
                                                    
                                                    if(enemies.path[i].length > 0){
                                                        
                                                        for(var s = enemies.path[i].length-1; s>0; s--){
                                                            
                                                            if(floor(enemies.x[i]/20) === enemies.path[i][s][0] && floor(enemies.y[i]/20) === enemies.path[i][s][1]){
                                                                
                                                                if(enemies.next[i][0] === enemies.path[i].length-1){
                                                                    
                                                                    enemies.next[i][0] = enemies.path[i].length-2;
                                                                    
                                                                }
                                                                
                                                                enemies.path[i].splice(enemies.path[i].length-1,s);
                                                                
                                                                break;
                                                                
                                                            }
                                                            
                                                        }
                                                        
                                                    }
                                                    
                                                    if(enemies.path[i].length-1 > 1){
                                                        
                                                        enemies.next[i][0]=constrain(enemies.next[i][0],0,enemies.path[i].length-1);
                                                        enemies.next[i][1]=constrain(enemies.next[i][1],0,enemies.path[i].length-2);
                                                        
                                                        var start = enemies.next[i][0];
                                                        var end = enemies.next[i][1];
                                                        
                                                        //+(enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].height/2)
                                                        var sh = hitScan(-1*(atan2((enemies.path[i][end][0]*20+10)-(enemies.path[i][start][0]*20+10),(enemies.path[i][end][1]*20+10)-(enemies.path[i][start][1]*20+10))-180),enemies.path[i][start][0]*20+10,enemies.path[i][start][1]*20+10,0,'none',primaryOrSecondary(enemies.path[i][start][0]*20+10,enemies.path[i][start][1]*20+10,'floor')+(enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].height/3)-300,Infinity);
                                                        
                                                        var ds = dist(enemies.path[i][start][0]*20+10,enemies.path[i][start][1]*20+10,enemies.path[i][end][0]*20+10,enemies.path[i][end][1]*20+10);
                                                        
                                                        if((dist(enemies.path[i][start][0]*20+10,enemies.path[i][start][1]*20+10,sh.x,sh.y)<ds || end === 0) && start !== end){
                                                            
                                                            if(end < enemies.path[i].length-1){
                                                                
                                                                if(end===0){
                                                                    
                                                                    enemies.path[i].splice(end+1,(start-end-1));
                                                                    
                                                                }
                                                                
                                                                else{
                                                                    
                                                                    enemies.path[i].splice(end+2,(start-end-2));
                                                                    
                                                                }
                                                                
                                                                enemies.next[i][0] = end;
                                                                
                                                                enemies.next[i][1]--;
                                                                
                                                                //enemies.next[i] = [enemies.path[i][s+1][0]*20+10,enemies.path[i][s+1][1]*20+10];
                                                                
                                                                //nextStepInPath = [enemies.path[i][s+1][0]*20+10,enemies.path[i][s+1][1]*20+10];
                                                                
                                                            }
                                                            
                                                            else{
                                                                
                                                                enemies.path[i]=[];
                                                                
                                                            }
                                                            
                                                        }
                                                        
                                                        else if(start !== end){
                                                            
                                                            enemies.next[i][1]--;
                                                            
                                                        }
                                                        
                                                    }
                                                    
                                                    if(enemies.path[i].length > 0){
                                                        
                                                        var last = enemies.path[i].length-1;
                                                        
                                                        nextStepInPath = [enemies.path[i][last][0]*20+10,enemies.path[i][last][1]*20+10];
                                                        
                                                    }
                                                    
                                                }
                                                
                                                //If we can, walk towards it
                                                else{
                                                    
                                                    enemies.path[i] = [];
                                                    
                                                    //enemies.next[i] = [enemies.target[i][0],enemies.target[i][1]];
                                                    nextStepInPath = [enemies.target[i][0],enemies.target[i][1]];
                                                    
                                                }
                                                
                                                if(nextStepInPath.length > 0){
                                                    
                                                    //enemies.next[i][0] = floor(enemies.next[i][0]/20)*20+10;
                                                    //enemies.next[i][1] = floor(enemies.next[i][1]/20)*20+10;
                                                    
                                                    nextStepInPath[0] = floor(nextStepInPath[0]/20)*20+10;
                                                    nextStepInPath[1] = floor(nextStepInPath[1]/20)*20+10;
                                                    
                                                    if(dist(nextStepInPath[0],nextStepInPath[1],enemies.x[i],enemies.y[i])>5){
                                                        
                                                        enemies.angle[i] = -1*(atan2(nextStepInPath[0]-enemies.x[i],nextStepInPath[1]-enemies.y[i])-180);
                                                        
                                                        enemies.speedX[i]=sin(enemies.angle[i])*enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].speed;
                                                        enemies.speedY[i]=-cos(enemies.angle[i])*enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].speed;
                                                        
                                                    }
                                                    
                                                    else{
                                                        
                                                        nextStepInPath = [];
                                                        
                                                    }
                                                    
                                                }
                                                
                                            }
                                            
                                        }
                                        
                                        else{
                                            
                                            enemies.animation[i] = 0;
                                            
                                            //Idle Animation
                                            enemies.animationTime[i]-=game.deltaF;
                                            
                                            if(enemies.animationTime[i]<= 0){
                                                
                                                if(enemies.animationFrame[i] < objectConstants[enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].object].texture[enemies.animation[i]].length-1){
                                                    
                                                    enemies.animationFrame[i]++;
                                                    
                                                }
                                                
                                                else{
                                                    
                                                    enemies.animationFrame[i]=0;
                                                    
                                                }
                                                
                                                enemies.animationTime[i] = enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].animationTimes[0];
                                                
                                            }
                                            
                                        }
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    break;
                                
                                case 'pain':
                                    
                                    if(dist(0,0,enemies.speedX[i],enemies.speedY[i]) <= enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].speed * 1.1){
                                        
                                        enemies.speedX[i] = 0;
                                        enemies.speedY[i] = 0;
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    if(enemies.moveTime[i] <= 0 && dist(0,0,enemies.speedX[i],enemies.speedY[i]) <= enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].speed * 1.1){
                                        
                                        enemies.state[i] = 'attacking';
                                        
                                        enemies.cooldown[i] = enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].attackWait;
                                        
                                        enemies.animation[i] = 0;
                                        
                                        enemies.animationFrame[i] = 0;
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    enemies.moveTime[i]-=game.deltaF;
                                    break;
                                
                                case 'ambush':
                                    
                                    if(playerInLine){
                                        
                                        enemies.state[i] = 'attacking';
                                        
                                        enemies.angle[i] = da;
                                        
                                        enemies.moving[i] = false;
                                        
                                        enemies.moveTime[i] = 20;
                                        
                                        enemies.cooldown[i] = enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].attackWait;
                                        
                                        enemies.lastSeen[i] = [player.x,player.y,-1*(atan2(player.speedX,player.speedY)-180)];
                                        
                                        enemies.animation[i] = 0;
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    //Idle Animation
                                    enemies.animationTime[i]-=game.deltaF;
                                    
                                    if(enemies.animationTime[i]<= 0){
                                        
                                        if(enemies.animationFrame[i] < objectConstants[enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].object].texture[enemies.animation[i]].length-1){
                                            
                                            enemies.animationFrame[i]++;
                                            
                                        }
                                        
                                        else{
                                            
                                            enemies.animationFrame[i]=0;
                                            
                                        }
                                        
                                        enemies.animationTime[i] = enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].animationTimes[0];
                                        
                                    }
                                    
                                    
                                    break;
                                
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        
                            
                        default:
                        
                        //Dont move if weird type
                        //enemies.speedX[i]=0;
                        //enemies.speedY[i]=0;
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(enemies.state[i] !== 'pain'){
                        
                        //Stop them from walking off ledges
                        var width = enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].width;
                        
                        var tilesIn = [];
                        
                        tilesIn.push([floor((enemies.x[i])/20),floor((enemies.y[i])/20)]);
                        
                        tilesIn.push([floor((enemies.x[i]-width/2)/20),floor((enemies.y[i]-width/2)/20)]);
                        tilesIn.push([floor((enemies.x[i]-width/2)/20),floor((enemies.y[i]+width/2)/20)]);
                        tilesIn.push([floor((enemies.x[i]+width/2)/20),floor((enemies.y[i]-width/2)/20)]);
                        tilesIn.push([floor((enemies.x[i]+width/2)/20),floor((enemies.y[i]+width/2)/20)]);
                        
                        for(var t = 0; t<=tilesIn.length-1;t++){
                            
                            if((enemies.speedX[i]<0 && ((loadedMap.boundaries[tilesIn[t][1]][tilesIn[t][0]-1] > 0 && loadedMap.doors[tilesIn[t][1]][tilesIn[t][0]-1] === 0) || abs(tempMap.floorHeight[floor((enemies.y[i])/20)][floor((enemies.x[i])/20)]-tempMap.floorHeight[tilesIn[t][1]][tilesIn[t][0]-1]) > 100) && enemies.x[i]/20-tilesIn[t][0] < 0.5 && enemies.x[i]/20-tilesIn[t][0] > 0 && !enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].flying) || (enemies.speedX[i]>0 && ((loadedMap.boundaries[tilesIn[t][1]][tilesIn[t][0]+1] > 0 && loadedMap.doors[tilesIn[t][1]][tilesIn[t][0]+1] === 0) || abs(tempMap.floorHeight[floor((enemies.y[i])/20)][floor((enemies.x[i])/20)]-tempMap.floorHeight[tilesIn[t][1]][tilesIn[t][0]+1]) > 100) && enemies.x[i]/20-tilesIn[t][0] > 0.5 && enemies.x[i]/20-tilesIn[t][0] < 1 && !enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].flying)){
                                
                                enemies.speedX[i]=0;
                                if(enemies.speedY[i]!==0){
                                    
                                    enemies.speedY[i]=enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].speed * (enemies.speedY[i]/abs(enemies.speedY[i]));
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
