                            if((enemies.speedY[i]<0 && ((loadedMap.boundaries[tilesIn[t][1]-1][tilesIn[t][0]] > 0 && loadedMap.doors[tilesIn[t][1]-1][tilesIn[t][0]] === 0)|| abs(tempMap.floorHeight[floor((enemies.y[i])/20)][floor((enemies.x[i])/20)]-tempMap.floorHeight[tilesIn[t][1]-1][tilesIn[t][0]]) > 100) && enemies.y[i]/20-tilesIn[t][1] < 0.5 && enemies.y[i]/20-tilesIn[t][1] > 0 && !enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].flying) || (enemies.speedY[i]>0 && ((loadedMap.boundaries[tilesIn[t][1]+1][tilesIn[t][0]] > 0 && loadedMap.doors[tilesIn[t][1]+1][tilesIn[t][0]] === 0) || abs(tempMap.floorHeight[floor((enemies.y[i])/20)][floor((enemies.x[i])/20)]-tempMap.floorHeight[tilesIn[t][1]+1][tilesIn[t][0]]) > 100) && enemies.y[i]/20-tilesIn[t][1] > 0.5 && enemies.y[i]/20-tilesIn[t][1] <1 && !enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].flying)){
                                
                                if(enemies.speedX[i]!==0){
                                    
                                    enemies.speedX[i]=enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].speed * (enemies.speedX[i]/abs(enemies.speedX[i]));
                                    
                                }
                                enemies.speedY[i]=0;
                                
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    //Slow
                    var slowRate=0.5;
                    
                    //Slow enemy
                    if(sqrt(sq(enemies.speedX[i])+sq(enemies.speedY[i]))>0){
                        
                        if(sqrt(sq(enemies.speedX[i]) + sq(enemies.speedY[i]))<slowRate){
                            
                            enemies.speedX[i]=0;
                            
                            enemies.speedY[i]=0;
                            
                        }
                        
                        else{
                            
                            var tempSpeedX=enemies.speedX[i];
                            
                            var tempSpeedY=enemies.speedY[i];
                            
                            enemies.speedX[i]-=(tempSpeedX/sqrt(sq(tempSpeedX)+sq(tempSpeedY)))*slowRate*game.deltaF;
                            
                            enemies.speedY[i]-=(tempSpeedY/sqrt(sq(tempSpeedX)+sq(tempSpeedY)))*slowRate*game.deltaF;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                else{
                    
                    enemies.speedX[i] = 0;
                    enemies.speedY[i] = 0;
                    
                }
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Check all enemies
        for(var i = 0; i <= enemies.x.length-1; i++){
            
            var enHeight = enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].height;
            
            if((enemies.speedX[i] !== 0 || enemies.speedY[i] !== 0) && !enemies.dud[i]){
                
                //Set variables for enemies next position
                var nextX=enemies.x[i]+enemies.speedX[i]*game.deltaF;
                var nextY=enemies.y[i]+enemies.speedY[i]*game.deltaF;
                
                var x = floor(nextX/20);
                var y = floor(nextY/20);
                
                //var enHeight = 20;
                
                var bottom = enemies.verticalPos[i];
                
                var top = enemies.verticalPos[i] + enHeight;
                
                var col = checkBoundaries(enemies.x[i],enemies.y[i],enemies.speedX[i],enemies.speedY[i],top,bottom,enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].width,true,game.enemyEnemyCollision,game.enemyPlayerCollision);
                
                if(col[4]){
                    
                    enemies.x[i] = col[0];
                    enemies.y[i] = col[1];
                    enemies.speedX[i] = col[2];
                    enemies.speedY[i] = col[3];
                    
                }
                
            }
            
            var mapFloorHeight = primaryOrSecondary(enemies.x[i],enemies.y[i],'floor');
            var mapCeilingHeight = primaryOrSecondary(enemies.x[i],enemies.y[i],'ceiling');
            
            //if(enemies.vertSpeed[i]>0){println(enemies.vertSpeed[i]);}
            
            enemies.verticalPos[i] += enemies.vertSpeed[i];
            
            if(!enemyConstants[enemies.type[i]].flying){
                
                enemies.vertSpeed[i] -= game.gravity*game.deltaF;
                
            }
            
            //player.floorHeight+floorH
            if(enemies.verticalPos[i]<=mapFloorHeight){
                
                enemies.verticalPos[i]=mapFloorHeight;
                
                enemies.vertSpeed[i] = 0;
                
            }
            
            if(enemies.verticalPos[i]+enHeight>mapCeilingHeight){
                
                enemies.verticalPos[i]=mapCeilingHeight-enHeight;
                
                enemies.vertSpeed[i] = 0;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Move and destroy particles
        for(var i = 0; i <= particles.x.length-1; i++){
            
            if(!particles.dud[i] && !game.semiPause){
                
                //Move the particle
                particles.x[i]+=particles.speedX[i]*game.deltaF;
                particles.y[i]+=particles.speedY[i]*game.deltaF;
                
                particles.verticalPos[i]+=particles.verticalSpeed[i]*game.deltaF;
                
                //Kill particles
                particles.life[i]-=game.deltaF;
                
                if(particles.life[i]<=0){
                    
                    particles.dud[i]=true;
                    
                }
                
                //Change Transparency
                particles.transparency[i]+=particleConstants[particles.type[i]].difference*game.deltaF;
                //Physics
                if(particleConstants[particles.type[i]].physics){
                    
                    var particleHeight = particleConstants[particles.type[i]].height;
                    
                    var floorH = primaryOrSecondary(particles.x[i],particles.y[i],'floor');
                    
                    var ceilH = primaryOrSecondary(particles.x[i],particles.y[i],'ceiling');
                    
                    if(particles.verticalPos[i] - particleHeight/2>floorH-game.realHeight/2){
                        
                        particles.verticalSpeed[i]-=game.gravity*game.deltaF;
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(particles.verticalPos[i]+particles.verticalSpeed[i] - particleHeight/2<=floorH-game.realHeight/2){
                        
                        if(particles.verticalSpeed[i]<-15/game.deltaF){
                            
                            particles.verticalSpeed[i]*=-0.5;
                            
                            particles.verticalPos[i] = floorH-game.realHeight/2 + particleHeight/2; 
                            
                        }
                        
                        else{
                            
                            particles.verticalSpeed[i]=0;
                            
                            particles.verticalPos[i] = floorH-game.realHeight/2 + particleHeight/2; 
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(particles.verticalPos[i]+particles.verticalSpeed[i] - particleHeight/2>=ceilH-game.realHeight/2){
                        
                        if(particles.verticalSpeed[i]>15*game.deltaF){
                            
                            particles.verticalSpeed[i]*=-0.5;
                            
                        }
                        
                        else{
                            
                            particles.verticalSpeed[i]=0;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(particles.speedX[i] !== 0 || particles.speedY[i] !== 0){
                        
                        //Particle Collison
                        var partHeight = 20;
                        
                        var partWidth = 3;
                        
                        var bottom = particles.verticalPos[i] + game.realHeight/2;
                        
                        var top = particles.verticalPos[i] + partHeight + game.realHeight/2;
                        
                        var col = checkBoundaries(particles.x[i],particles.y[i],particles.speedX[i],particles.speedY[i],top,bottom,partWidth,true,false,true);
                        
                        if(col[4]){
                            
                            particles.x[i] = col[0];
                            particles.y[i] = col[1];
                            particles.speedX[i] = col[2];
                            particles.speedY[i] = col[3];
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //Slow Particle
                if(sqrt(sq(particles.speedX[i])+sq(particles.speedY[i]))>0){
                    
                    if(sqrt(sq(particles.speedX[i]) + sq(particles.speedY[i]))<0.1){
                        
                        particles.speedX[i]=0;
                        
                        particles.speedY[i]=0;
                        
                    }
                    
                    else{
                        
                        var tempA=-0.1;
                        
                        var tempSpeedX=particles.speedX[i];
                        
                        var tempSpeedY=particles.speedY[i];
                        
                        particles.speedX[i]+=(tempSpeedX/sqrt(sq(tempSpeedX)+sq(tempSpeedY)))*tempA*game.deltaF;
                        
                        particles.speedY[i]+=(tempSpeedY/sqrt(sq(tempSpeedX)+sq(tempSpeedY)))*tempA*game.deltaF;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //Light projectiles
                //Add to temp lighting
                if(!particles.dud[i] && !game.semiPause){
                    
                    var y = constrain(floor(particles.y[i]/20),0,tempMap.lighting.length-1);
                    
                    tempMap.lighting[y][constrain(floor(particles.x[i]/20),0,tempMap.lighting[y])] += particleConstants[particles.type[i]].lighting/2;
                    
                    for(var y = constrain(floor(particles.y[i]/20)-1,0,tempMap.lighting.length-1); y <= constrain(floor(particles.y[i]/20)+1,0,tempMap.lighting.length-1);y++){
                        
                        for(var x = constrain(floor(particles.x[i]/20)-1,0,tempMap.lighting[y].length-1); x <= constrain(floor(particles.x[i]/20)+1,0,tempMap.lighting[y].length-1);x++){
                            
                            tempMap.lighting[y][x] += particleConstants[particles.type[i]].lighting/2;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //Extra stuff for specific particles
                switch(particles.type[i]){
                    
                    //Flare Shots
                    case 18:
                        
                        //Create Particles a few times a second
                        if(floor(game.globalTimer) % floor(random(5,6)) === 0 && !game.semiPause && !particles.dud[i]){
                            
                            var lateralSpeed = random(-3,3);
                            
                            var horizontalSpeed = random(-3,3);
                            
                            var v = dist(particles.speedX[i],particles.speedY[i],0,0);
                            
                            var finalX = horizontalSpeed*(particles.speedX[i]/v);
                            
                            var finalY = lateralSpeed*(particles.speedY[i]/v);
                            
                            if(v <= 0.1){
                                
                                finalX=horizontalSpeed;
                                finalY=lateralSpeed;
                                
                            }
                            
                            createParticle(particles.x[i],particles.y[i],finalX,finalY,particles.verticalPos[i],random(0,60),16);
                            
                            
                        }
                        
                        if(floor(game.globalTimer) % 10=== 0 && !game.semiPause && !particles.dud[i]){
                            
                            var v = dist(particles.speedX[i],particles.speedY[i],0,0);
                            
                            var lateralSpeed = random(-0.1,0.1);
                            
                            var horizontalSpeed = random(-0.1,0.1);
                            
                            var finalX = horizontalSpeed*(particles.speedX[i]/v);
                            
                            var finalY = lateralSpeed*(particles.speedY[i]/v);
                            
                            if(v <= 0.1){
                                
                                finalX=horizontalSpeed;
                                finalY=lateralSpeed;
                                
                            }
                            
                            createParticle(particles.x[i],particles.y[i],finalX,finalY,particles.verticalPos[i],random(-2,2),17);
                            
                        }
                        
                        break;
                        
                    //Rocket Sparks
                    case 19:
                        
                        //println(particles.life[i]);
                        
                        particles.animationFrame[i] = floor(particles.life[i]/4)%2;
                        
                        break;
                        
                    //Rocket Falling Sparks
                    case 20:
                        
                        //println(particles.life[i]);
                        
                        particles.animationFrame[i] = floor(particles.life[i]/4)%3;
                        
                        break;
                    
                    case 23:
                    case 25:
                    case 27:
                    case 29:
                        
                        if(floor(game.globalTimer) % 2 === 0){
                            
                            createParticle(particles.x[i],particles.y[i],0,0,particles.verticalPos[i],0,particles.type[i]+1);
                            
                        }
                        
                        break;
                    
                }
                
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Projectile Collison
        for(var i = 0; i <= projectiles.x.length-1; i++){
            
            if(!projectiles.dud[i]){
                
                //Set variables for projectiles next position
                var nextX=projectiles.x[i]+projectiles.speedX[i]*game.deltaF;
                var nextY=projectiles.y[i]+projectiles.speedY[i]*game.deltaF;
                var radius = projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].width/2;
                
                //Variables about impact
                var impact = false;
                var impactX=projectiles.x[i];
                var impactY=projectiles.y[i];
                
                //The normal vector to the wall
                var normalX=0;
                var normalY=0;
                
                var x = floor(nextX/20);
                var y = floor(nextY/20);
                
                //Check all boundaries
                
                //checkBoundaries = function(ix,iy,vx,vy,top,bottom,oWidth,stairs)
                
                var projHeight = projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].height;
                
                var bottom = projectiles.verticalPos[i] + game.realHeight/2;
                
                var top = projectiles.verticalPos[i] + projHeight + game.realHeight/2;
                
                var col = checkBoundaries(projectiles.x[i],projectiles.y[i],projectiles.speedX[i],projectiles.speedY[i],top,bottom,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].width,false,false,false);
                
                if(col[4]){
                    
                    projectiles.x[i] = col[0];
                    projectiles.y[i] = col[1];
                    projectiles.speedX[i] = col[2];
                    projectiles.speedY[i] = col[3];
                    
                    impact = true;
                    
                }
                
                var floorH = primaryOrSecondary(nextX,nextY,'floor');
                var ceilingH = primaryOrSecondary(nextX,nextY,'ceiling');
                
                if(projectiles.verticalPos[i]+game.realHeight/2 <= floorH){
                    
                    impact = true;
                    
                }
                
                if(projectiles.verticalPos[i]+game.realHeight/2 >= ceilingH){
                    
                    impact = true;
                    
                }
                
                //Don't explode on contact if you're dynamite
                if(projectiles.type[i] === 14){
                    
                    impact = false;
                    
                    projectiles.fuse[i] +=game.deltaF;
                    
                    if(projectiles.fuse[i] >= 30){
                        
                        projectiles.dud[i]=true;
                        
                        explosion(impactX,impactY,projectiles.verticalPos[i],normalX,normalY,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].range,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].damage,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].knockback,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].expType,projectiles.friendly[i]);
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //Physics
                if(projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].physics){
                    
                    var projectileHeight = 20;
                    
                    if(projectiles.verticalPos[i] - projectileHeight/2>floorH-game.realHeight/2){
                        
                        projectiles.verticalVelocity[i]-=game.gravity*game.deltaF/20;
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(projectiles.verticalPos[i]+projectiles.verticalVelocity[i] - projectileHeight/2<=floorH-game.realHeight/2){
                        
                        if(projectiles.verticalVelocity[i]<-1){
                            
                            projectiles.verticalVelocity[i]*=-0.5;
                            
                            projectiles.verticalPos[i] = floorH-game.realHeight/2 + projectileHeight/2+10; 
                            
                        }
                        
                        else{
                            
                            projectiles.verticalVelocity[i]=0;
                            
                            projectiles.verticalPos[i] = floorH-game.realHeight/2 + projectileHeight/2; 
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(projectiles.verticalPos[i]+projectiles.verticalVelocity[i] - projectileHeight/2>=ceilingH-game.realHeight/2){
                        
                        if(projectiles.verticalVelocity[i]>1){
                            
                            projectiles.verticalVelocity[i]*=-0.5;
                            
                        }
                        
                        else{
                            
                            projectiles.verticalVelocity[i]=0;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                    //Slow Particle
                    if(sqrt(sq(projectiles.speedX[i])+sq(projectiles.speedY[i]))>0){
                        
                        if(sqrt(sq(projectiles.speedX[i]) + sq(projectiles.speedY[i]))<0.1){
                            
                            projectiles.speedX[i]=0;
                            
                            projectiles.speedY[i]=0;
                            
                        }
                        
                        else{
                            
                            var tempA=-0.1;
                            
                            var tempSpeedX=projectiles.speedX[i];
                            
                            var tempSpeedY=projectiles.speedY[i];
                            
                            projectiles.speedX[i]+=(tempSpeedX/sqrt(sq(tempSpeedX)+sq(tempSpeedY)))*tempA*game.deltaF;
                            
                            projectiles.speedY[i]+=(tempSpeedY/sqrt(sq(tempSpeedX)+sq(tempSpeedY)))*tempA*game.deltaF;
                            
                        }
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                //Do stuff with the values based on the projectiles
                if(impact){
                    
                    projectiles.dud[i]=true;
                    
                    //impactX,impactY,impactZ,normalX,normalY,range,xLimit,yLimit,minPart,maxPart,damage,knockback,type
                    explosion(impactX,impactY,projectiles.verticalPos[i],normalX,normalY,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].range,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].damage,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].knockback,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].expType,projectiles.friendly[i]);
                    
                }
                
                
                for(var e = 0; e <= enemies.x.length-1; e++){
                    
                    if(dist(impactX,impactY,enemies.x[e],enemies.y[e])<=enemyConstants[enemies.type[e]].width/2+projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].width/2 && !projectiles.dud[i]  && !enemies.dud[e] && projectiles.friendly[i] && top >= enemies.verticalPos[e] && bottom <= enemies.verticalPos[e] + enemyConstants[enemies.type[e]].height){
                        
                        projectiles.dud[i]=true;
                        
                        var impactZ = projectiles.verticalPos[i] + projHeight/2 + game.realHeight/2;
                        
                        var eHeight = enemies.verticalPos[e] + enemyConstants[enemies.type[e]].height/2;
                        
                        hurt(1,e,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].damage,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].knockback,atan2(enemies.y[e]-projectiles.y[i],enemies.x[e]-projectiles.x[i]),atan(((eHeight-impactZ))/(dist(impactX,impactY,enemies.x[e],enemies.y[e]))));
                        
                        //impactX,impactY,impactZ,normalX,normalY,range,xLimit,yLimit,minPart,maxPart,damage,knockback,type
                        explosion(impactX,impactY,projectiles.verticalPos[i],normalX,normalY,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].range,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].damage,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].knockback,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].expType,projectiles.friendly[i]);
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
                if(dist(impactX,impactY,player.x,player.y)<=5+projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].width/2 && !projectiles.dud[i] && !projectiles.friendly[i]&& top >= player.height+player.floorHeight + 300 && bottom <= player.height + 300 && projectiles.type[i]){
                    
                    hurt(0,0,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].damage,0,0,0);
                    
                    projectiles.dud[i]=true;
                    
                    //impactX,impactY,impactZ,normalX,normalY,range,xLimit,yLimit,minPart,maxPart,damage,knockback,type
                    explosion(impactX,impactY,projectiles.verticalPos[i],normalX,normalY,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].range,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].damage,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].knockback,projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].expType,projectiles.friendly[i]);
                    
                }
                
                //Animate some projectiles
                switch(projectiles.type[i]){
                    
                    case 13:
                    case 14:
                        
                        if(floor(game.globalTimer/10)%2 === 0){
                            
                            projectiles.animationFrame[i] = 0;
                            
                        }
                        
                        else{
                            
                            projectiles.animationFrame[i] = 1;
                            
                        }
                        
                    break;
                    
                }
                
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Check Pickups
        for(var i = 0; i <= pickups.x.length-1; i++){
            
            if(!pickups.dud[i]){
                
                var pickedUp=false;
                
                if(dist(pickups.x[i],pickups.y[i],player.x,player.y)<=pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].width){
                    
                    //Health
                    if(pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].health>0 && player.health<100){
                        
                        give('player',0,'health',0,pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].health);
                        
                        pickups.dud[i]=true;
                        
                        pickedUp=true;
                        
                    }
                    
                    //Suit
                    if(pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].suit>0 && player.suit<100){
                        
                        give('player',0,'suit',0,pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].suit);
                        
                        pickups.dud[i]=true;
                        
                        pickedUp=true;
                        
                    }
                    
                    //Ammo
                    if(pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].ammo>0 && player.ammo[pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].ammoType]<player.maxAmmo[pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].ammoType]){
                        
                        give('player',0,'ammo',pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].ammoType,pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].ammo);
                        
                        pickups.dud[i]=true;
                        
                        pickedUp=true;
                        
                    }
                    
                    //Weapon
                    if(pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].weapon && !player.weapons[pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].weaponType]){
                        
                        give('player',0,'weapon',pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].weaponType,0);
                        
                        pickups.dud[i]=true;
                        
                        pickedUp=true;
                        
                    }
                    
                    //Key
                    if(pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].key){
                        
                        give('player',0,'key',pickupConstants[pickups.type[i]].keyNum,0);
                        
                        pickups.dud[i]=true;
                        
                        pickedUp=true;
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(pickedUp){
                        
                        playSoundEffect(18);
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Move projectiles
        for(var i = 0; i <= projectiles.x.length-1; i++){
            
            if(!projectiles.dud[i] && !game.semiPause){
                
                projectiles.x[i]+=projectiles.speedX[i]*game.deltaF;
                projectiles.y[i]+=projectiles.speedY[i]*game.deltaF;
                projectiles.verticalPos[i] += projectiles.verticalVelocity[i] * 20 * game.deltaF;
                
            }
            
            switch(projectiles.type[i]){
                
                //Pistol Shots
                case 5:
                    
                    if(!game.semiPause){
                        
                        projectiles.verticalVelocity[i]+=(sin((game.globalTimer%10)*36)/7)*game.deltaF;
                        //projectiles.speedX[i] +=nvx*sin(game.globalTimer*40);
                        
                        //projectiles.verticalVelocity[i]+=sin(game.globalTimer*40)*25 * nvz;
                        //projectiles.speedX[i] += sin(game.globalTimer*40)*25 * nvx;
                        
                    }
                    
                    //Create Particles a few times a second
                    if(floor(game.globalTimer) % floor(random(3,5)) === 0 && !game.semiPause && !projectiles.dud[i]){
                        
                        var lateralSpeed = random(-0.2,0.2);
                        
                        var v = dist(projectiles.speedX[i],projectiles.speedY[i],0,0);
                        
                        createParticle(projectiles.x[i],projectiles.y[i],lateralSpeed*(projectiles.speedY[i]/v),lateralSpeed*(projectiles.speedX[i]/v),projectiles.verticalPos[i],random(-1,1),5);
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                    
                //Flare Shots
                case 1:
                    
                    if(!game.semiPause){
                        
                        projectiles.verticalVelocity[i]+=(sin((game.globalTimer%10)*36)/7)*game.deltaF;
                        
                        var total = dist(0,0,projectiles.speedY[i],projectiles.speedX[i]);
                        
                        var perpVect = [projectiles.speedY[i]/total,-projectiles.speedX[i]/total];
                        
                        projectiles.speedX[i]+=(sin((game.globalTimer%10)*36)/7)*game.deltaF*perpVect[0];
                        
                        projectiles.speedY[i]+=(cos((game.globalTimer%10)*36)/7)*game.deltaF*perpVect[1];
                        
                        //projectiles.speedX[i] +=nvx*sin(game.globalTimer*40);
                        
                        //projectiles.verticalVelocity[i]+=sin(game.globalTimer*40)*25 * nvz;
                        //projectiles.speedX[i] += sin(game.globalTimer*40)*25 * nvx;
                        
                    }
                    
                    //Create Particles a few times a second
                    if(floor(game.globalTimer) % floor(random(3,5)) === 0 && !game.semiPause && !projectiles.dud[i]){
                        
                        var lateralSpeed = random(-3,3);
                        
                        var v = dist(projectiles.speedX[i],projectiles.speedY[i],0,0);
                        
                        createParticle(projectiles.x[i],projectiles.y[i],lateralSpeed*(projectiles.speedY[i]/v),lateralSpeed*(projectiles.speedX[i]/v),projectiles.verticalPos[i],random(-60,60),16);
                        
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(floor(game.globalTimer) % 4=== 0 && !game.semiPause && !projectiles.dud[i]){
                        
                        var v = dist(projectiles.speedX[i],projectiles.speedY[i],0,0);
                        
                        var lateralSpeed = random(-0.1,0.1);
                        
                        createParticle(projectiles.x[i],projectiles.y[i],lateralSpeed*(projectiles.speedY[i]/v),lateralSpeed*(projectiles.speedX[i]/v),projectiles.verticalPos[i],random(-2,2),17);
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                
                //Rockets
                case 4:
                    
                    if(!game.semiPause){
                        
                        if(projectiles.fuse[i]>=20){
                            
                            projectiles.verticalVelocity[i]*=1.1;
                            
                            projectiles.speedX[i]*=1.1;
                            
                            projectiles.speedY[i]*=1.1;
                            
                        }
                        
                        projectiles.fuse[i] ++;
                        
                        
                    }
                    
                    
                    //Create Particles a few times a second
                    if(floor(game.globalTimer) % floor(random(3,5)) === 0 && !game.semiPause && !projectiles.dud[i] && projectiles.fuse[i]>=20){
                        
                        var lateralSpeed = random(-1,1);
                        
                        var v = dist(projectiles.speedX[i],projectiles.speedY[i],0,0);
                        
                        createParticle(projectiles.x[i],projectiles.y[i],lateralSpeed*(projectiles.speedY[i]/v),lateralSpeed*(projectiles.speedX[i]/v),projectiles.verticalPos[i],random(-5,5),19);
                        
                        var lateralSpeed = random(-3,3);
                        
                        var v = dist(projectiles.speedX[i],projectiles.speedY[i],0,0);
                        
                        createParticle(projectiles.x[i],projectiles.y[i],lateralSpeed*(projectiles.speedY[i]/v),lateralSpeed*(projectiles.speedX[i]/v),projectiles.verticalPos[i],random(-60,60),20);
                        
                    }
                    
                    if(floor(game.globalTimer) % 2=== 0 && !game.semiPause && !projectiles.dud[i]){
                        
                        var v = dist(projectiles.speedX[i],projectiles.speedY[i],0,0);
                        
                        var lateralSpeed = random(-0.1,0.1);
                        
                        createParticle(projectiles.x[i],projectiles.y[i],lateralSpeed*(projectiles.speedY[i]/v),lateralSpeed*(projectiles.speedX[i]/v),projectiles.verticalPos[i],random(-2,2),21);
                        
                    }
                    
                    break;
                
            }
            
            //Light projectiles
            //Add to temp lighting
            if(!projectiles.dud[i] && !game.semiPause){
                
                tempMap.lighting[floor(projectiles.y[i]/20)][floor(projectiles.x[i]/20)] += projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].lighting/2;
                
                for(var y = constrain(floor(projectiles.y[i]/20)-1,0,tempMap.lighting.length-1); y <= constrain(floor(projectiles.y[i]/20)+1,0,tempMap.lighting.length-1);y++){
                    
                    for(var x = constrain(floor(projectiles.x[i]/20)-1,0,tempMap.lighting[y].length-1); x <= constrain(floor(projectiles.x[i]/20)+1,0,tempMap.lighting[y].length-1);x++){
                        
                        tempMap.lighting[y][x] += projectileConstants[projectiles.type[i]].lighting/2;
                        
                    }
                    
                }
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Move enemies
        for(var i = 0; i <= enemies.x.length-1; i++){
            
            if(!enemies.dud[i] && !game.semiPause){
                
                enemies.x[i]+=enemies.speedX[i]*game.deltaF;
                enemies.y[i]+=enemies.speedY[i]*game.deltaF;
                //enemies.verticalPos[i]+=enemies.verticalSpeed[i]*game.deltaF;
                
            }
            
        } 
        
    }
    
    //Reset Angle
    if(player.angle>=360){
        
        player.angle-=360;
        
    }
    
    if(player.angle<0){
        
        player.angle+=360;
        
    }
    
    var friction = player.friction;
    
    if(!player.canJump){
        
        friction *= 0.1;
        
    }
    
    //Slow player
    if(!player.move && sqrt(sq(player.speedX)+sq(player.speedY))>0 && player.canJump){
        
        if(sqrt(sq(player.speedX) + sq(player.speedY))<player.acceleration*friction*game.deltaF){
            
            player.speedX=0;
            
            player.speedY=0;
            
        }
        
        else{
            
            var tempA=-player.acceleration*friction*game.deltaF;
            
            var tempSpeedX=player.speedX;
            
            var tempSpeedY=player.speedY;
            
            player.speedX+=(tempSpeedX/sqrt(sq(tempSpeedX)+sq(tempSpeedY)))*tempA;
            
            player.speedY+=(tempSpeedY/sqrt(sq(tempSpeedX)+sq(tempSpeedY)))*tempA;
            
        }
        
    }
    
    if(sqrt(sq(player.speedX)+sq(player.speedY))>player.maxSpeed && !player.overSpeed){
        
        var tempSpeedX=player.speedX;
        var tempSpeedY=player.speedY;
        
        player.speedX=(tempSpeedX/sqrt(sq(tempSpeedX)+sq(tempSpeedY)))*player.maxSpeed;
        
        player.speedY=(tempSpeedY/sqrt(sq(tempSpeedX)+sq(tempSpeedY)))*player.maxSpeed;
        
    }
    
    if(dist(0,0,player.speedX,player.speedY) < player.maxSpeed * 1.1){
        
        player.overSpeed = false;
        
    }
    
    
    //Check boundaries for player
    if(!game.noClip){
        
        //Check if the player teleports or leaves the level before continuing to the boundary check
        //Teleport
        for(var i = 0; i <= loadedMap.teleporters.length-1; i++){
            
            if(player.x >= loadedMap.teleporters[i].x1*20 && player.x < loadedMap.teleporters[i].x1*20 + loadedMap.teleporters[i].w1*20 + 20 && player.y >= loadedMap.teleporters[i].y1*20 && player.y < loadedMap.teleporters[i].y1*20 + loadedMap.teleporters[i].h1*20 + 20){
                
                //Find x and y percent
                var xp = (player.x - loadedMap.teleporters[i].x1*20)/(loadedMap.teleporters[i].w1*20 + 20);
                var yp = (player.y - loadedMap.teleporters[i].y1*20)/(loadedMap.teleporters[i].h1*20 + 20);
                
                //Place player at correct height relative to ground
                var dh = player.height - primaryOrSecondary(player.x,player.y,'floor');
                
                player.x = loadedMap.teleporters[i].x2*20 + (loadedMap.teleporters[i].w2*20 + 20)*xp;
                player.y = loadedMap.teleporters[i].y2*20 + (loadedMap.teleporters[i].h2*20 + 20)*yp;
                
                player.height = primaryOrSecondary(player.x,player.y,'floor') + dh;
                
            }
            
        }
        
        //Level Exit
        for(var i = 0; i <= loadedMap.levelExits.length-1; i++){
            
            if(player.x >= loadedMap.levelExits[i].x*20 && player.x < loadedMap.levelExits[i].x*20 + loadedMap.levelExits[i].w*20 + 20 && player.y >= loadedMap.levelExits[i].y*20 && player.y < loadedMap.levelExits[i].y*20 + loadedMap.levelExits[i].h*20 + 20){
                
                game.map = loadedMap.levelExits[i].level;
                
                loadMap(loadedMap.levelExits[i].sx,loadedMap.levelExits[i].sy);
                
            }
            
        }
        
        var bottomOfPlayer = 0;
        
        if(player.canJump){
            
            bottomOfPlayer = abs(player.floorHeight/3);
            
        }
        
        var p = checkBoundaries(player.x,player.y,player.speedX,player.speedY,game.realHeight/2 + player.height,game.realHeight/2 + player.height + player.floorHeight,player.width,player.canJump,game.enemyPlayerCollision,false);
        
        if(p[4]){
            
            player.x = p[0];
            player.y = p[1];
            player.speedX = p[2];
            player.speedY = p[3];
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Player falling temp
    var leftMost = constrain(floor((player.x-(player.width/2-0.1))),0,(tempMap.floorHeight[floor(player.y/20)].length-1)*20);
    var rightMost = constrain(floor((player.x+(player.width/2-0.1))),0,(tempMap.floorHeight[floor(player.y/20)].length-1)*20);
    var upMost = constrain(floor((player.y-(player.width/2-0.1))),0,(tempMap.floorHeight.length-1)*20);
    var downMost = constrain(floor((player.y+(player.width/2-0.1))),0,(tempMap.floorHeight.length-1)*20);
    
    var lastFloorHeight = player.mapFloorHeight;
    
    player.mapFloorHeight = primaryOrSecondary(player.x,player.y,'floor');
    player.mapCeilingHeight = primaryOrSecondary(player.x,player.y,'ceiling');
    
    //Check what the player is standing on by tracing a circle in them a few times
    for(var theta = 0; theta <= 360; theta+=20){
        
        for(var r = (player.width/2-0.1); r >= 0; r-=player.width/4){
            
            var x = constrain(player.x + r*cos(theta),0,(tempMap.floorHeight[floor(player.y/20)].length)*20);
            var y = constrain(player.y + r*sin(theta),0,(tempMap.floorHeight.length)*20);
            
            if(primaryOrSecondary(x,y,'floor')>player.mapFloorHeight){
                
                player.mapFloorHeight = primaryOrSecondary(x,y,'floor');
                
            }
            
            if(primaryOrSecondary(x,y,'ceiling')<player.mapCeilingHeight){
                
                player.mapCeilingHeight = primaryOrSecondary(x,y,'ceiling');
                
            }
            
        }
        
    }
    
    //Check if the player is being lowered by the floor
    var sameSpot = (lastMapX === floor(player.x/20)) && (lastMapY === floor(player.y/20)) && (lastPS === ps);
    
    lastMapX = floor(player.x/20);
    lastMapY = floor(player.y/20);
    lastPS = ps;
    
    //Small thing that pushes you slightly as you fall off a ledge so that you don't end up between the wall and the boundary for it (only seems to matter for diagonals)
    if(player.mapFloorHeight < lastFloorHeight && !sameSpot && !player.overSpeed){
        
        player.speedX *= 1.5;
        player.speedY *= 1.5;
        
        if(dist(0,0,player.speedX,player.speedY)<1 && player.speedX !== 0 && player.speedY !== 0){
            
            player.speedX += 1 * (player.speedX/abs(player.speedX));
            player.speedY += 1 * (player.speedY/abs(player.speedY));
            
        }
        
    }
    
    player.height+=player.vertSpeed*game.deltaF;
    
    //player.floorHeight+floorH
    if(player.height<=-300-player.floorHeight+player.mapFloorHeight){
        
        player.height=-300-player.floorHeight+player.mapFloorHeight;
        
        if(player.vertSpeed<0){
            
            player.vertSpeed=0;
            
        }
        
        player.canJump=true;
        
    }
    
    if(player.height>=-game.realHeight/2+player.mapCeilingHeight-5){
        
        player.height=-game.realHeight/2+player.mapCeilingHeight-5;
        
        player.vertSpeed=0;
        
    }
    
    if(player.vertSpeed < -game.gravity*2 || player.vertSpeed>0){
        
        player.canJump = false;
        
    }
    
    player.vertSpeed-=game.gravity*game.deltaF;
    
    //Move Player
    player.x+=player.speedX*game.deltaF;
    player.y+=player.speedY*game.deltaF;
    
};

/** Functions that are run once **/

//Load the map on start (Temporary)

loadMap();

game.lastFTime = millis() - (1/(game.baseF*1000));

updateVars();

/** Functions that are run continuously **/
var draw= function() {
    
    if(game.cam>=2){
        
        background(loadedMap.skyBox[0], loadedMap.skyBox[1], loadedMap.skyBox[2]);
        
    }
    
    else{
        
        background(225,225,225);
        
    }
    
    pushMatrix();
    
    if(game.cam===2){
        
        scale(game.realHeight/game.height);
        
    }
    
    drawWorld();
    
    popMatrix();
    if(!game.pause){
        
        if(!game.semiPause){
            
            updateMap();
            
            mapScripts();
            
        }
        
        updateVars();
        
        physics();
        
    }
    
    drawMenu();
    
    interact();
    //timing for FPS counter
    game.oldTime = game.time;
    game.time = millis();
    
    var trueFPS = floor(1/((game.time - game.oldTime) / 1000.0));
    
    if(game.time>=game.resetTime+game.maxTime){
        
        game.frameRate=trueFPS;
        
        game.resetTime=game.time;
        
    }
    
    fill(255, 255, 255);
    
    textAlign(LEFT,BASELINE);
    
    updateObjects();
    //Reset Mouse Scroll (Don't put anything that uses mouse scroll after this.)
    mouseIsScrolled = false;
    
    //println(dist(0,0,player.speedX,player.speedY));
    
    //println('');
    
};
